  Empirically, V-JEPA 2.1 achieves state-of-the-art performance on several challenging benchmarks, including 7.71 mAP on Ego4D for short-term object-interaction anticipation and 40.8 Recall@5 on EPIC-KITCHENS for high-level action anticipation, as well as a 20-point improvement in real-robot grasping success rate over V-JEPA-2 AC. The model also demonstrates strong performance in robotic navigation (5.687 ATE on TartanDrive), depth estimation (0.307 RMSE on NYUv2 with a linear probe), and global recognition (77.7 on Something-Something-V2). These results show that V-JEPA 2.1 significantly advances the state of the art in dense visual understanding and world modeling.

</details>


### [107] [OccAny: Generalized Unconstrained Urban 3D Occupancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23502)
*Anh-Quan Cao,Tuan-Hung Vu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OccAny是首个无需域内标注和传感器先验的通用3D占用预测模型，能够处理域外无标定场景，支持序列/单目/环视多种输入，通过Segmentation Forcing和新视角渲染技术实现了优越的几何补全和语义分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D占用预测方法依赖域内标注和精确传感器先验，导致可扩展性差且难以泛化到域外场景；而视觉几何基础模型虽泛化能力强，但缺乏度量预测、杂乱场景几何补全和城市场景适配等关键能力。

Method: 提出OccAny通用3D占用框架，包含三项贡献：(i)首个通用化3D占用框架；(ii)Segmentation Forcing技术提升占用质量并实现掩码级预测；(iii)新视角渲染管线，通过测试时视角增强实现几何补全。

Result: 在3D占用预测任务上优于所有视觉几何基线方法，并在两个主流城市占用预测数据集、三种输入设置下与域内自监督方法具有竞争力。

Conclusion: OccAny首次实现了无约束的通用城市3D占用预测，突破了域内标注和传感器先验的限制，为3D占用预测的泛化性和实用性提供了新方向。

Abstract: Relying on in-domain annotations and precise sensor-rig priors, existing 3D occupancy prediction methods are limited in both scalability and out-of-domain generalization. While recent visual geometry foundation models exhibit strong generalization capabilities, they were mainly designed for general purposes and lack one or more key ingredients required for urban occupancy prediction, namely metric prediction, geometry completion in cluttered scenes and adaptation to urban scenarios. We address this gap and present OccAny, the first unconstrained urban 3D occupancy model capable of operating on out-of-domain uncalibrated scenes to predict and complete metric occupancy coupled with segmentation features. OccAny is versatile and can predict occupancy from sequential, monocular, or surround-view images. Our contributions are three-fold: (i) we propose the first generalized 3D occupancy framework with (ii) Segmentation Forcing that improves occupancy quality while enabling mask-level prediction, and (iii) a Novel View Rendering pipeline that infers novel-view geometry to enable test-time view augmentation for geometry completion. Extensive experiments demonstrate that OccAny outperforms all visual geometry baselines on 3D occupancy prediction task, while remaining competitive with in-domain self-supervised methods across three input settings on two established urban occupancy prediction datasets. Our code is available at https://github.com/valeoai/OccAny .

</details>


### [108] [BSViT: A Burst Spiking Vision Transformer for Expressive and Efficient Visual Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23165)
*Hongxiang Peng,Dewei Bai,Hong Qu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BSViT通过双通道爆发脉冲自注意力机制和邻域掩码策略，在提升脉冲视觉Transformer表示能力的同时保持加法计算特性和能效优势，在多个视觉基准上超越现有脉冲Transformer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有S-ViT受限于二进制脉冲编码的信息容量不足和全局自注意力带来的密集token交互问题，亟需在保持神经形态硬件兼容性的前提下提升表示能力和计算效率。

Method: 提出BSViT，核心是DBSSA机制：查询用二进制脉冲编码、键用爆发脉冲编码以增强表示能力；值通路采用双通道兴奋/抑制二进制脉冲实现符号化调制；引入邻域掩码策略限制注意力范围为局部区域，实现结构感知稀疏性；在全局网络中系统集成爆发脉冲编码。

Result: 在静态和事件视觉基准上，BSViT在精度上持续超越现有脉冲Transformer，同时保持竞争性的能效。

Conclusion: 爆发脉冲编码和局部注意力掩码策略能有效克服二进制脉冲信息瓶颈和密集交互问题，为高精度低功耗神经形态视觉提供可行方案。

Abstract: Spiking Vision Transformers (S-ViTs) offer a promising framework for energy-efficient visual learning. However, existing designs remain limited by two fundamental issues: the restricted information capacity of binary spike coding and the dense token interactions introduced by global self-attention. To address these challenges, this work proposes BSViT, a burst spiking-driven Vision Transformer featuring a Dual-Channel Burst Spiking Self-Attention (DBSSA) mechanism. DBSSA encodes queries with binary spikes and keys with burst spikes to enhance representational capacity. The value pathway adopts dual excitatory and inhibitory binary channels, enabling signed modulation and richer spike interactions. Importantly, the entire attention operation preserves addition-only computation, ensuring compatibility with energy-efficient neuromorphic hardware. To further reduce spike activity and incorporate spatial priors, a patch adjacency masking strategy is introduced to restrict attention to local neighborhoods, resulting in structure-aware sparsity and reduced computational overhead. In addition, burst spike coding is systematically integrated across the network to increase spike-level representational capacity beyond conventional binary spiking. Extensive experiments on both static and event-based vision benchmarks demonstrate that BSViT consistently outperforms existing spiking Transformers in accuracy while maintaining competitive energy efficiency.

</details>


### [109] [VISTA: Video Interaction Spatio-Temporal Analysis Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01391)
*Alejandro Aparcedo,Akash Kumar,Aaryan Garg,Dalton Pham,Wen-Kai Chen,Anirudh Bharadwaj,Aman Chadha,Yogesh Rawat*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VISTA是一个面向开放集、多实体、多动作场景的视频交互时空理解基准，包含约1.2万个视频-查询对，通过分解实体、动作和关系实现多轴诊断，系统评估了11个SOTA VLM并揭示了传统指标无法捕捉的时空偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM基准仅评估简单单动作视频、封闭属性集和受限实体类型，缺乏对真实世界中自由形式、多实体多动作交互的理解能力，且缺少系统性框架来分析模型在互补时空轴上的失败模式。

Method: 将视频分解为可解释的实体、关联动作及关系动态，构建多轴诊断框架；整合多数据集形成统一交互感知分类法，构建约1.2万视频-查询对；在分类法下对11个SOTA VLM进行系统性评估和细粒度性能分解。

Result: VISTA揭示了VLM在时空理解上的显著弱点和偏差，这些偏差被传统评估指标所掩盖，通过分类法驱动的细粒度诊断提供了传统指标无法体现的模型性能洞察。

Conclusion: VISTA是首个大规模、交互感知的VLM时空理解诊断基准，为模型设计、预训练策略和评估协议提供了精细化指导框架。

Abstract: Existing benchmarks for Vision-Language Models (VLMs) primarily evaluate spatio-temporal understanding on simple single-action videos, closed attribute sets and restricted entity types, failing to capture the freeform, multi-action interactions between diverse entities which characterize real-world video understanding. Furthermore, the lack of a systematic framework for analyzing model failures across complementary spatio-temporal axes hinders comprehensive evaluation. To address these gaps, we introduce VISTA, a Video Interaction Spatio-Temporal Analysis benchmark designed for open-set, multi-entity and multi-action spatio-temporal understanding in VLMs. VISTA decomposes videos into interpretable entities, their associated actions, and relational dynamics, enabling multi-axis diagnostics and unified assessment of relational, spatial, and temporal understanding. Our benchmark integrates multiple datasets into a single interaction-aware taxonomy and comprises ~12K curated video-query pairs spanning diverse scenes and complexities. We systematically evaluate 11 state-of-the-art VLMs on VISTA, and break down aggregate performance across our taxonomy to reveal shortcomings and pronounced spatio-temporal biases obscured by traditional metrics. By providing detailed, taxonomy-driven diagnostics on a challenging dataset, VISTA offers a nuanced framework to guide advances in model design, pretraining strategies, and evaluation protocols. Overall, VISTA is the first, large-scale, interaction-aware diagnostic benchmark for spatio-temporal understanding in VLMs.

</details>


### [110] [GEASS: Gated Evidence-Adaptive Selective Caption Trust for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01733)
*Zeshang Li,Shuoyang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现直接拼接生成字幕会降低VLM回答准确性（如Qwen2.5-VL-3B在HallusionBench上准确率下降近10%），原因在于字幕效用是逐查询的属性：同一字幕帮助全局问题但损害细节问题，其机制是嵌入字幕与图像竞争注意力，将模型证据拉向自身文本。基于此提出GEASS（门控证据自适应选择性字幕信任），一个无训练、仅logit级别的模块，通过干净路径的置信度门控、熵降低加权、以及证据门槛提升来决定每查询对字幕的信任程度。在4个VLM和2个基准上，GEASS在单次固定设置下优于标准推理和对比解码，仅增加两次前向传播且无额外参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作假设生成字幕可以作为辅助证据减少VLM的物体幻觉，但作者发现直接拼接字幕反而可能降低准确率。需要理解为何字幕在某些情况下有害，并设计更好的利用方法。

Method: 首先构建诊断数据集GD-Probe（成对全局-细节问题）来隔离分析字幕效用。发现字幕是逐查询属性：同一字幕帮助全局问题、损害细节问题，机制是字幕与图像竞争注意力。基于此提出GEASS：无训练、logit级别模块，通过干净路径置信度门控、熵降低加权、路径不一致时提高证据门槛，逐查询决定字幕信任程度。

Result: GEASS在4个VLM和POPE、HallusionBench两个基准上，单次固定设置下优于标准推理和对比解码，仅需两次前向传播且无额外参数。

Conclusion: 直接拼接生成字幕不可靠，字幕效用是逐查询属性。GEASS通过门控机制有效利用字幕辅助证据，避免了简单拼接带来的负面影响。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) hallucinate objects that are not present, and a growing line of work tries to curb this by feeding the model its own generated caption as auxiliary evidence -- assuming that a caption, once available, is something to consume. We show this fails: naively appending a caption can lower accuracy rather than raise it, dropping Qwen2.5-VL-3B$^\dagger$ on HallusionBench by nearly ten points. To understand why, we build \textbf{GD-Probe}, a diagnostic set that pairs a global and a detail question on the same image, so that any difference in caption effect is attributable to the question alone. Caption utility proves to be a \emph{per-query} property: the same caption helps global questions and harms detail ones, through a single mechanism -- an embedded caption competes with the image for attention and pulls the model's evidence onto its own text -- whose sign is set by whether the caption \emph{covers} the queried content. Crucially, this regime is readable from quantities the decoder already emits, with no attention access or grounding. We turn this into \textbf{GEASS} (Gated Evidence-Adaptive Selective Caption Trust), a training-free, logit-level module that decides per query how much of the caption to trust, gating it by the clean path's confidence, weighting it by the entropy reduction it induces, and raising the evidence bar when the two pathways disagree. Across four VLMs and two benchmarks (POPE and HallusionBench), GEASS improves over both vanilla inference and contrastive decoding under a single fixed setting, adding only two forward passes and no parameters.

</details>


### [111] [GeoWorld-VLM: Geometry from World Models for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16713)
*Renjie Gu,Kaichen Zhou,Yan Luo,Mengyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoWorld-VLM提出一种VLM端的蒸馏框架，通过将冻结的摄像机条件视频世界模型中的几何结构迁移到VLM中，仅微调图像编码器和多模态投影器，使后投影图像特征与中间世界模型表示对齐，从而在不损害语言能力的前提下提升视觉空间推理能力，在What'sUp和VSR基准上均提升约4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在语义识别上表现优异，但对基础空间关系（如左、上、后、之间）的判断仍然脆弱，根本原因在于视觉通路在特征提取阶段压缩或丢弃了关键3D结构线索，导致语言模型接收到的图像表示不足以支持可靠的空间判断。

Method: 提出GeoWorld-VLM框架，使用冻结的摄像机条件视频世界模型作为教师，将静态图像输入通过采样摄像机轨迹转换为合成多视图空间信号；仅微调VLM的图像编码器和多模态投影器，使后投影图像特征与教师模型的中间表示对齐；训练结合空间答案监督、师生特征对齐和原始VLM保留锚定，语言模型保持冻结。

Result: 在两种不同VLM架构上均观察到一致改进，在What'sUp和VSR基准上均提升约4%，表明世界模型引导的视觉对齐能够跨模型结构和空间推理数据集泛化。

Conclusion: GeoWorld-VLM通过增强视觉通路而非语言模型来改善空间推理能力，证明了世界模型引导的视觉特征对齐是一种有效且通用的空间推理增强方法。

Abstract: Modern Vision-Language Models (VLMs) achieve strong semantic recognition, yet remain brittle on elementary spatial relations such as left of, on, behind, and between. One cause of this failure arises before language reasoning begins: the visual pathway may compress or discard critical 3D structural cues during feature extraction, so the language model receives image representations that are already insufficient for reliable spatial judgment. We introduce GeoWorld-VLM, a VLM-side distillation framework that transfers geometric structure from frozen camera-conditioned video world models into VLMs. GeoWorld-VLM fine-tunes only the image encoder and multimodal projector, aligning post-projector image features with intermediate world-model representations while leaving the main backbone frozen. Given images, a prompt, and a sampled camera trajectory, the world-model teacher converts static visual input into a synthetic multi-view spatial signal. Training combines spatial answer supervision, teacher-student feature alignment, and a preservation anchor to the original VLM. Since the language model remains frozen, GeoWorld-VLM preserves the original model's linguistic capabilities while attributing spatial improvements to the enhanced visual pathway. To evaluate the effectiveness and generality of the proposed method, we apply GeoWorld-VLM to two distinct VLM architectures and observe consistent improvements across both backbones. GeoWorld-VLM improves performance by approximately 4 percent on both the What'sUp and VSR benchmarks, suggesting that world-model-guided visual alignment generalizes across model structures and spatial reasoning datasets.

</details>


### [112] [Appearance-Invariant Detection of Suggestive Motion via Laban Movement Descriptors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24488)
*Jaehoon Ahn,Jeonghan Kong,Moon-Ryul Jung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Laban运动分析描述符的纯运动分类流程，通过SMPL骨架轨迹检测在线3D虚拟环境中的暗示性及露骨动作。在17+小时视频数据集上，逻辑回归达到68%的SFW/NSFW二分类准确率（随机森林70%），其性能与基于同一运动重新渲染的无外观视频训练的模型相当。轨迹迂曲度（间接性）在暗示性动作层级达到峰值，为Laban空间因子中的直接-间接极性提供了可解释的标记。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线多人3D虚拟环境中的内容审核日益自动化，但现有检测集中在图像、视频和音频，忽略了暗示性动作这一盲区。

Method: 使用Laban运动分析描述符（61维特征）对SMPL骨架轨迹进行特征提取，训练逻辑回归和随机森林分类器进行二分类（SFW/NSFW）。评估采用无泄漏协议，并与基于外观无关视频（灰色无服装/皮肤/场景的人形）训练的学习型视频模型进行对比。

Result: 逻辑回归68%准确率，随机森林70%准确率；轨迹迂曲度在暗示性动作层级达到峰值，可作为可解释性标记。

Conclusion: 基于Laban的kinematic描述符为暗示性动作检测提供了轻量级、可解释的方法，每个决策可分解为有命名的理论依据特征；分类器仅基于姿态轨迹，可在不依赖外观数据的虚拟环境中直接运行。

Abstract: Content moderation in online multiplayer 3D virtual environments is increasingly automated, yet detection has focused on images, video, and audio, leaving suggestive motion a blind spot. We present a motion-only classification pipeline that detects suggestive and explicit movement from SMPL skeleton trajectories using Laban Movement Analysis (LMA) descriptors. On a dataset spanning everyday, artistic, suggestive, and explicit movement (17+ hours of video), a logistic regression trained on 61-feature LMA descriptors reaches 68% binary SFW/NSFW accuracy (70% random forest) under a leak-free evaluation protocol. At this level, our descriptor performs comparably to a learned video model trained on the same motion re-rendered as appearance-free video, a gray figure with no clothing, skin, or scene. The indirectness (tortuosity) of each joint's trajectory, measured as the ratio of the joint's path length to its net displacement, peaks at the suggestive tier, showing that the Direct-to-Indirect polarity of Laban's Space factor provides an interpretable marker of the shift from functional to suggestive motion. Ultimately, Laban-based kinematic descriptors offer a lightweight, interpretable approach to suggestive-motion detection: every decision decomposes into named, theory-grounded features. Because the classifier operates on pose trajectories alone, moderation can run directly on avatar poses in virtual environments, with no appearance data.

</details>


### [113] [Spatially Grounded Concept Bottleneck Models via Part-Factorized Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04364)
*Dhanesh Ramachandram*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种部件分解的概念瓶颈模型（Part-Factorized CBM），通过固定DINOv3视觉Transformer、可学习前景门控、部件查询和二维高斯先验，强制每个概念头仅从对应身体部位读取证据，在CUB-200-2011上达到88.85% top-1准确率（完全监督基线88.95%），并将指向准确率从36.4%提升至52.6%，且仅需约27张图像初始化先验即可。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准概念瓶颈模型（CBM）中，概念头可以自由关注图像任意区域，导致命名指代某身体部位的概念头可能被其他部位的证据所满足，破坏了模型的可审计性和部件对应性。

Method: 三个组件：1）基于DINOv3 patch特征训练的**前景门控**，抑制背景patch的注意力；2）**部件查询**交叉关注patch特征，通过固定概念-部件映射将312个CUB属性路由到对应部件token读取；3）**可学习二维高斯先验**（在log空间加性注入注意力logits），均值初始化为数据集平均关键点位置，无需逐张图像的关键点监督。

Result: CUB-200-2011上：空间先验模型达到88.85% top-1准确率（完全监督基线88.95%），指向准确率提升16个百分点至52.6%；用PCA前景目标替代边界框监督，结合高斯先验达到88.6% top-1和~70%指向准确率；仅需0.5%训练集（约27张图像）初始化先验；无空间先准时指向准确率暴跌至2.9%。

Conclusion: 通过构造性强制概念-部件对应，在几乎不损失分类准确率的前提下大幅提升模型的可审计性和部件定位能力，且所需监督极少。

Abstract: Concept bottleneck models (CBMs) predict a layer of human-named attributes before predicting a class, which makes their decisions auditable. On fine-grained recognition tasks the concept heads are usually free to attend anywhere in the image, so a head named for one body region can be satisfied by evidence on another. This work studies a part-factorized CBM that removes that freedom by construction. The method has three components built on a frozen DINOv3 vision transformer. A learned foreground gate, trained on DINOv3 patch features, suppresses background patches inside the part attention. A set of part queries cross-attends to patch features and each of the 312 CUB attributes is routed, through a fixed concept-to-part map, to read only from the part token its name implies. A learnable two-dimensional Gaussian prior, injected additively in log space into the attention logits, breaks the permutation symmetry among part queries; its means are initialized from the dataset-average keypoint location of each part, which requires no per-image keypoint supervision at training or test time. On CUB-200-2011 the spatial-prior model matches a fully supervised baseline (88.85% versus 88.95% top-1) while raising pointing accuracy by 16 points (52.6% versus 36.4%). Replacing bounding-box supervision with a PCA foreground target and combining it with the Gaussian prior removes all per-image supervision and reaches 88.6% top-1 at about 70% pointing accuracy. A keypoint-fraction sweep shows that 0.5% of the training set (about 27 images) suffices to initialize the prior with no measurable loss. Removing part identity entirely is the harder case: without any spatial prior, pointing accuracy collapses to $2.9\%$.

</details>


### [114] [Where, What, Why, and Importance: Structured Defect Grounding for Text-to-Image Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06113)
*Huaisong Zhang,Hao Yu,Yuxuan Zhang,Jiahe Wang,Xinrui Chen,Haoxiang Cao,Feng Lu,Wendong Zhang,Changqian Yu,Chun Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出结构化缺陷定位（SDG），将文生图诊断建模为结构化集合预测（位置、类型、原因、重要性四元组），并配套构建SDG-30K数据集和SDG-Eval评估协议，进一步提出诊断到对齐框架（VLM检测器+BoxFlow-GRPO奖励），在缺陷定位和对齐提升上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生图诊断方法依赖热图回归，难以定位可变数量的缺陷并将语义原因绑定到个体缺陷上，缺乏实例级的结构化反馈能力。

Method: 1) 提出SDG，将T2I诊断建模为结构化集合预测，每个缺陷表示为(位置, 类型, 原因, 重要性)元组；2) 构建SDG-30K数据集（30K图像，四类T2I生成器，框级标注）；3) 提出SDG-Eval评估协议；4) 构建诊断到对齐框架，VLM作为SDG检测器，BoxFlow-GRPO将缺陷集转化为框导出、重要性加权的空间奖励用于扩散模型对齐。

Result: SDG检测器在结构化缺陷定位上超越领先的闭源VLM；SDG引导的奖励一致性地提升T2I对齐质量，并支持局部图像精炼。

Conclusion: SDG为诊断、评估和增强现代生成模型提供了统一的实例级接口，突破了传统热图回归的表征瓶颈。

Abstract: Despite generating increasingly photorealistic images, text-to-image (T2I) models still exhibit localized, subtle, and structurally complex failures. Diagnosing these failures requires instance-level feedback that answers where a defect occurs, what type it is, why it is defective, and its importance to overall image quality. While recent dense-feedback methods move beyond scalar supervision, their heatmap-centric representations still formulate diagnosis as pixel-field regression, making it difficult to localize variable-cardinality defects and bind semantic reasons to individual failures. To address this representation bottleneck, we propose Structured Defect Grounding (SDG), which casts T2I diagnosis as structured set prediction by modeling each defect as a (location, type, reason, importance) tuple. To make this formulation trainable and measurable, we introduce SDG-30K, a 30K-image dataset with box-grounded annotations across four modern T2I generators, together with a dedicated evaluation protocol, SDG-Eval. Building on this structured representation, we further present a diagnosis-to-alignment framework in which a Vision-Language Model (VLM) serves as the SDG detector, and BoxFlow-GRPO converts predicted defect sets into box-derived, importance-weighted spatial rewards for diffusion model alignment. Extensive experiments show that our SDG detector outperforms leading proprietary VLMs on structured defect grounding, while SDG-guided rewards consistently improve T2I alignment and support localized image refinement. These results establish SDG as a unified, instance-level interface for diagnosing, evaluating, and enhancing modern generative models.

</details>


### [115] [Skill-3D: Evolving Scene-Aware Skills for Agentic 3D Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07436)
*Haoyuan Li,Zhengdong Hu,Jun Wang,Hehe Fan,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Skill-3D框架，通过学习场景感知技能来解决3D空间推理中智能体工具使用不均匀和误用的问题，使工具利用率从39%提升至78%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM智能体在3D空间推理中对所有场景使用统一的工具使用策略，而非根据具体场景和任务选择合适的工具，导致工具误用和偏好偏差，智能体范式相比非智能体策略仅获得边际提升。

Method: Skill-3D框架包含：场景识别与轨迹记录的Scene Memory；将相似场景的成功轨迹聚合蒸馏为可复用场景感知技能，失败轨迹作为教训附加；当相似场景再次出现时注入对应技能引导智能体；新轨迹的成功/失败进一步优化技能，形成记忆与技能库共同演化的闭环。

Result: 在VSI-Bench上工具利用率从39%提升至78%；Gemini-3-Flash在MMSI-Bench上提升67%；对技能引导轨迹进行智能体后训练使Qwen3-VL-8B在VSI-Bench上提升60%。

Conclusion: Skill-3D通过自演化的场景感知技能显著改善了3D空间推理中的工具使用正确性和充分性，验证了场景自适应的工具选择策略优于统一策略。

Abstract: This paper explores agentic 3D spatial understanding, i.e., MLLM agents performing 3D reasoning through tool use. Existing methods often misuse tools and exhibit biased tool preferences under 3D scenarios, leaving the agentic paradigm with only marginal gains over non-agentic strategies. We reveal that 3D spatial reasoning tasks are heterogeneous across scenes, while these agents apply a uniform tool-use strategy to all scenes rather than selecting tools according to the specific scene and task. To address this, we propose Skill-3D, a framework that learns self-evolving scene-aware skills. Specifically, Skill-3D identifies the task scene and records the agent's tool-use trajectory into a Scene Memory, where successful trajectories from similar scenes are aggregated and distilled into a reusable scene-aware skill, with failed ones attached to the skill as lessons. During training, once a similar scene recurs, the corresponding skill is injected to guide the agent, producing new trajectories whose successes and failures further refine the skill, forming a loop in which the memory and the skill library co-evolve. Experiments show that Skill-3D substantially improves tool utilization in 3D spatial reasoning (from 39% to 78% on VSI-Bench), driving the agent toward correct and sufficient tool use. For instance, it improves Gemini-3-Flash by 67% on MMSI-Bench. Furthermore, we conduct agentic post-training over skill-guided trajectories, which boosts Qwen3-VL-8B by 60% on VSI-Bench.

</details>


### [116] [CACR:Reinforcing Temporal Answer Grounding in Instructional Video via Candidate-Aware Causal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08436)
*Muge Qi,Rong Fu,Pengbin Feng,Xianda Li,Yu Cai,Yifu Guo,Shizhe Zhang,Simon James Fong,Lei Ma,Bin Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种候选感知因果推理（CACR）框架用于教学视频中的时间答案定位（TAGV），通过视觉语言预训练的候选选择算法和时序逻辑推理模块，结合拒绝奖励机制和GRPO优化，在六个基准上取得了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法对无关内容敏感、视觉推理能力不足，且难以处理未修剪视频与短目标时刻之间的长度不匹配问题。

Method: 1) 视觉语言预训练候选选择(VBCS)算法高效生成K个候选片段；2) 时序逻辑推理模块，结合拒绝奖励机制，通过GRPO优化进行鲁棒推理。

Result: 在六个基准数据集上，以mIoU指标达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: CACR框架为长视频中基于推理的检索提供了新视角，验证了候选感知因果推理在TAGV任务上的有效性。

Abstract: The task of temporal answer grounding in instructional video (TAGV), which aims to locate precise video segments that respond to natural language queries, is increasingly important for direct video answer retrieval. This task remains challenging due to the need to comprehend semantically complex questions and to address the significant length mismatch between untrimmed videos and short target moments. Existing methods often suffer from sensitivity to irrelevant content or insufficient visual reasoning capabilities. To tackle these limitations, we propose a Candidate-Aware Causal Reasoning (CACR) framework. Our approach first employs a Visual-Language Pre-training based Candidate Selection (VBCS) algorithm to efficiently generate K candidate segments, then applies a temporal logic reasoning module enhanced by a rejection reward mechanism and optimized via Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) for robust inference. Extensive experiments on six benchmarks demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance in terms of mean Intersection-over-Union (mIoU), providing a new perspective for reasoning-based retrieval in long videos.

</details>


### [117] [CineDance: Towards Next-Generation Multi-Shot Long-Form Cinematic Audio-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09639)
*Yuheng Chen,Teng Hu,Yuji Wang,Qingdong He,Zhucun Xue,Qianyu Zhou,Jason Li,Lizhuang Ma,Jiangning Zhang,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CineDance-1M，一个大规模、开放研究的多镜头长视频文本-音频-视频联合生成数据集，通过三阶段策展流程实现高质量，并配套CineBench评估体系和CineDance模型，以推动开放视频生成研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源视频生成模型受限于高质量训练数据的稀缺，而商业系统已具备生成电影级叙事的能力。为弥合这一差距，亟需构建大规模、高质量的开放研究数据集。

Method: 构建CineDance-1M数据集，采用三阶段策展流程：i)多样化来源与全面清洗，ii)基于电影理论的叙事解析，iii)层次化双模态标注。提出CineBench评估体系，包含多样提示集和六维度人工对齐指标。基于LTX-2.3适配CineDance模型进行验证。

Result: CineDance-1M平均每个视频时长92.8秒、包含24.2个连续镜头；CineDance模型在单模态质量、音视频对齐、主体与环境一致性方面表现优异，验证了策展策略和数据集的高质量。

Conclusion: 该工作为多镜头长视频联合音频-视频生成领域的未来研究提供了坚实基础，有望加速该方向的进展。

Abstract: The fidelity and structural diversity of training datasets fundamentally determine the capabilities of video generation models. While commercial systems showremarkableabilitytogeneratecinematicnarratives, the progress of open-source models remains limited by the scarcity of high-quality training data. To bridge this gap, we introduce CineDance-1M, a large-scale, open research Text-to-Audio-Video (T2AV) dataset designed specifically for multi-shot, long-form joint audio-video generation. Averaging 92.8 seconds and 24.2 continuous shots per video, it provides configurable, structured annotations for both audio and video modalities. This exceptional quality is achieved through a rigorous three-stage curation pipeline: i) diverse sourcing and comprehensive cleansing, ii) film-theory-inspired narrative parsing, and iii) hierarchical dual-modal captioning. For a comprehensive assessment, we propose CineBench, featuring a diverse prompt suite and a six-dimensional, human-aligned metric system tailored for complex narrative audio-video evaluation. Furthermore, we adapt LTX-2.3 into CineDance, which demonstrates exceptional single-modality quality alongside precise audio-video alignment and robust subject and environment consistency, effectively validating our curation strategy and the high quality of CineDance-1M. We anticipate that this work will serve as a solid foundation for accelerating future research in multi-shot, long-form joint audio-video generation. Our project page is available at https://aliothchen.github.io/projects/CineDance/.

</details>


### [118] [An Improved Generative Adversarial Network for Micro-Resistivity Imaging Logging Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10200)
*Ahmed Faizul Haque,S. M. Riaz Rahman Antu,Saif Ahmed,Asadullah Hil Galib,Souvik Pramanik,Mohammad Ashrafuzzaman Khan,Mohammad Abdul Qayum,Mohsin Sajjad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于改进GAN的微电阻率成像测井图像修复方法，通过FCN生成网络、深度可分离卷积残差块、Inception模块、多尺度特征提取模块和空间注意力残差块，结合全局与局部判别网络，实现了对部分缺失图像的高质量修复，实验平均结构相似度达0.903，较其他方法提升约0.3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决微电阻率成像测井图像部分缺失的问题，为后续测井图像解释提供可靠的深度学习修复方法。

Method: 以FCN为生成网络基础架构，添加深度可分离卷积残差块保留有效像素与语义信息；引入Inception模块增加多尺度感受野并减少参数量；增加多尺度特征提取模块和空间注意力残差块，结合通道注意力机制实现多尺度特征提取；设计全局判别网络与局部判别网络，通过与生成网络相互对抗逐步提升修复区域与整图的内容及语义结构一致性。

Result: 测试集五组不同大小缺失区域的成像测井图像平均结构相似度（SSIM）为0.903，较其他类似方法提升约0.3。

Conclusion: 该方法可用于微电阻率成像测井图像修复，在语义结构连贯性和纹理细节方面均有良好改善，为后续图像解释提供了新的深度学习方法保障。

Abstract: An improved GAN-based imaging logging image restoration method is presented in this paper for solving the problem of partially missing micro-resistivity imaging logging images. The method uses FCN as the generative network infrastructure and adds a depth-separable convolutional residual block to learn and retain more effective pixel and semantic information; an Inception module is added to increase the multi-scale perceptual field of the network and reduce the number of parameters in the network; and a multi-scale feature extraction module and a spatial attention residual block are added to combine the channel attention. The multi-scale module adds a multi-scale feature extraction module and a spatial attention residual block, which combine the channel attention mechanism and the residual block to achieve multi-scale feature extraction. The global discriminative network and the local discriminative network are designed to gradually improve the content and semantic structure coherence between the restored parts and the whole image by playing off each other and the generative network. According to the experimental results, the average structural similarity measure of the five sets of imaged logging images with different sizes of missing regions in the test set is 0.903, which is an improvement of about 0.3 compared with other similar methods. It is shown that the method in this study can be used for the restoration of micro-resistivity imaging log images with good improvement in semantic structural coherence and texture details, thus providing a new deep learning method to ensure the smooth advancement of the subsequent interpretation of micro-resistivity imaging log images.

</details>


### [119] [MultiToP: Learning to Patch Visual Tokens to Mitigate Hallucinations in Video Large Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11792)
*Yuansheng Gao,Wenbin Xing,Jiahao Yuan,Kaiwen Zhou,Han Bao,Zonghui Wang,Wenzhi Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MultiToP提出了一种多模态上下文感知的视觉标记修补框架，通过选择性替换不可靠的视觉标记来减少视频多模态大模型中的幻觉问题，在Vript-HAL上F1提升50.60%，在ActivityNet-QA上准确率提升18.58%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频大语言多模态模型在视频理解中仍存在严重的幻觉问题，即生成内容与输入视频不匹配。现有方法缺乏细粒度、轻量级的视觉证据修正手段，且往往需要修改原始模型结构。

Method: 提出MultiToP框架：1）轻量级Visual Token Patcher（视觉标记修补器）预测token级替换分布；2）使用动态全局修补token选择性替换不可靠的视觉标记；3）提出信息引导的排名校准机制，利用答案条件化的帧级信息线索指导替换；4）结合真实答案监督与稀疏正则化训练，无需修改原始模型。

Result: 在Vript-HAL上，Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct的F1分数提升50.60%；在ActivityNet-QA上，Video-LLaVA-7B相对准确率提升18.58%，且推理开销极小。

Conclusion: MultiToP能有效减少视频多模态大模型的幻觉，保持良好的通用视频理解能力，且以轻量级、即插即用的方式实现，无需修改原始模型。

Abstract: Video Large Multimodal Models have achieved remarkable progress in video understanding, yet they remain prone to hallucinations, where generated responses are not faithfully supported by the input video. In this paper, we propose MultiToP, a multimodal-context-aware visual token patching framework that mitigates hallucinations by refining unreliable visual tokens before language generation. MultiToP introduces a lightweight Visual Token Patcher to predict token-level replacement distributions and selectively substitute unreliable visual tokens with a dynamic global patch token. To train the patcher effectively, we further propose information-guided rank calibration, which uses answer-conditioned frame-level information cues derived from the backbone to guide token replacement. Combined with ground-truth answer supervision and sparsity regularization, MultiToP enables localized visual evidence refinement without modifying the original model. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MultiToP effectively reduces hallucinations on Vript-HAL with negligible inference overhead, improving the F1 scores of Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct by 50.60% over the vanilla model. Meanwhile, MultiToP preserves general video understanding ability, yielding an 18.58% relative accuracy gain on ActivityNet-QA for Video-LLaVA-7B.

</details>


### [120] [Wild3R: Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian Splatting from Unconstrained Sparse Photo Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11894)
*Yuto Furutani,Takashi Otonari,Kaede Shiohara,Toshihiko Yamasaki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Wild3R是一种前馈式3D高斯泼溅方法，用于处理不受约束的稀疏照片集。通过构建包含200个场景、170种光照条件和瞬态物体的WildCity数据集（共337,500张图像），该方法学习跨视角的外观一致性并去除瞬态内容，在性能上超越现有前馈方法，并接近基于每场景优化的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈式3DGS方法在处理真实世界照片集时，难以应对多样的光照条件和瞬态物体；同时缺乏同时包含多视角、多种光照和瞬态变化的训练数据。

Method: 提出Wild3R模型，通过构建WildCity大规模数据集（200场景、170光照条件、瞬态物体、337,500张图像），学习以参考视图为条件的跨视角外观一致性，同时去除瞬态内容。

Result: Wild3R在不受约束的稀疏照片集上优于现有前馈方法，并与基于每场景优化的方法达到竞争性结果。

Conclusion: 通过大规模多样化数据集和外观一致性学习，前馈式3DGS方法能够有效处理真实世界中光照变化和瞬态物体的挑战，缩小了与每场景优化方法的差距。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) removes the need for time-consuming per-scene optimization required by traditional 3DGS. However, existing feed-forward approaches struggle with real-world photo collections that include diverse lighting conditions and transient objects. In this paper, we present Wild3R, a feed-forward approach for unconstrained sparse photo collections. The main bottleneck is the lack of training data that provides multiple viewpoints, a variety of illuminations, and transient variations necessary for learning robust scene representations. To address this, we introduce the WildCity dataset, which comprises 200 scenes, 170 lighting conditions, and transient objects, resulting in 337,500 images in total. By leveraging the dataset, our model learns appearance consistency across viewpoints conditioned on reference views, while removing transient content. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method outperforms existing feed-forward approaches and achieves results competitive with prior per-scene optimization-based methods.

</details>


### [121] [VOID: Defeating Unauthorized Mimicry in Latent Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12263)
*Chunlin Qiu,Ang Li,Tianxiao Huang,Ruilin Gan,Yunjie Ge,Shenyi Zhang,Huayi Duan,Lingchen Zhao,Chao Shen,Qian Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对潜在扩散模型（LDM）被滥用于未经授权模仿个人身份的问题，现有防御方法依赖的"微小扰动在LDM长生成过程中保持欺骗效果"的假设不成立——模型的内在修复机制会消除扰动导致身份重现。本文提出VOID框架，通过操控LDM固有随机性，采用两种新方式扰动扩散管道：1）放大潜编码误差以破坏图像语义结构；2）抵消目标引导信号以抑制模型修复能力。VOID在保护图像的同时将扰动限制在人眼不可感知区域，在24种防御方法、10种模仿攻击、5个数据集上的评估显示，其将平均FID从113提升至365，比最强现有防御提升223%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对LDM被滥用于未经授权模仿个人的防御方法基于一个有缺陷的假设：微小扰动能在LDM的整个生成过程中保持欺骗效果。实际上，LDM固有的修复机制会消除这些扰动，导致个体身份在生成图像中重新浮现。因此，需要一种新的防御框架来解决这一根本性矛盾。

Method: 提出VOID防御框架，通过操控LDM的固有随机性来克服扰动被消除的问题。具体包含两种创新扰动方式：1）放大潜编码误差以破坏图像的语义结构；2）抵消目标引导信号以抑制模型的修复能力。同时，将扰动限制在保护图像中人眼不可感知的区域，以保持视觉可用性。

Result: 在24种最先进防御方法、10种模仿攻击、5个数据集上的全面评估中，VOID将平均FID从113提升至365，相比当前最强防御实现了223%的提升，展现出前所未有的保护能力。

Conclusion: VOID通过操控LDM的随机性而非依赖微小扰动的持久性，从根本上解决了现有防御的局限性，实现了安全性与视觉可用性的兼顾，为防御LDM被滥用于未经授权模仿提供了有效方案。

Abstract: While Latent Diffusion Models (LDMs) have revolutionized visual synthesis, they are increasingly exploited for unauthorized mimicry of individuals. Existing defenses inject deceptive perturbations to steer the generated images toward irrelevant targets. However, this approach hinges on an ungrounded assumption: subtle perturbations can maintain their deceptive efficacy throughout an LDM's extensive generation process. In reality, the model's innate restoration mechanism will remove such perturbations and cause individual identities to re-emerge in the images generated.
  We propose VOID, a defense framework that overcomes this conundrum by manipulating an LDM's intrinsic stochasticity. VOID perturbs the diffusion pipeline in two novel ways: 1) amplifying the latent encoding errors to shatter an image's semantic structure, and 2) counteracting the target guidance signals to suppress the model's restoration capabilities. This results in a semantic corruption that thwarts any unauthorized mimicry. Notably, the security gain does not come at the price of visual utility, as VOID simultaneously manages to confine perturbations to human-imperceptible regions of protected images. Our comprehensive evaluation of 24 state-of-the-art defenses against 10 mimicry attacks on 5 datasets demonstrates VOID's unprecedented protection power: it increases the average Frechet Inception Distance (FID) from 113 to 365, a 223% improvement over the strongest defense to date.

</details>


### [122] [DepthMaster: Unified Monocular Depth Estimation for Perspective and Panoramic Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12368)
*Pengfei Wang,Shihao Wang,Liyi Chen,Zhiyuan Ma,Guowen Zhang,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DepthMaster 是一个统一的度量深度估计框架，通过将全景图像分解为重叠的透视补丁，引入对应一致性损失（CCL）和虚拟投影相机几何先验，无需专用网络或特殊算子即可实现窄视场角和360°全景的通用度量深度估计，在13个零样本数据集上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常针对特定相机类型设计，无法在窄视场角透视和360°全景之间泛化；全景训练数据稀缺且带有度量标注；透视相机与全景相机之间存在固有的几何差异。

Method: 将全景图像分解为重叠的透视补丁，统一为规范的透视表示；提出对应一致性损失（CCL）来无缝拼接补丁；注入虚拟投影相机作为几何先验；保持骨干网络与标准Transformer设计兼容，避免专用算子。

Result: 仅使用一个全景数据集混合训练，在13个多样化数据集上实现了零样本SOTA性能，不仅超越通用方法，还超越了透视和全景领域的领先专用模型。

Conclusion: 通过将全景问题重构成透视图块处理，并利用虚拟投影相机先验和对应一致性损失，可以有效统一不同相机类型的度量深度估计，同时利用大量透视数据缓解全景数据稀缺问题。

Abstract: While monocular depth estimation has achieved significant progress, achieving generalized metric depth estimation for both narrow field-of-view (FoV) perspectives and $360^\circ$ panoramas remains an unsolved challenge. Existing methods are often tailored to specific camera types and struggle to produce accurate metric depth that generalizes across diverse settings. This limitation stems from two key challenges: the inherent geometric discrepancy between perspective and panoramic cameras, and the scarcity of panoramic training data with metric annotations. In this work, we introduce DepthMaster, a unified metric depth estimation framework. Rather than employing specialized networks to learn spherical distortions, we reformulate the problem by decomposing panoramic images into overlapping perspective patches. Crucially, distinct from prior projection-based methods that rely on ad-hoc architectural modifications to handle boundaries, we introduce a novel Correspondence Consistency Loss (CCL) and inject virtual projection cameras as geometric priors, allowing us to seamlessly stitch the patches while avoiding specialized operators and keeping the backbone largely compatible with standard Transformer designs. This strategy also resolves the geometric differences by unifying all inputs into a canonical perspective representation, and effectively circumvents data scarcity by directly unlocking powerful metric priors from vast perspective datasets. Trained on a mixed dataset that contains only one panorama dataset, DepthMaster achieves state-of-the-art zero-shot performance on 13 diverse datasets, outperforming not only universal methods but also leading specialist models in both perspective and panoramic domains.

</details>

# q-bio.NC [[Back]](#toc)

### [123] [A quantum-like benchmark for context-sensitive associative memory with adaptive plasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12449)
*Yashine H. Goolam Hossen,Lea Gassab,Travis J. A. Craddock*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于阶段性联想回忆的顺序敏感自适应可塑性基准测试，比较了类量子联想记忆模型与匹配的实值无相位和马尔可夫率控制模型。结果显示，在弱结构支持下，自适应可塑性（特别是稳态稳定化）是回忆增益的主要来源；马尔可夫率控制模型在原始回忆强度上更强，但类量子模型在顺序敏感性和阶段依赖性组织方面更一致。研究不支持类量子模型的普遍优势，而主张通过多目标特征（回忆、时间组织、上下文敏感性）来区分模型类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆学习需要在可塑性与稳定性之间取得平衡，但现有联想记忆模型在固定背景连接已承载部分任务时，难以区分真正的回忆动力学与结构性辅助。因此需要一种受控基准来测试模型在弱支持条件下的上下文敏感记忆动态。

Method: 开发了顺序敏感的阶段性联想回忆基准，比较类量子联想记忆模型与实值无相位和马尔可夫率控制模型，在相同任务调度、扰动剖面、弱支持条件和可塑性设置下进行阶乘比较。

Result: 弱结构支持的可用范围狭窄且非单调；单独弱结构在无可塑性消融实验中无法挽救回忆；回忆增益主要来自自适应可塑性，尤其是稳态稳定化；马尔可夫率控制模型原始回忆更强，但类量子模型更一致地保持顺序敏感性和阶段组织。

Conclusion: 不存在普遍的类量子优势；模型类别应通过多目标特征（回忆、时间组织、上下文敏感性）来区分，而非单一回忆分数；该基准为研究弱支持、受调节可塑性和匹配经典比较下的上下文敏感记忆动态提供了受控框架。

Abstract: Learning and memory require a balance between plasticity and stability: synaptic connections must encode new information without collapsing, saturating, or erasing previously useful structure. Associative-memory models can appear to learn successfully when fixed background connectivity already carries part of the task, making it difficult to distinguish genuine recall dynamics from structural assistance. We test this issue using an order-sensitive adaptive-plasticity benchmark for staged associative recall. The benchmark compares a quantum-like associative-memory model with matched real-valued no-phase and Markov-rate controls under the same task schedule, perturbation profiles, weak-support conditions, and plasticity settings. Here, "quantum-like" refers to the modeling formalism, not to a biological claim about quantum computation. We first screen weak structural support and then fix a conservative operating point for factorial comparisons across model families and plasticity mechanisms. The useful weak-support regime is narrow and non-monotonic. Weak structure alone does not rescue recall in the no-plasticity ablation, whereas most useful recall gains arise from adaptive plasticity, especially homeostatic stabilization. The Markov-rate control often achieves stronger raw recall, but the quantum-like model more consistently preserves order sensitivity and stage-dependent organization. These results do not support a universal quantum-like advantage. Instead, they show that model classes are better distinguished by a multi-objective profile combining recall, temporal organization, and context sensitivity than by any single recall score. The benchmark therefore provides a controlled framework for studying context-sensitive memory dynamics under weak support, regulated plasticity, and matched classical comparison.

</details>


### [124] [Multifractal human signals at the edge of life reveal a heart-brain anti-correlation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12600)
*Yago Emanoel Ramos,Maria Eloá do Ó,Henrique Ferraz de Arruda,Mauro Copelli,G. Camelo-Neto,Pedro V. Carelli*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本研究使用MF-DFA分析临终患者同步EEG和ECG时间序列，发现神经活动多重分形谱塌缩而心脏信号谱异常展宽，两者负相关表明神经-心脏系统功能解耦，揭示了一种由外周向中枢崩溃的身体-大脑解体过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索临终过程中人体神经生理功能的终极崩溃机制，特别是通过非线性动力学视角分析神经与心脏系统之间的耦合关系如何解体。

Method: 采用多重分形去趋势波动分析（MF-DFA）量化临终患者同步采集的脑电图（EEG）和心电图（ECG）时间序列的复杂性时间演化特征。

Result: 神经活动多重分形性塌缩至受限态，而心脏信号出现异常谱展宽（非线性波动增加、动力学不稳定性增强），两者谱宽呈负相关，表明有效功能解耦和反相关动力学。

Conclusion: 临终过程并非均匀生理衰退，而是身体-大脑的解体——外周功能障碍逐步压倒中枢调控；观察到的反向趋势与其他外周驱动适应过程模式一致，提示耦合系统间逆向动力学可能源于外周而非中枢约束；死亡是跨系统整合崩溃的极端形式。

Abstract: This study investigates the terminal breakdown of human neurophysiological function through the lens of non-linear dynamics by analyzing the multifractal spectrum. Using Multifractal Detrended Fluctuation Analysis (MF-DFA), we quantify the temporal evolution of complexity in synchronized electroencephalogram (EEG) and electrocardiogram (ECG) time series from patients in the terminal stage. Our results reveal a marked divergence in multifractal spectrum width: while neural activity exhibits a collapse of multifractality toward a more constrained state, cardiac signals undergo anomalous spectral broadening, indicating increased non-linear fluctuations and dynamical instability. A negative correlation between these spectral widths suggests effective functional decoupling and the emergence of anti-correlated dynamics between neural and cardiac systems. Rather than reflecting a uniform physiological decline, this divergence is consistent with a body-to-brain breakdown in which peripheral dysfunction progressively overwhelms central regulatory processes. In a broader context, the observed opposing trends resemble patterns reported in other body-driven adaptive processes, suggesting that inverse dynamics across coupled systems may emerge when constraints originate from peripheral rather than central mechanisms. Ultimately, the dying process appears to represent an extreme form of cross-system disintegration, marked by the collapse of the hierarchical coordination that normally sustains integrated physiological function.

</details>


### [125] [Phase model analysis of the effect of M-current on neural synchrony in hippocampal networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12684)
*Megha Manoj,Sue Ann Campbell*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文通过研究乙酰胆碱（ACh）对神经元同步性的影响，构建了包含M电流的锥体神经元网络模型，发现低ACh条件下网络可完全同步，高ACh条件下网络会去同步化为多个稳定对称簇解，代表不同的神经集群，从而解释了ACh在记忆编码和巩固中的双向作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索乙酰胆碱（ACh）如何通过影响神经元同步性来调节神经集群的形成，从而支持记忆编码（高ACh）和记忆巩固（低ACh）这一双向假说。

Method: 构建包含M电流（受ACh下调）的锥体神经元网络模型，利用弱耦合相位模型约化方法分析不同耦合架构（全局均匀、对称距离依赖、最近邻）下的簇解稳定性。

Result: 低ACh（高M电流）条件下网络可实现完全同步；高ACh（低M电流）条件下网络去同步化为多个稳定的对称簇解，对应于不同的神经集群。

Conclusion: ACh通过调节M电流调控神经元同步性，进而控制神经集群的形成与分解，这为ACh在记忆编码（高ACh促去同步化形成多个集群）和记忆巩固（低ACh促完全同步）中的双向作用提供了机制解释。

Abstract: Neural assemblies, transiently coordinated groups of neurons, observed in the hippocampus are thought to underlie the formation of episodic memories. Acetylcholine (ACh), a neuromodulator, that is received by the hippocampus, plays a critical role in memory and learning. A well supported hypothesis suggests that high levels of ACh during active exploration and rapid eye movement (REM) sleep promote memory encoding, while low levels during quiet waking and slow-wave sleep (SWS) support memory consolidation. We study this bidirectional role of ACh in neural assembly formation through its effect on the synchrony among neurons. We consider a network model of pyramidal neurons, each equipped with a slow, voltage-dependent, non-inactivating potassium current (M-current), which is downregulated in the presence of ACh. Neural assemblies are represented as cluster solutions to this system. Using a one-dimensional phase model reduction of a pair of weakly coupled pyramidal neurons under different levels of the M-current, we predict the symmetric cluster solutions that may emerge in larger networks equipped with all-to-all globally homogeneous, symmetric distance-dependent and nearest-neighbours coupling architectures. We find that under low ACh conditions, the network can fully synchronize, whereas high levels can desynchronize the network into multiple stable symmetric cluster solutions representing distinct neural assemblies.

</details>


### [126] [Deep Sleep Classification via EEG Signal Criticality: A Passive BCI Approach for Sleep-Improvement Neurofeedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13017)
*Stanisław Narębski,Tomasz Komendziński,Tomasz M. Rutkowski*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本研究利用去趋势波动分析（DFA）提取的临界性特征，通过朴素贝叶斯分类器实现了对深度睡眠（N3）的高准确率自动分期（平衡准确率87.17%），为被动脑机接口中的状态依赖神经反馈提供了鲁棒的感知机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动睡眠分期是被动脑机接口（pBCI）的基础应用，旨在解码自发神经状态以实现不依赖用户意图的闭环干预。本研究致力于评估DFA临界性特征对深度睡眠（N3）的特异性识别能力，并寻找最优的"状态感知"分类引擎以支持神经反馈应用。

Method: 分析了290名老年女性的347,232个EEG时段，使用UMAP流形学习可视化状态转换；在此基础上通过10折交叉验证对六种分类器进行基准测试，以平衡准确率评估最优神经反馈引擎。

Result: 朴素贝叶斯分类器达到最高平均平衡准确率（87.17%±0.24%），显著优于全连接深度神经网络（81.58%）和随机森林（80.97%）；线性模型表现较差（LDA: 57.21%; SVM: 51.01%），表明DFA临界性特征位于一个独特的非线性流形上。

Conclusion: 基于EEG临界性的概率解码为pBCI提供了高精度的感知机制。该稳健的分类流程支持状态依赖神经反馈（如靶向听觉刺激）的开发，以增强认知恢复。

Abstract: Automated sleep staging is a fundamental application of passive Brain-Computer Interfaces (pBCI), decoding spontaneous neural states to enable closed-loop interventions independent of user intent. This study evaluates criticality features derived from Detrended Fluctuation Analysis (DFA) for the specific identification of deep sleep (N3).
  We analyzed $347,232$ EEG epochs from $290$ older women using UMAP manifold learning to visualize state transitions. Subsequently, six classifiers were benchmarked via 10-fold cross-validation, using balanced accuracy to determine the optimal "state-sensing" engine for neurofeedback.Naive Bayes achieved the highest mean balanced accuracy ($87.17\% \pm 0.24\%$), significantly outperforming a fully connected deep neural network (FNN: $81.58\%$) and Random Forest ($80.97\%$). Linear models (LDA: $57.21\%$; SVM: $51.01\%$) performed poorly, indicating that DFA-derived criticality features reside on a distinct, non-linear manifold.
  Probabilistic decoding of EEG criticality provides a high-accuracy sensing mechanism for pBCIs. This robust classification pipeline supports the development of state-dependent neurofeedback, such as targeted auditory stimulation, to enhance cognitive recovery.

</details>


### [127] [Including the Cost of Irreducible Uncertainty in the Policy Compression Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13132)
*Álvaro Garrido-Pérez,Pieter Simoens,Amrapali Pednekar,Yara Khaluf*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文在政策压缩框架中引入条件熵作为认知成本的组成部分，提出了一个扩展模型，使得最优策略的精度可以更独立于奖励敏感性变化，从而更好地解释人类决策偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准政策压缩框架将状态与动作之间的条件熵（即给定状态下选择哪个动作的不可约不确定性）视为无成本项，但实验证据表明条件熵会调节人类反应时间，因此现有框架对认知成本的估计可能不完整。

Method: 将认知成本定义为政策复杂度（状态与动作的互信息）与加权条件熵之和，引入新参数η控制条件熵的权重，推导出最优策略的形式。

Result: 扩展后的最优策略保持标准指数形式，但随着η增大策略变得更尖锐（更确定），使得政策精度可以更独立于奖励敏感性而变化。

Conclusion: 标准政策压缩框架可能低估了动作选择的认知成本；扩展模型能更好地解释人类决策偏差，但增加了模型拟合人类数据的复杂性，需要未来工作进一步解决。

Abstract: AI decision-support systems can benefit from anticipating biases in human decision-making. Many such biases may arise from human cognitive limitations. The policy compression framework models decision-making as a trade-off between reward maximization and the cognitive cost of encoding state-dependent action policies, formalized as the mutual information between states and actions (policy complexity). We argue that this account is incomplete because it treats conditional entropy--the irreducible uncertainty about which action should be selected given a state--as costless, even though empirical evidence suggests that it modulates reaction times. We therefore extend the framework by defining cognitive cost as the sum of policy complexity and a weighted conditional-entropy term, governed by a new parameter, $η$. The resulting optimal policy retains the standard exponential form but becomes sharper as $η$ increases, allowing policy precision to vary more independently of reward sensitivity. This modification implies that the standard policy compression framework may underestimate the cognitive cost of action selection, and it has the potential to better account for biases in human decision-making. At the same time, it introduces additional complexity for fitting the model to human data, which future work will need to address.

</details>


### [128] [Robust State-space Reconstruction of Brain Dynamics via Bootstrap Monte Carlo SSA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.00011)
*Sir-Lord Wiafe,Carter Hinsley,Vince D. Calhoun*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 提出BMC-SSA方法，结合蒙特卡洛SSA与bootstrap稳定性，从短时、高噪声、粗采样的时间序列中提取可靠的振荡模式，提升状态空间嵌入的稳定性与确定性，并在fMRI数据中验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有延迟坐标嵌入理论要求长时、无噪声记录，但真实场景（如神经影像fMRI）中数据常为短时、高噪声、粗采样，且包含自相关背景结构，掩盖振荡成分并破坏嵌入稳定性，亟需一种能鲁棒处理有限噪声信号的方法。

Method: 将蒙特卡洛奇异谱分析（MC-SSA）与bootstrap重采样稳定性结合，通过统计检验保留具有统计支持且在不同重采样数据中可重复的振荡模式，从而重构强调可靠振荡结构的信号，增强确定性并稳定后续嵌入。

Result: BMC-SSA提升了功能度量的可靠性，并揭示了fMRI状态空间动力学中的差异，证明其对噪声、有限信号的鲁棒嵌入能力。

Conclusion: BMC-SSA为短时、高噪声、粗采样时间序列的鲁棒状态空间嵌入提供了一个通用框架，尤其适用于fMRI等复杂神经影像数据。

Abstract: Reconstructing latent state-space geometry from time series provides a powerful route to studying nonlinear dynamics across complex systems. Delay-coordinate embedding provides the theoretical basis but assumes long, noise-free recordings, which many domains violate. In many real-world domains, recordings are short, noisy, and coarsely sampled; in neuroimaging, for example, fMRI additionally contains autocorrelated background structure that can obscure oscillatory components and destabilize embeddings. We propose bootstrap Monte Carlo singular spectrum analysis (BMC-SSA), which combines Monte Carlo SSA with bootstrap stability to retain oscillatory modes that are statistically supported and reproducible across resampled data. This produces reconstructions that emphasize reliable oscillatory structure, enhancing determinism and stabilizing subsequent embeddings. Our results show that BMC-SSA improves the reliability of functional measures and uncovers differences in state-space dynamics in fMRI, offering a general framework for robust embedding of noisy, finite signals.

</details>


### [129] [Cognitive Field Theory of Learning, Inference, and Emergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.10221)
*Byung Gyu Chae*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种认知场论（Cognitive Field Theory），将认知描述为在自适应黎曼认知流形上的集体非平衡现象。通过引入记忆修饰的认知场方程和非局部记忆核，推导出时间尺度态密度（TDOS）作为认知的基本动态描述符。弱阻尼集体模式的累积驱动系统进入近临界区域，实现长时上下文持久性和集体认知一致性，从而统一描述学习、记忆、推理、自我和涌现认知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物和人工系统中的学习、推理、记忆和涌现现象通常使用不同的理论框架分别描述，缺乏统一的宏观理论来揭示这些认知过程背后的共同物理机制。

Method: 从随机认知场方程出发，在自适应黎曼认知流形上推导出包含非局部记忆核和延迟自能反馈的记忆修饰认知场方程，通过时间尺度态密度（TDOS）表征集体弛豫模式的分布。

Result: TDOS决定了涌现的记忆核、集体响应和认知场的红外时间组织；弱阻尼集体模式的累积抑制了认知遗忘差距，增强了集体磁化率，驱动系统进入受保护的近临界区域。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一个统一的动力学描述，将学习、记忆、推理、自我和涌现认知归结为记忆修饰认知场的集体组织现象。

Abstract: Learning, inference, memory, and emergence in biological and artificial systems are often described using disparate theoretical frameworks. Here we develop a cognitive field theory in which cognition is described as a collective nonequilibrium phenomenon governed by the geometry and relaxation spectrum of a learned cognitive manifold. Starting from a stochastic cognitive-field equation on an adaptive Riemannian cognitive manifold, we derive a memory-dressed cognitive field equation incorporating nonlocal memory kernels and retarded self-energy feedback. We show that the local stability structure of learned cognitive geometry generates a spectrum of collective relaxation modes whose distribution is characterized by a time-scale density of states (TDOS). The TDOS provides a fundamental dynamical descriptor of cognition and determines the emergent memory kernel, collective response, and infrared temporal organization of the cognitive field. The accumulation of weakly damped collective modes suppresses the cognitive forgetting gap, enhances collective susceptibility, and drives the system toward a protected near-critical regime characterized by long-time contextual persistence and collective cognitive coherence. The resulting framework provides a unified dynamical description of learning, memory, inference, selfhood, and emergent cognition in terms of the collective organization of a memory-dressed cognitive field.

</details>


### [130] [Fusion Learning from Dynamic Functional Connectivity: Combining the Amplitude and Phase of fMRI Signals to Identify Brain Disorders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24603)
*Jinlong Hu,Jiatong Huang,Zijian Cai*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多尺度融合学习框架（MSFL），结合滑动窗口相关（SWC）的幅度信息和相位同步（PS）的相位信息，构建动态功能连接特征，用于脑部疾病检测。在ABIDE I（自闭症）和REST-meta-MDD（抑郁症）数据集上，MSFL显著优于现有模型，SHAP分析表明两种特征均对检测有贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的动态功能连接（dFC）研究多采用滑动窗口相关（SWC）方法，仅利用fMRI信号的幅度信息，忽略了相位信息。作者认为同时整合幅度和相位信息可以更全面地刻画脑功能连接的动态特性，从而提升脑部疾病的检测性能。

Method: 提出MSFL（多尺度融合学习框架），融合两种互补的dFC特征：SWC（捕获幅度相关性）和PS（相位同步，测量相位一致性）。采用多尺度融合策略对两种特征进行整合学习，并在两个公开数据集上进行分类验证。

Result: 在ABIDE I（自闭症谱系障碍）和REST-meta-MDD（重度抑郁症）数据集上，MSFL的分类性能显著优于现有对比模型。SHAP可解释性分析表明，SWC和PS两种dFC特征均对脑疾病检测有重要贡献。

Conclusion: 融合幅度信息（SWC）和相位信息（PS）的dFC特征能够更有效地检测脑部疾病，MSFL框架为动态功能连接分析提供了一种新的视角和方法。

Abstract: Dynamic functional connectivity (dFC) derived from resting-state functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) has been extensively utilized in brain science research. The sliding window correlation (SWC) method is a widely used approach for constructing dFC by computing correlation coefficients between amplitude time series of signals from pairs of brain regions. In this study, we propose an integrated approach that incorporates both amplitude and phase information of fMRI signals to improve the detection of brain disorders. Specifically, we introduce a multi-scale fusion learning framework, namely MSFL, which leverages two complementary dFC features derived from SWC and phase synchronization (PS). Here, SWC captures amplitude correlations, while PS measures phase coherence within dFC. We evaluated the efficacy of MSFL in classifying autism spectrum disorder and major depressive disorder using two publicly available datasets: ABIDE I and REST-meta-MDD, respectively. The results indicate that MSFL significantly outperforms existing comparative models. Moreover, we performed model explanation analysis using the SHAP framework, which showed that both types of dFC features from SWC and PS contribute to detecting brain disorders.

</details>


### [131] [Contextual Role Modulates Object Representational Geometry in the Human Brain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23111)
*Julien Dirani,Shankar Chawla,Leila Wehbe,Bradford Z. Mahon*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本研究使用fMRI让被试观看自然电影，发现同一物体在作为被动场景元素与作为目标导向行为对象时，大脑表征发生动态重映射：目标物体激活 parietal 动作网络，按动作可供性组织；被动物体激活枕颞视觉网络，按语义维度组织。视觉表征结构保持不变，灵活性与不变性在同一表征系统的不同层级运作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大脑如何在自然场景中，根据同一物体所处的不同上下文角色（被动场景元素 vs. 目标导向行为的对象）动态重映射其神经表征，揭示物体表征的灵活性与不变性如何共存。

Method: 使用fMRI在自然电影观看范式下，比较同一物体分别作为被动场景元素与目标导向行为对象时的脑活动模式，分析不同脑区内的表征几何结构（representational geometry）。

Result: 目标物体激活顶叶动作网络（缘上回、中央后回），按动作可供性和手姿势可供性维度组织；被动物体激活枕颞视觉网络，按语义维度组织。视觉表征结构在不同上下文间保持恒定。上下文选择性网络外部的表征内容保持不变。

Conclusion: 大脑对物体的神经表征会根据时刻变化的上下文角色进行动态重映射，灵活性与不变性在同一表征系统的不同层级上同时运作。

Abstract: The human brain represents objects in a way that is both invariant across instances and flexible enough to support different contexts and tasks. Yet it remains unknown how object representations are dynamically remapped as the same object shifts across contextual roles. Using fMRI during naturalistic movie viewing we investigated how the same objects are represented when they are passive scene elements versus targets of goal-directed actions. Action targets engaged a parietal action network centered in the supramarginal and postcentral gyri, while passive objects recruited a distributed occipito-temporal network involved in visual object recognition. Within context-selective networks, representational geometry showed a double dissociation: target objects were organized by action affordance and hand posture affordance dimensions, while passive objects aligned with semantic dimensions. Visual representational structure was invariant to context. Outside these networks, representational content retained invariance, indicating that flexibility and invariance operate at different levels of the same representational system. These findings demonstrate neural remapping of object representations depending on moment-to-moment changes in contextual roles during a naturalistic scene.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [132] [Learning to Assist: Collaborative VLAs for Implicit Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12475)
*Leo Xu,Letian Li,Alex Cuellar,Michael Hagenow*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于端到端模仿学习的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在人机协作（HRC）中的应用，发现了动作分块策略在隐式协作中的"演示动作泄漏"问题（即动作块跨越潜在任务转换导致过早的辅助行为），并提出了一种推理时引导（steering）方法来缓解该问题，通过16人用户研究验证了该方法在长周期协作任务中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人机协作系统多依赖手工设计的流水线（hand-engineered pipelines），限制了其对新任务的扩展性和灵活性。作者希望探索端到端训练的VLA模型能否支持协作操作任务，并识别影响其实际性能的关键因素。

Method: 评估了两种最先进的VLA模型，识别了隐式人机协作中动作分块策略的失败模式——演示动作泄漏（action chunks跨越潜在任务边界导致过早辅助行为）。提出了一种推理时引导（inference-time steering）方法来减轻错误的辅助动作，并通过16人参与的长周期协作装配任务用户研究进行验证。

Result: 发现动作泄漏问题在更长的执行时间范围内加剧，并在实际协作VLA系统中出现（如机器人未等人类准备好就试图传递工具）。提出的引导方法能够缓解过早辅助行为，同时保持策略性能。在用户研究中，引导方法使系统能够实现更长的执行时间范围，同时减少过早辅助，相比短时间范围策略实现了更快的协作和更少的失败。

Conclusion: 端到端训练的VLA模型可以支持协作操作，但动作分块策略存在演示动作泄漏的问题。推理时引导是一种有效的缓解手段，能够在不影响策略性能的前提下延长执行时间范围，提升人机协作的效率与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Human-robot collaboration (HRC) combines the complementary strengths of humans and robots to improve task efficiency. However, many existing collaborative systems rely on hand-engineered pipelines, limiting their scalability and flexibility for new tasks. In this work, we show that models trained end-to-end with imitation learning, specifically vision-language-action (VLA) models, can support collaborative manipulation, and characterize the key factors affecting their real-world performance. We evaluate two state-of-the-art models and identify a failure mode of action-chunking policies in implicit HRC, where demonstration action leakage (i.e., action chunks crossing latent task transitions) can cause premature assistive behavior. We find that this issue increases with longer execution horizons and occurs in real-world collaborative VLA systems, such as when a robot attempts to hand over a tool before the person is ready. We propose an inference-time steering method to mitigate these erroneous assistive actions while preserving policy performance. Finally, through a 16-participant user study on a long-horizon collaborative assembly task, we show that steering enables a longer execution horizon while mitigating premature assistance, leading to faster collaboration and fewer failures compared to a shorter-horizon policy.

</details>


### [133] [Action-Effect Memory Pretraining for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12499)
*Yijing Zhou,Qiwei Liang,Sitong Zhuang,Jiaxi Li,Xianpeng Wang,Boyang Cai,Yunyang Mo,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AEM是一个面向机器人操作的行动-效应记忆预训练框架，通过交错视觉与动作特征并进行掩码建模，学习紧凑的时间序列表征，在仿真和真实环境中均优于基线方法，且推理效率更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人表征预训练方法主要关注单帧视觉编码，忽视了操作任务中观测时序依赖性和部分可观测性问题，导致当前观测单独不足以支撑准确决策。

Method: 提出AEM框架：将视觉与动作特征交错排列，应用掩码建模恢复不完整历史序列中的缺失内容，学习动作条件下的状态演化；使用Mamba编码后的最终视觉token作为紧凑历史表征（单向量时间瓶颈），作为全局上下文用于解码和下游控制。

Result: 在Diffusion Policy和Flow Policy上集成AEM后，仿真与真实场景中操作性能一致提升，在整洁场景、杂乱随机场景及非马尔可夫任务中均优于基线；消融实验表明历史感知预训练优于单帧预训练和直接帧堆叠，同时降低推理延迟和计算成本。

Conclusion: AEM通过行动-效应记忆预训练有效捕获了操作任务中的时间依赖性，紧凑的单向量瓶颈设计兼顾了表征丰富性与推理效率，为机器人操作中的时序表征学习提供了新范式。

Abstract: We present AEM, an Action-Effect Memory pretraining framework for robot manipulation that learns compact temporal representations from vision-action history. Unlike prior robot representation pretraining methods that mainly focus on single-frame visual encoding, AEM targets the temporal nature of manipulation, where the current observation alone is often insufficient under partial observability. AEM models manipulation as an action-driven interaction process by interleaving visual and action features and applying masked modeling to recover missing content from incomplete histories, thereby learning action-conditioned state evolution. The Mamba-encoded output of the final vision token is used as a compact history representation, serving as the global context for decoding and downstream control. This design preserves a single-vector temporal bottleneck while keeping inference efficient. We evaluate AEM with Diffusion Policy and Flow Policy. AEM consistently improves manipulation performance in both simulation and real-world settings, outperforming baselines across clean scenes, cluttered and random scenes, and non-Markovian tasks. Ablation studies further show that history-aware pretraining surpasses single-frame pretraining and direct frame stacking, while reducing inference latency and computational cost.

</details>


### [134] [Foresight: Iterative Reasoning About Clues that Matter for Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12550)
*Arthur Zhang,Carl Qi,Donne Su,Xiangyun Meng,Amy Zhang,Joydeep Biswas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Foresight是一个测试时框架，利用微调的视觉语言模型(VLM)交替提出图像空间运动计划并进行批评，通过人类反馈的奖励模型进行强化学习后训练，在开放世界无地图导航中比基线方法任务成功率提升37%，干预次数减少52%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放世界无地图导航方法受限于依赖已知导航因子和封闭类别集，或在运动规划前识别线索而遗漏依赖计划的线索。需要一种能自适应发现指令相关线索并指导运动规划的方法。

Method: 采用测试时框架Foresight，使用微调VLM在图像空间中交替进行运动规划提议和基于语言目标及视觉上下文的批评；通过人类反馈学习奖励模型，利用强化学习在规划-批评循环中对VLM进行后训练。

Result: 在离线评估和6个真实世界环境中，任务平均成功率提升37%，每次任务干预次数减少52%，并在Jetson AGX Orin上实时运行。

Conclusion: 预训练VLM经过适当的测试时适应和后训练，能有效发现开放世界导航中的指令相关线索并指导运动规划，为机器人运动优化的测试时推理提供了新思路。

Abstract: Open-world mapless navigation from sparse language instructions requires resolving underspecified goals and inferring which environmental cues are relevant for reaching the goal. For instance, reaching an out-of-view destination may require interpreting ramps, signs, or detours that reveal where to go or which route to take. Prior works are limited by their reliance on known navigation factors and closed-set factor categories, or identify cues before motion planning and miss plan-dependent cues. We argue that pretrained Vision-Language Models (VLMs) can discover novel instruction-relevant cues, but require adaptation to focus on which cues matter and how they should influence motion planning. We realize these ideas in Foresight, a test-time framework in which a finetuned VLM alternates between proposing image-space motion plans and critiquing them using the language goal and visual context. Subsequent plans are conditioned on prior critiques, enabling iterative motion refinement before execution. To align plan critiques and refinements with open-set behavior preferences, we learn a reward model from human feedback and use it to post-train the VLM with reinforcement learning in the plan-critique loop. In offline evaluations and 6 real-world environments, Foresight improves average task success by 37% and reduces interventions per mission by 52% relative to state-of-the-art test-time reasoning and foundation-model baselines, while running in real-time on a Jetson AGX Orin. We will release code, data, and training details to support future work on test-time reasoning for robot motion refinement. Additional videos at: https://amrl.cs.utexas.edu/foresight

</details>


### [135] [G-MAPP: GPU-accelerated Multi-Agent Planning and Perception for Reactive Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12579)
*Tanmay Bishnoi,Riddhiman Laha,Tobias Löw,Jose Alex Chandy,Luis F. C. Figueredo,Sami Haddadin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于GPU加速的机器人运动生成框架，通过在GPU上加速世界建模和矢量场规划，实现实时准全局轨迹规划，在动态杂乱环境中比CPU版本提速5倍，且成功避开碰撞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动生成方法要么只能处理静态场景的全局轨迹，要么对环境做保守假设，主要瓶颈在于高保真环境下的规划计算效率以及感知与规划模块的时间集成问题。

Method: 利用GPU加速世界建模和基于矢量场的规划，实现并行状态探索，从而进行准全局轨迹规划；同时通过GPU加速实现感知-行动环的紧密耦合，支持实时运行。

Result: GPU版本相比CPU版本实现了最高5倍的加速；在7自由度Franka Emika机器人上的真实实验中，该方法在简单和具有挑战性的物理场景中均成功避免了碰撞。

Conclusion: 通过GPU加速世界建模和规划，可以在不牺牲运行时性能和环境表征质量的前提下，实现动态杂乱环境中的实时反应式运动生成，验证了该框架在实际机器人上的有效性。

Abstract: Reactive motion generation in unstructured environments remains an open challenge in robotics. Due to the computational complexity of collision-free motion generation, existing methods either generate global trajectories for static scenarios, or employ models that make conservative assumptions about the environment. This paper identifies the primary bottleneck as the runtime performance demand of planning on high-fidelity environments, and the temporal integration between the perception and planning modules. Therefore, we propose a framework that does not compromise on runtime performance and world representations for perception and planning by accelerating world modeling and vector-field based planning using the GPU. This allows us to achieve faster parallel state exploration for quasi-global trajectory planning, and tighter coupling of the perception-action loop in real-time for dynamic cluttered environments with off-the-shelf depth sensors. We quantitatively evaluate the computation-time and success rate differences for the CPU and GPU versions of our planner, and perform qualitative evaluations of our coupled framework using real-world experiments on a 7-DoF Franka Emika robot. Experimental results demonstrate that our GPU-based framework achieves up to a 5x speedup over the CPU version and successfully avoids collisions across both trivial and challenging physical world scenarios.

</details>


### [136] [From Imitation to Alignment: Human-Preference Flow Policies for Long-Horizon Sidewalk Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12603)
*Honglin He,Zhizheng Liu,Yukai Ma,Bolei Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FlowPilot提出了一种基于锚定流匹配和人类偏好学习的无地图导航策略，仅使用单目RGB相机即可实现长时域人行道导航，在仿真和真实实验中显著提升了成功率、路径完成度和社会合规性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时域人行道导航对微机动应用至关重要，但现有模仿学习方法存在复合误差、缺乏社会合规性和反事实推理能力，且轻量级感知（仅单目RGB相机）下难以处理人行道的复杂多变环境。

Method: 首先提出锚定流匹配（anchored flow matching）作为动作表示，在大规模机器人车队数据上进行策略预训练，捕捉人行道导航行为的多模态分布；然后设计人机环内的偏好学习方案，利用少量人类干预数据对齐策略，增强反事实推理和社会合规性。

Result: 仿真中成功率达42%、路径完成度66%；FlowPilot-HP在真实世界中干预率降低40.0%、导航干预率降低52.1%，鲁棒性和社会合规性显著提升。

Conclusion: FlowPilot证明了仅凭单目RGB相机结合锚定流匹配预训练和人类偏好微调，即可实现鲁棒、高效且社会合规的长时域人行道自主导航。

Abstract: Autonomous long-horizon sidewalk navigation is essential for micro-mobility applications such as robotic food delivery and assistive electronic wheelchairs. Unlike autonomous driving on the road, long-horizon sidewalk navigation requires precise maneuvering through unpredictable sidewalk terrains and pedestrians, with a lightweight perception stack as minimal as a single monocular RGB camera. While imitation learning (IL) from demonstrations offers a practical solution, the resulting autopilot policy often suffers from compounding errors, a lack of social compliance on sidewalks, and deficiencies in counterfactual reasoning to handle complex situations. To address these challenges, we introduce FlowPilot, a mapless navigation policy that achieves robust and efficient long-horizon navigation performance using only a monocular RGB camera. We first propose to use anchored flow matching as an action representation for policy pre-training on large-scale robot fleet data and to capture the diverse, complex, multimodal distribution of sidewalk navigation behaviors. To bridge the gap between imitation and alignment, we further design a human-in-the-loop preference learning scheme to tune the policy on a small amount of human intervention data. It strengthens the model's counterfactual reasoning and social compliance on sidewalks. We evaluate FlowPilot through extensive simulation and real-world experiments in diverse sidewalk environments. FlowPilot achieves 42% success rate and 66% route completion in simulation, while FlowPilot-HP further improves real-world robustness and social compliance, reducing IR by 40.0% and NIR by 52.1% relative to the base model.

</details>


### [137] [EgoEngine: From Egocentric Human Videos to High-Fidelity Dexterous Robot Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12604)
*Yangcen Liu,Shuo Cheng,Xinchen Yin,Woo Chul Shin,Alfred Cueva,Yiran Yang,Zhenyang Chen,Chuye Zhang,Danfei Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EgoEngine是一个将第一人称人类操作视频转化为高保真机器人数据的框架，能够同时生成机器人的观测视频和可执行的动作轨迹，首次实现了从第一人称人类视频中进行零样本灵巧操作策略学习，无需真实机器人演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作依赖于大规模机器人演示数据，但收集此类数据成本高昂。第一人称人类视频是丰富的操作行为数据源，但存在视觉差异（人类与机器人的观察差异）和动作差异（人类运动与机器人可执行动作之间的差距）两大鸿沟，亟需一种可扩展的方法来弥合这些差距。

Method: EgoEngine以第一人称RGB视频为输入，输出两部分结果：(i) 高保真机器人观测视频——将人类替换为机器人，同时保留场景上下文和时间对齐；(ii) 任务对齐且可执行的机器人动作轨迹——在可行性约束下生成。

Result: 仿真和真实机器人实验表明，EgoEngine能够将人类视频可扩展地转化为机器人数据，并首次实现了从第一人称人类视频进行零样本视觉运动灵巧操作策略学习，无需任何真实机器人演示数据。

Conclusion: EgoEngine有效弥合了人类与机器人之间的视觉鸿沟和动作鸿沟，为利用大规模第一人称人类视频进行机器人灵巧操作学习提供了可扩展的解决方案，显著降低了机器人数据获取的成本。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation is limited by the cost of collecting large-scale robot demonstrations. Egocentric human videos offer a scalable source of diverse manipulation behaviors, but directly using them for robot learning requires bridging two gaps: the visual gap between human and robot observations, and the action gap between human motion and robot-executable action. We propose EgoEngine, a scalable framework for transforming egocentric human manipulation videos into high-fidelity robot data. Given an egocentric RGB video, EgoEngine produces: (i) a high-fidelity robot observation video replacing human with robot while preserving scene context and temporal alignment, and (ii) a task-aligned, executable robot action trajectory under feasibility constraints. Experiments in simulation and on real robots show that EgoEngine enables scalable conversion of human videos into robot data and, to our knowledge, demonstrates the first zero-shot visuomotor dexterous policy learning from egocentric human videos without real-robot demonstrations. Project website: https://egoengine.github.io.

</details>


### [138] [DARRMS -- An Efficient Algorithm for Dynamic Attention Radius in Resource-Constrained Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12614)
*Benjamin Alcorn,Eman Hammad*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通过限制智能体观察半径（注意力半径）来降低计算资源需求的轻量级多智能体决策算法，在资源受限环境下实现协调与可扩展性的优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多智能体决策框架假设完全可观测和无限计算能力，与真实世界资源受限的条件不符，亟需高效轻量级算法。

Method: 引入注意力半径概念，智能体主动忽略环境中对行动规划不必要的部分，同时优化注意力半径与决策策略。

Result: 理论与实验验证表明，自适应观测方法在不显著牺牲其他性能指标的前提下，有效降低了计算资源需求，提升了系统性能与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 自适应注意力半径机制能够在资源受限的多智能体系统中保持稳健的决策策略，并提升协调性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems are integral tools for various domains such as robotics, cybersecurity, and autonomous vehicle planning. These types of systems often have constraints on the computational resources, leading to a need for efficient lightweight algorithms. Traditional decision making frameworks often assume ideal conditions, such as full observability and unlimited computational capacity, which do not align with real-world challenges. In this paper, we introduce a new algorithm that allows for reduced demand on computational resources without a large cost of other performance metrics. Agents will limit their observability to some attention radius, which intentionally allows them to ignore parts of the environment that might be unnecessary for action planning. By optimizing both the attention radius and decision-making, our approach enhances coordination and scalability in uncertain environments. Through both theoretical analysis and empirical validation, we demonstrate the effectiveness of adaptive observation in improving system performance and maintaining robust decision-making strategies in resource-constrained systems.

</details>


### [139] [EWAM: An Enhanced World Action Model for Closed-Loop Online Adaptation in Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12690)
*Xin Zhou,Cong Miao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出增强世界行动模型(EWAM)，在冻结的Cosmos3骨干网络上通过四个轻量神经层实现零样本在线自适应，无需额外微调或演示数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 减少新任务布局下所需的额外部署数据量，实现零样本条件下的高效在线自适应。

Method: 在冻结的Cosmos3骨干网络上插入四个轻量神经层：神经经验记忆层（提供任务上下文）、神经异常检测层（实时监控状态偏差）、神经策略路由层（动态选择执行/重规划/回滚）、神经动作修正层（优化动作块）。记忆、检测、修正模块以可微方式集成前向路径，仅路由决策为离散监督。

Result: 零样本协议下评估，无需额外任务演示集或骨干网络微调，性能提升完全来自推理时协同推理机制。

Conclusion: 通过闭环在线自适应架构和轻量层插入，在完全冻结的预训练模型上实现零样本任务布局适应，显著降低部署数据需求。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose the Enhanced World Action Model (EWAM), a closed-loop online adaptation architecture built upon a pretrained and fully frozen Cosmos3 backbone network. Evaluated entirely under a zero-shot task protocol, EWAM is centrally focused on reducing the amount of additional deployment data required to adapt to new task layouts. Notably, no extra task-specific demonstration sets were introduced in any of the evaluations, and no fine-tuning was performed on the backbone network. Its performance gains stem entirely from an inference-time co-reasoning mechanism composed of four inserted lightweight neural layers: the Neural Experience Memory Layer located in the intermediate layers of the Diffusion Transformer (DiT) provides task-relevant execution context; the Neural Anomaly Detection Layer after the state prediction head monitors the divergence between predicted and actual states in real time; the Neural Policy Routing Layer dynamically selects direct execution, conservative replanning, or rollback recovery based on the anomaly severity; and the Neural Action Correction Layer refines the generated action chunks using execution diagnostics. Unlike naive feature fusion, the memory, anomaly detection, and correction modules are deeply integrated into the Cosmos3 forward path in a differentiable manner, with only the final routing decision being a discrete supervised one.

</details>


### [140] [EquiDexFlow: Contact-Grounded SE(3)-Equivariant Dexterous Grasp Generative Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12728)
*Clinton Enwerem,John S. Baras,Calin Belta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EquiDexFlow是一个SE(3)等变流匹配模型，能够联合预测抓取姿态、手指接触点、法向量和接触力，通过将接触点投影到物体表面、将力约束在库仑摩擦锥内，实现无损失的摩擦和接触合规性。在Allegro Hand上训练后零摩擦违规，在LEAP Hand上通过逆运动学重定位，物理机器人实验成功完成所有6个测试物体的开环抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数灵巧抓取生成器将接触力作为下游验证步骤，导致运动学可行的抓取姿态仍可能违反物理稳定抓取的条件。因此需要一种能够联合预测运动学和力学量的方法。

Method: 提出SE(3)-等变流匹配模型EquiDexFlow，联合预测手腕姿态、关节角度、指尖接触点、表面法向量和接触力。通过将接触点投影到物体表面、将力约束在摩擦锥内实现硬约束，并证明端到端SE(3)等变性。训练使用8100个力闭合抓取（81个物体，16-DoF Allegro Hand），再通过逐指逆运动学重定位到LEAP Hand。

Result: 在200次旋转测试中，手腕残差低于0.04°，关节偏差为零。零摩擦违规，综合得分最优，扳手残差最低。重定位后每个关节至少处于执行器包络的5%以内。物理机器人上，重定位的抓取在6个测试物体上全部成功完成开环抓取，不对称物体在标准姿态和120°旋转下均成功。

Conclusion: 通过硬约束和等变设计，EquiDexFlow能够生成同时满足运动学和力学条件的灵巧抓取，并成功从仿真迁移到物理机器人上。

Abstract: Most learned dexterous grasp generators relegate contact forces to a downstream verification step, so a kinematically-plausible pose can still violate the conditions for a stable physical grasp. We address this with EquiDexFlow, an SE(3)-equivariant flow-matching model that jointly predicts wrist pose, joint angles, fingertip contacts, surface normals, and contact forces from an object point cloud. Our architecture projects contacts onto the object surface and forces into the Coulomb friction cone by construction, so placement and friction compliance hold without loss penalties. We prove end-to-end SE(3) equivariance and verify it empirically over 200 rotations, with wrist residuals below $0.04^\circ$ and exactly zero joint deviation. Trained on 8,100 force-closure grasps across 81 objects for the 16-DoF Allegro Hand, our model achieves zero friction violations, the best composite score, and the lowest wrench residual among all ablation variants. We retarget decoded fingertip contacts to a 16-DoF LEAP Hand via per-finger inverse kinematics, and our hardware-feasible refinement places every joint at least 5% inside its actuator envelope while preserving wrench balance. On the physical robot, retargeted EquiDexFlow-decoded grasps complete open-loop pick-and-hold trials on all six test objects, with every asymmetric object succeeding at both the canonical pose and a $120^\circ$ co-rotation. Videos, code, and checkpoints are available at https://equidexflow.github.io.

</details>


### [141] [Sparse2Act: Learning Action-Aligned Sparse 3D Representations for Cross-Domain Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12759)
*Yu Guo,Chang Yu,Siyu Ma,Yunuo Chen,Yin Yang,Ying Nian Wu,Chenfanfu Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Sparse2Act框架，通过将任务空间中的末端执行器动作作为几何监督信号，对稀疏点云编码器进行预训练。该方法在LIBERO-10基准上实现86.9%的成功率，支持跨域迁移，并在真实机器人实验中达到72.5%的平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏3D编码器通常通过下游任务目标进行学习，导致表示与特定数据分布、策略架构和动作参数化紧密绑定，缺乏可迁移的通用3D表示。

Method: 提出观测-动作对齐框架Sparse2Act，利用任务空间末端执行器动作作为几何监督信号，对掩蔽的稀疏3D token进行训练，使其围绕观测对应的工作空间运动组织场景特征。预训练后仅重用编码器初始化，下游策略保留自身架构和动作空间。

Result: LIBERO-10基准上500步微调后达86.9%平均成功率；LIBERO到Meta-World跨域迁移在Meta-World-5上达73.4%；真实机器人实验模拟预训练+有限真实数据微调在四个任务上达72.5%平均成功率。

Conclusion: 机器人动作可以提供紧凑的几何监督信号，用于学习可复用的稀疏3D表示，实现有效的模拟到现实迁移。

Abstract: Explicit 3D representations are attractive for manipulation because they expose object shape, workspace geometry, and robot-object relations in metric coordinates. However, sparse 3D encoders are often learned through downstream task objectives, tying the representation to a particular data distribution, policy architecture, and action parameterization. We introduce Sparse2Act, an observation-action alignment framework for pretraining sparse point-cloud encoders. The key idea is to use task-space end-effector actions as geometric supervision: masked sparse 3D tokens are trained to organize scene features around the workspace motion paired with the observation. After pretraining, only the encoder initialization is reused by downstream policies, allowing them to retain their own architectures and action spaces, including joint-space commands. On the LIBERO-10 benchmark, our method achieves 86.9% average success after 500 fine-tuning steps. The same pretrained encoder supports LIBERO-to-Meta-World cross-domain transfer, achieving 73.4% average success on the Meta-World-5 benchmark. Ablations on the objective and decoder capacity show that the gains come from the masked action-alignment signal and remain useful across downstream action decoders. In real-world experiments, simulation pretraining followed by limited real-data fine-tuning achieves an average success rate of 72.5% across four tasks, demonstrating effective sim-to-real transfer. These results suggest that robot actions can provide compact geometric supervision for reusable sparse 3D representations.

</details>


### [142] [Stubborn: A Streamlined and Unified Reinforcement Learning Framework for Robust Motion Tracking and Fall Recovery for Humanoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12814)
