*Xiao Ren,Yuhui Yang,Zongbiao Weng,Zhijie Liu,He Kong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Stubborn提出了一种统一的强化学习框架，通过非对称Actor-Critic架构、偏航对齐跟踪表示、伯努利概率终止机制和自适应采样策略，实现了人形机器人的鲁棒运动跟踪与摔倒恢复，在无需多阶段训练的情况下取得了竞争性性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将运动跟踪和摔倒恢复视为不同任务，需要多阶段训练及专门的恢复奖励或策略，且训练过程中在严重跟踪失败后立即终止回合，限制了恢复导向的探索。

Method: 采用非对称Actor-Critic架构，包含三个核心组件：1) 偏航对齐跟踪表示，减少对全局漂移和航向干扰的敏感性；2) 伯努利概率终止机制，鼓励探索不同失败模式下的摔倒恢复行为；3) 概率终止与跟踪误差驱动策略，根据跟踪性能动态重塑采样分布，提高困难运动片段和不稳定状态的训练效率。

Result: 与SOTA方法的广泛比较和消融实验表明，Stubborn取得了竞争性性能，所提出的概率终止机制和自适应采样策略对性能和鲁棒性提升有贡献。

Conclusion: Stubborn作为一个统一的强化学习框架，有效解决了人形机器人运动跟踪与摔倒恢复任务，无需多阶段训练，并通过概率终止和自适应采样机制显著提升了训练效率和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Recent reinforcement learning approaches have shown great promise in improving humanoid motion tracking performance and achieving fall recovery under disturbances. However, most existing works treat motion tracking and fall recovery as different tasks and require multi-stage training with specialized recovery rewards and/or separate recovery policies. Moreover, existing reinforcement learning-based methods often terminate training episodes immediately after severe tracking failures, limiting recovery-oriented exploration in unstable or fallen states. To address the above issues, we propose Stubborn, a streamlined and unified reinforcement learning framework to achieve robust humanoid motion tracking and fall recovery. Specifically, Stubborn uses an asymmetric Actor-Critic architecture and consists of three major components. First, a yaw-aligned tracking representation is adopted to reduce sensitivity to global drift and heading disturbances while preserving gravity-related balance information. Second, we introduce a Bernoulli-based probabilistic termination mechanism that enables the policy to encourage exploration of fall-recovery behaviors under varying failure modes. Third, we propose a probabilistic termination and tracking-error-driven strategy that dynamically reshapes the sampling distribution based on tracking performance, increasing the training efficiency for difficult motion segments and unstable states. Extensive comparisons with SOTA methods and ablation studies show that Stubborn achieved competitive performance, and the proposed probabilistic termination mechanism and adaptive sampling strategy contributed to the performance and robustness gains. For real-world demonstrations, please refer to https://aislab-sustech.github.io/Stubborn/.

</details>


### [143] [AIR-VLA+: Decoupling Movement and Manipulation via Cascaded Dual-Action Decoders with Asymmetric MoE for Aerial Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12859)
*Jianli Sun,Bin Tian,Qiyao Zhang,Zijian Liu,Yutong Wang,Zhiyong Cui,Bai Li,Yisheng Lv,Yonglin Tian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AIR-VLA+，一种面向空中操纵的流匹配动作生成架构，通过级联双动作解码器和非对称特征级MoE，解决无人机平台运动与机械臂操作之间的表征耦合问题，在AIR-VLA基准上取得48.0的总体平均分，任务完成度相比单头策略提升80.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中操纵系统中，端到端控制长期存在表征耦合问题：平台级无人机运动与末端执行器级机械臂操纵在动作尺度、动力学特性和控制目标上存在显著差异，导致异构协调控制冲突。

Method: 1) 构建级联操作解码器和运动解码器，实现无人机单向观测机械臂意图以协调工作流，同时隔离无人机运动信息反向传播对机械臂稳定性的影响；2) 针对无人机运动对高层语义依赖强的特点，设计输入特征增强模块，引入隐式视觉抓取投影器感知夹爪与物体的交互状态，并注入压缩的全局语义特征；3) 在无人机运动解码器中部署隐式MoE架构，使不同运动专家在训练中自发形成对特定任务阶段的能力偏好，通过特征流形上的密集软混合计算增强任务阶段适应性。

Result: 在标准化AIR-VLA基准上，方法以48.0的总体平均分全面超越所有基线；整体任务完成得分相比单头π₀.₅策略提升80.2%，有效缓解了复合机器人的异构协调控制冲突。

Conclusion: 所提出的级联双解码器与非对称MoE设计能够有效解耦空中操纵系统中无人机运动与机械臂操作之间的异构控制冲突，流匹配动作生成范式在空中操纵领域展现出显著优势。

Abstract: Aerial manipulation systems have long suffered from representation coupling in end-to-end control, as platform-level Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) movement and end-effector-level arm manipulation differ substantially in action scale, dynamics, and control objectives. In this paper, we propose AIR-VLA+, a flow matching action generation architecture specifically designed for aerial manipulation, featuring cascaded dual-action decoders and an asymmetric feature-level Mixture of Experts (MoE). We construct cascaded manipulation and movement decoders, allowing the UAV to unidirectionally observe the manipulator's intent during movement to achieve workflow coordination, while isolating the impact of UAV movement information backpropagation on arm manipulation stability. Addressing the characteristic that UAV movement is highly dependent on high-level semantics and responsible for task state transitions in aerial manipulation, we design an input feature enhancement module for the UAV movement decoder. This module introduces an implicit visual grasp projector to perceive the interaction state between the gripper and the object, and injects compressed global semantic features. Within the UAV movement decoder, we deploy an implicit MoE architecture, enabling different movement experts to spontaneously exhibit capacity inclinations for various task stages during training. Through dense soft blending computation on the feature manifold, the UAV movement is endowed with stronger task-stage adaptability. Experiments on the standardized AIR-VLA benchmark demonstrate that our method comprehensively surpasses all baselines with an overall average score of 48.0. The overall task completion score improves by 80.2\% compared to the single-head $π_{0.5}$ policy, effectively mitigating the heterogeneous coordinated control conflicts of composite robots.

</details>


### [144] [Learning to Adapt: Representation-Based Reinforcement Learning for Multi-Task Skill Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12890)
*Aryan Naveen,Haitong Ma,Haldun Balim,Na Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RepMT-SAC框架，通过谱MDP分解实现多任务强化学习中的高效知识共享与迁移，在四旋翼轨迹跟踪任务上相比基线提升30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法样本效率低且跨任务泛化能力差，限制了其在实际场景中的适用性。

Method: RepMT-SAC采用谱MDP分解捕获可迁移动力学，将价值函数结构化为任务无关核心加最小任务特定调整，实现知识共享与迁移。

Result: 在四旋翼轨迹跟踪任务上，RepMT-SAC在分布内任务上实现强零样本性能，在分布外任务上实现快速少样本适应，相比基线提升30%。

Conclusion: RepMT-SAC通过谱MDP分解与价值函数解耦设计，有效提升了多任务RL的样本效率和泛化能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has achieved remarkable success in learning complex control policies, yet its applicability remains limited due to sample inefficiency and poor generalization across tasks. In this work, we propose RepMT-SAC, a framework for multi-task RL that enables efficient knowledge sharing and robust transfer to new tasks. RepMT-SAC uses spectral MDP decomposition to capture transferable dynamics, structuring the value function into a task-agnostic core with a minimal task-specific adjustment. This design allows for strong zero-shot performance on in-distribution tasks and rapid few-shot adaptation to out-of-distribution tasks. We evaluate RepMT-SAC on quadcopter trajectory-following tasks across in-distribution and out-of-distribution contexts, demonstrating that it outperforms baselines by up to 30%.

</details>


### [145] [Bounding Boxes as Goals: Language-Conditioned Grasping via Neuro-Symbolic Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12910)
*Allison Andreyev,Landon Eum,Nestor Tiglao,Romel Gomez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GRASP是一个轻量级框架，利用预训练VLM将自然语言查询转化为神经符号目标状态，实现零样本开放词汇桌面操作，无需任务特定训练即可完成73.3%的真实机器人操作成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM驱动的机器人任务规划方法计算开销大或需要大量训练数据，无法高效实现机器人在家庭/工业环境中对自然语言提示的实时适应。

Method: 采用预训练VLM将自然语言查询翻译为神经符号目标状态，通过边界框检测管线实现物理世界接地，利用抽象空间概念（如"顶层架子"）进行推理，无需微调。

Result: 在90次真实机器人试验中，三个难度级别上实现了73.3%的整体成功率，且无需任务特定训练。

Conclusion: GRASP展示了将预训练VLM与符号规划相结合实现开放词汇桌面操作的有效性，为机器人在非结构化环境中理解抽象语言指令提供了可行路径。

Abstract: For robotics to be effectively integrated into household or industrial environments, machines must adapt to natural-language prompts in real time. Although Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have enabled zero-shot generalization in robot task and motion planning (TAMP), current state-of-the-art approaches often remain computationally "heavyweight" or require extensive training on thousands of demonstrations. We present GRASP (Grounded Reasoning and Symbolic Planning), a framework designed as a step toward open-vocabulary tabletop manipulation. Our approach leverages a pretrained VLM to translate natural-language queries into neuro-symbolic goal states, grounded in the physical world via a bounding-box detection pipeline. Unlike methods that rely on fixed color lists or hard-coded coordinates, GRASP enables robots to interpret abstract spatial concepts such as "top shelf" and execute tasks without additional fine-tuning. We achieve 73.3% overall success across 90 real-robot trials at three difficulty levels, requiring no task-specific training.

</details>


### [146] [An Embodied Simulation Platform, Benchmark, and Data-Efficient Augmentation Framework for Wet-Lab Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12936)
*Zhe Liu,Huanbo Jin,Zhaohui Du,Zhe Wang,He Xu,Peijia Li,Jiaming Gu,Quan Lu,Qi Wang,Bin Ji,Ting Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Pipette是一个面向湿实验室机器人学习的仿真平台、基准测试和数据增强框架，包含43个开源可编辑资产、11个任务基准以及基于仿真的数据增强流水线，能够从少量人类演示中扩展训练数据，显著提升视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 湿实验室机器人可提升生物医学实验的可重复性、通量和安全性，但其学习规模化面临三大挑战：缺乏可定制的仿真器用于安全且可重复的任务生成、缺乏开放可编辑的实验室资产、缺乏高效流水线将有限的演示转化为可用的训练数据。

Method: Pipette提供了：(1) 43个开源可编辑湿实验室资产及可扩展的资产构建流水线；(2) 基于仿真的数据增强流水线，通过重放人类演示并施加光照、相机、速度和动作扰动，再经自动任务成功检查过滤生成片段；(3) 11个任务的湿实验室具身基准测试，涵盖样品处理、培养器具操作、设备操作和精确放置；(4) 自然语言驱动的场景构建和任务注册功能。

Result: 仅用每任务30个演示，ACT达到65.5%平均成功率；仿真增强将SmolVLA从44.1%提升至74.7%，π0从40.4%提升至46.5%，验证了Pipette在数据高效VLA训练和评估中的有效性。

Conclusion: Pipette通过提供可定制的仿真环境、开源资产和数据增强流水线，有效解决了湿实验室机器人学习中的数据稀缺问题，提升了VLA模型的性能，并降低了非专家用户定义新任务的门槛。

Abstract: Wet-lab robots can improve the reproducibility, throughput, and safety of biomedical experiments, but scaling their learning requires customizable simulators for safe and reproducible task generation, open editable laboratory assets, and efficient pipelines that turn limited demonstrations into usable training data. We present Pipette, an embodied simulation platform, benchmark, and data-efficient augmentation framework for wet-lab robot learning. Pipette releases over 43 open-source and re-editable wet-lab assets, together with an extensible asset-building pipeline. A key component of Pipette is its simulation-based data augmentation pipeline, replaying human demonstrations in simulation, applies lighting, camera, speed, and action perturbations, and filters generated episodes with automatic task success checks, rapidly expanding usable training data from limited manual demonstrations. We further introduce an 11-task wet-lab embodied benchmark covering sample handling, culture-ware manipulation, device operation, and precision placement. With only 30 demonstrations per task, ACT achieves 65.5% average success rate, while simulation augmentation improves SmolVLA from 44.1% to 74.7% and π0 from 40.4% to 46.5%, validating the effectiveness of Pipette for data-efficient VLA training and evaluation. Pipette also supports natural-language-driven scene construction and task registration, lowering the barrier for non-expert users to define new wet-lab robotic tasks.

</details>


### [147] [Towards Reliable Sequential Object Picking in Clutter: The Runner-up Solution to RGMC 2025](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12954)
*Wei Yu,Xidan Zhang,Ziyi Zheng,Weijie Kong,Huixu Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对机器人在杂乱环境中稳定高效抓取这一长期挑战，提出了一套集成硬件-软件流水线，结合多功能夹爪设计、物体识别、清理和多功能抓取策略，在ICRA 2025第10届机器人抓取与操作竞赛的Pick-in-Clutter赛道中获得第二名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 杂乱环境下的稳定高效抓取是机器人操作领域的长期挑战，在工业场景中具有重要意义。现有方法虽在杂乱抓取上取得较高成功率，但在顺序物体搜索和分拣等更复杂任务上仍缺乏成熟解决方案。

Method: 设计了一套集成硬件-软件流水线，包括：(1) 多功能夹爪的硬件设计；(2) 杂乱空间中物体分布和遮挡关系的新型表示方法；(3) 结合物体识别、清理和多功能抓取的完整策略。

Result: 在实验室测试和竞赛场景中均展现出强劲性能，最终在RGMC 2025的Pick-in-Clutter赛道中获得第二名。

Conclusion: 所提出的集成流水线能够有效实现杂乱环境中物体的高效识别、搜索和顺序抓取，验证了其在复杂抓取任务中的实用性和竞争力。

Abstract: As a long-standing challenge in robotic manipulation, stable and efficient grasping in cluttered environments is of great importance in industrial settings. While recent studies have achieved relatively high success rates in grasping from clutter, there remain few mature solutions for more demanding tasks such as sequential object search and sorting. This work addresses sequential object picking in cluttered environments based on the Cluttered Environment Picking Benchmark (CEPB) and presents our solution to the Pick-in-Clutter track of the 10th Robotic Grasping and Manipulation Competition (RGMC) at ICRA 2025. The task poses several key challenges. First, it requires robust and collision-aware grasping with high success rates across a diverse set of objects, including both rigid and deformable ones. Second, it demands efficient search for target objects, which places stringent requirements on the decluttering and searching strategies of the solution. To address the above challenges, we design an integrated hardware-software pipeline that combines object recognition, decluttering, and multi-modal grasping. The main contributions include the hardware design of a multifunctional gripper and novel representations for object distribution and occlusion relationships in cluttered space. This pipeline enables efficient recognition, search, and sequential grasping of objects in clutter, demonstrating strong performance in both laboratory tests and competition scenarios, and ultimately achieving second place in the Pick-in-Clutter track of the RGMC 2025.

</details>


### [148] [SERF: Spatiotemporal Environment and Robot Feature Map for Long-Horizon Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12956)
*Sunghwan Kim,Byeonghyun Pak,Kehan Long,Yulun Tian,Nikolay Atanasov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SERF（时空环境与机器人特征图），通过将移动操作策略条件化为共享潜空间中表示的神经点云（包含环境和机器人本体），结合在线更新的刚性跟踪和前向运动学，为VLA模型提供多参考帧和多尺度的局部-全局上下文，在BEHAVIOR-1K基准上超越纯图像基线，在长时程移动操作任务中实现更直接轨迹、更强鲁棒性和失败恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程机器人移动操作需要持续推理定位、环境变化和任务进度，而这些难以仅从图像观测中推断。

Method: 构建SERF图：将环境和机器人本体表示为共享潜空间中的神经点，环境点通过物体级刚性跟踪在线更新，机器人点通过前向运动学更新；从多参考帧和多尺度提取图token作为VLA模型的状态输入，提供局部与全局上下文。

Result: 在BEHAVIOR-1K基准上，SERF VLA策略优于纯图像基线，能以更直接轨迹更快到达子目标，对场景配置变化更鲁棒，并能从物体掉落等失败中恢复。

Conclusion: 将时空特征图作为VLA模型的状态输入，能够有效提升长时程移动操作策略的推理能力和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Long-horizon robot mobile manipulation requires continual reasoning about localization, environment changes, and task progress, all of which are challenging to infer from image observations alone. In this paper, we show that conditioning a mobile manipulation policy on a spatiotemporal feature map improves reasoning over long horizons. The map represents the environment and the articulated robot body as neural points in a shared latent space and is updated online from egocentric observations and proprioceptive state. We update the environment neural points using object-level rigid tracking and the robot neural points using forward kinematics. We use our spatiotemporal environment and robot feature (SERF) map as a state input to a vision-language-action (VLA) model by extracting map tokens from multiple reference frames and spatial scales, providing the policy with both local and global context. We demonstrate SERF on BEHAVIOR-1K, a benchmark for long-horizon mobile manipulation in household environments. Experiments show that the SERF VLA policy outperforms image-only baselines, reaches subgoals faster by following more direct trajectories, improves robustness to scene-configuration shifts, and recovers from object-drop failures.

</details>


### [149] [EmbodiSteer: Steering Embodiment-Agnostic Visuomotor Policies with Joint-Space Guidance for Zero-Shot Cross-Embodiment Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12965)
*Shihefeng Wang,Kangchen Lv,Mingrui Yu,Xiang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出EmbodiSteer，一种无需训练的框架，通过将笛卡尔空间的策略采样提升到关节空间，并引入全身碰撞感知引导，使与具体机器人无关的视觉运动策略能够零样本适应不同机器人本体，实现具身感知的部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于笛卡尔末端执行器动作的模仿学习策略与具体机器人无关，但缺乏对机器人本体约束（如全身碰撞避免）的感知，导致部署时脆弱。作者希望在不重新训练策略的前提下，让embodiment-agnostic的策略具备embodiment-aware的能力。

Method: EmbodiSteer是一种无需训练的框架，核心包括：(1) 保持策略在笛卡尔空间学习；(2) 推理时通过前向运动学和雅可比矩阵将扩散采样提升到目标机器人的关节空间；(3) 在每次去噪步骤后加入全身碰撞感知引导，将机械臂远离碰撞，同时保留已学习的末端执行器行为。

Result: 在9个模拟机器人上，与纯笛卡尔执行相比，碰撞率降低46.1%，任务成功率提升28.5%；在2个实体机器人的高约束场景中，碰撞率降低90.0%，成功率提升36.7%。

Conclusion: EmbodiSteer证明了无需重新训练即可将embodiment-agnostic的策略零样本迁移到embodiment-aware部署中，有效克服了笛卡尔策略对机器人本体约束无感知的局限性。

Abstract: Scalable robot imitation learning relies on large-scale heterogeneous data from diverse robots or body-free data, making Cartesian end-effector actions a key interface for embodiment-agnostic policy learning. However, end-effector-only abstraction leaves Cartesian policies unaware of the deployed robot body, making them brittle under robot-specific constraints such as whole-body collision avoidance. To overcome this limitation, we present EmbodiSteer, a training-free framework that steers embodiment-agnostic visuomotor policies toward zero-shot, embodiment-aware deployment. EmbodiSteer keeps policy learning in Cartesian space while efficiently lifting inference-time diffusion sampling into the target robot's joint space via forward kinematics and Jacobian-based updates. With whole-body collision-aware guidance over joint trajectories after each denoising step, the arm can be steered away from collisions while preserving learned end-effector behavior. Compared with Cartesian-only execution, EmbodiSteer reduces collision rate by 46.1% and improves task success rate by 28.5% across 9 simulated robots, and further achieves 90.0% collision rate reduction and 36.7% success rate increase on two physical robots in highly constrained scenarios. Our project page is at https://frankwang67.github.io/EmbodiSteer-Page.

</details>


### [150] [Trajectory-Level Redirection Attacks on Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12978)
*Gokul Puthumanaillam,Vardhan Dongre,Pranay Thangeda,Hooshang Nayyeri,Dilek Hakkani-Tür,Melkior Ornik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文发现并形式化了一种针对视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略的轨迹级对抗攻击：攻击者可以在不改变指令表面语义的情况下，通过微小扰动使机器人执行完全不同的物理任务，称为“命令保持的轨迹重定向”。作者提出了基于在线策略的提示搜索方法，在仿真和硬件实验中验证了该攻击的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA攻击研究仅关注针对低层级动作的对抗性提示，未考虑更危险的轨迹级失效模式——即提示看起来仍指定了原任务，但实际上改变了最终物理结果。

Method: 形式化定义了“命令保持的轨迹重定向”威胁模型：攻击者在回合前选择一个提示，策略和环境组件固定不变，提示需接近良性指令并禁止出现目标词和纠正性语言。提出基于在线策略的提示搜索方法，利用rollout搜索发现满足约束的对抗扰动。

Result: 仿真和硬件实验表明，接近良性的提示扰动能够将VLA rollout重定向到攻击者指定的目标，暴露了VLA指令 grounding 中的轨迹级漏洞。

Conclusion: 看似保留原命令的文本仍然可以使攻击者控制机器人的最终物理结果，揭示了VLA策略在指令理解层面存在严重的轨迹级安全脆弱性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies bring natural language into closed-loop robot control, enabling robots to execute manipulation tasks directly from text instructions. The same interface gives text a recurring role in control because the prompt is reused at every replanning step, and each prompt-conditioned action changes the future observations on which the policy acts. Existing VLA attacks study adversarial prompts that elicit targeted low-level actions or make such actions persist across changing images. We identify a stronger trajectory-level failure mode: a prompt that still $\textit{appears}$ to specify the intended task but redirects the final physical outcome. We mathematically formalize this setting as $\textit{command-preserving trajectory redirection}$, a prompt-only threat model in which the attacker chooses one prompt before the episode, all policy and environment components remain fixed, and the prompt must stay close to the benign instruction while omitting target words and correction language. To find such prompts, we introduce an on-policy prompt search method that uses rollouts to discover perturbations whose closed-loop behavior tracks a target task while satisfying the command-preserving constraints. Experiments in simulation and on hardware show that near-benign prompt perturbations can redirect VLA rollouts to attacker-specified targets. These results expose a trajectory-level vulnerability in VLA instruction grounding: text that appears to preserve the intended command can still give an adversary control over the robot's final physical outcome. Project website: https://vla-redirection-attack.github.io/

</details>


### [151] [GenHOI: Contact-Aware Humanoid-Object Interaction by Imitating Generated Videos without Task-Specific Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12995)
*Zhihai Bi,Qiang Zhang,Guoyang Zhao,Jiahang Cao,Xueyin Luo,Yushan Zhang,Jinglan Xu,Ruoyu Geng,Yulin Li,Andrew F. Luo,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GenHOI提出一种零样本人形机器人-物体交互框架，通过直接模仿单个生成视频实现多样化交互任务，无需任务特定训练或物理演示数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人与物体交互（HOI）中动态平衡与稳定交互紧密耦合，现有方法需要耗时的任务特定策略训练或依赖刚性的轨迹回放，难以适应新颖交互场景。

Method: 首先在仿真中重建机器人-物体场景并渲染首帧图像，结合语言指令生成任务导向交互视频；分析视频提取接触事件并估计手-物接触区域，编码为以物体为中心的几何约束；将视觉交互线索转化为物理优化先验，优化参考轨迹以解决2D视频生成的尺度模糊性并适应未见过的相对位姿；最后由闭环跟踪控制器执行。

Result: 在包括箱子抓取、非对称双臂搬椅子、从下方举桌子、圆柱物体包裹等多种交互任务的仿真和真实世界实验中验证了有效性。

Conclusion: GenHOI实现了零样本、无需任务特定训练或物理演示的多样化人形机器人-物体交互，通过单视频模仿即可泛化到新场景。

Abstract: Humanoid-Object Interaction (HOI) is a fundamental capability for humanoid robots, yet it remains challenging due to the tight coupling between dynamic balance and stable interaction with diverse objects. Existing methods often require time-consuming task-specific policy training or rely on rigid trajectory replay, which limits their ability to accommodate novel interaction scenarios. In this work, we present \textit{GenHOI}, a simple yet effective framework that enables humanoid robots to perform diverse object-interaction tasks in a zero-shot manner by directly imitating a single generated video, without task-specific training or physical demonstration data. GenHOI first reconstructs the robot-object scene in simulation and renders a first-frame image, which, together with the language command, conditions the synthesis of a task-oriented interaction video. The generated video is then analyzed to identify interaction-relevant contact events and estimate hand-object contact regions, which are encoded as object-centric geometric constraints that convert visual interaction cues into physically grounded optimization priors. Guided by these priors, the reference motion recovered from the video is refined and smoothed to resolve the scale ambiguity inherent in 2D video generation, while adapting a single reference trajectory to unseen robot-object relative poses. The optimized trajectory is finally executed by a closed-loop tracking controller. We validate the proposed framework in extensive simulation and real-world experiments across diverse object-interaction tasks, including box grasping, asymmetric bimanual chair carrying, table lifting from below, and cylindrical-object enveloping.

</details>


### [152] [Comparing Commercial Depth Sensor Accuracy for Medical Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13028)
*Pit Henrich,Maximilian Weiherer,Franziska Hansen,Bernhard Egger,Franziska Mathis-Ullrich*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文在猪骨、猪腹标本和硅胶肾脏模型上，使用触针采样参考，在约50cm距离下对四种深度传感器（Intel RealSense D405、PMD Flexx2、Stereolabs ZED 2i、Zivid 2M+ 60）进行了基准测试，评估其在均匀表面、镜面表面和次表面散射等真实挑战下的性能。Zivid 2M+ 60在所有对象和指标上表现最佳，ZED在真实组织上排名第二，但在仿体上排名最后。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度估计在医疗和外科领域有广泛应用，但不同深度传感器在面对真实组织（如均匀表面、镜面反射、次表面散射）时的性能尚未得到充分比较，因此需要系统地基准测试来指导传感器选型。

Method: 在猪骨、猪腹标本和硅胶肾脏模型上，以触针采样数据为参考真值，在约50cm距离下，对立体视觉（Stereolabs ZED 2i）、结构光（Zivid 2M+ 60）和飞行时间（Intel RealSense D405、PMD Flexx2）四类深度传感器进行性能对比。

Result: Zivid 2M+ 60在所有测试对象和评估指标上均表现最佳；Stereolabs ZED 2i在真实组织上排名第二，但在硅胶肾脏仿体上排名最后。

Conclusion: 在医疗手术场景的深度估计中，结构光传感器（Zivid 2M+ 60）综合性能最优，而立体视觉传感器在仿体表面存在明显局限性，选型需考虑具体应用场景中的目标材质特性。

Abstract: Depth estimation has numerous medical and surgical applications. We benchmark four depth sensors on a porcine bone specimen, a porcine belly specimen, and a silicone kidney phantom using stylus-sampled references. These objects contain several real-world challenges, including homogeneous surfaces, specular surfaces, and subsurface scattering. The comparison includes stereo, structured-light, and time-of-flight sensors at a distance of approximately 50 cm. Specifically, the Intel RealSense D405 (Intel RealSense, United States), PMD Flexx2 (pmdtechnologies, Germany), Stereolabs ZED 2i (Stereolabs, France), and Zivid 2M+ 60 (Zivid, Norway) are compared. The Zivid 2M+ 60 performed best across all objects and metrics considered in this work. The ZED ranked second for real tissue, but last on the phantom.

</details>


### [153] [RoboProcessBench: Benchmarking Process-Aware Understanding in Vision-Language Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13040)
*Dayu Xia,Yue Shi,Yao Mu,Huiting Ji,Chaofan Ma,Yingjie Zhou,Hua Chen,Yang Liu,Jiezhang Cao,Guangtao Zhai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboProcessBench是一个评估VLM对机器人操作过程理解能力的基准，包含静态监控和动态推理两个维度、12个诊断任务家族，约5.8万问答对。评测发现现有VLM在过程感知理解上存在广泛局限，但后训练可带来一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM评估集中在最终任务成功与否的判别上，缺乏对机器人操作执行过程中物理和时间进展的细粒度过程理解能力测试。

Method: 提出RoboProcessBench，将过程感知能力分解为静态监控和动态推理两个维度，实例化为12个诊断问题家族（涵盖阶段、接触、运动、协调、局部进度、时间顺序、结果、原语级转换等），基于物理真实执行轨迹构建ProcessData数据集（约5.8万QA对），分为ProcessData-SFT（后训练）和ProcessData-Eval（评估）两部分。

Result: 对多种VLM在ProcessData-Eval上的评估显示，模型在12个诊断任务家族上普遍表现有限；但使用ProcessData-SFT后训练的Qwen2.5-VL-7B和InternVL-3-8B在局部状态、运动、进度和原语感知提示上取得了一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: RoboProcessBench既可作为评估基准，也可作为可学习的监督来源，用于开发能够监控和评估机器人操作过程的VLM。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly explored as visual critics, reward generators, and failure detectors in robotic manipulation. These roles implicitly require models to judge not only final task success, but also how a manipulation execution is physically and temporally progressing. However, existing evaluations fail to test whether VLMs possess fine-grained process understanding. To address this gap, we present RoboProcessBench, a benchmark for process-aware understanding in vision-language robotic manipulation. RoboProcessBench decomposes such capability into two complementary dimensions, \emph{static monitoring} and \emph{dynamic reasoning}, instantiated as 12 diagnostic question families covering phase, contact, motion, coordination, primitive-local progress, temporal order, outcome, and primitive-level transitions. Built from physically grounded execution traces, the curated benchmark corpus ProcessData contains \textasciitilde 58k question-answer pairs across 260 manipulation tasks, which is further split into ProcessData-SFT and ProcessData-Eval for post-training and evaluation purposes. Extensive evaluation of various VLMs on ProcessData-Eval reveals broad limitations across 12 diagnostic task families, suggesting current models still lack robust process-aware understanding of manipulation executions. But with ProcessData-SFT, the post-trained \textit{Qwen2.5-VL-7B} and \textit{InternVL-3-8B} exhibit consistent gains on local state, motion, progress, and primitive-aware cues. These results demonstrate that RoboProcessBench serves as both an evaluation benchmark and a learnable supervision source for developing VLMs capable of monitoring and evaluating robotic manipulation processes. Project webpage: \href{https://processbench-2026.github.io/RoboProcessBench-Web/}{https://processbench-2026.github.io}.

</details>


### [154] [Y-BotFrame: An Extensible Embodied Agent Framework for Quadruped Robot Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13049)
*Luyao Zhang,Ke Li,Yuan Ding,Xulong Zhao,Guo Yu,Chengwei Yan,Fuyu Dong,Jiawei Hu,Di Wang,Nan Luo,Gang Liu,Quan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了 Y-BotFrame，一个将四足机器人转变为智能地面助手的可扩展具身平台。该系统集成多模态感知（语音、视觉、LiDAR），以大语言模型为认知核心，将用户自然语言指令映射为可执行的具身任务单元，支持语音和视觉反馈的自然交互，并具备即插即用的模块扩展能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人具有高灵活性和复杂地形适应能力，但缺乏一个统一、可扩展的智能平台来集成多模态感知与自然语言交互，使其成为真正通用、指令驱动的具身智能体。

Method: Y-BotFrame 集成语音、视觉、LiDAR 多模态感知，以大语言模型作为认知核心负责环境理解、上下文推理和任务规划，将用户自然语言指令映射为可执行的具身任务单元，支持语音命令和视觉反馈的自然交互，采用可扩展框架支持模块即插即用与迭代升级。

Result: 构建了一个无需遥控器、支持语音和视觉交互的四足机器人智能平台，实现了从自然语言指令到机器人实际动作的端到端映射，验证了模块化可扩展架构的可行性。

Conclusion: Y-BotFrame 为通用指令驱动具身智能体的实际部署提供了参考实现，展示了将大语言模型作为机器人认知核心的可行性与潜力。

Abstract: Quadruped robots are capable of traversing a wide range of complex terrains with high flexibility. As highly mobile ground-based intelligent platforms, they can be equipped with modules for navigation control, environmental perception, and intelligent interaction, thereby serving as real-world mobile deployment platforms for various algorithms. In this paper, we introduce Y-BotFrame, an extensible embodied platform that turns a robot into an intelligent ground assistant. Y-BotFrame integrates multimodal perception capabilities, including speech, vision, and LiDAR, and employs a large language model as the cognitive core for environmental understanding, contextual reasoning, and task planning. The system maps user natural-language instructions into executable embodied task units that can be carried out by the robot. Y-BotFrame supports natural interaction through voice commands and visual feedback, removing the need for a remote controller and enabling efficient human-robot collaboration. With a highly extensible framework, Y-BotFrame supports plug-and-play integration of new functional modules as well as modular upgrades and iterative development, offering a reference implementation for the real-world deployment of general-purpose, instruction-driven embodied agents.The supplementary video is available at https://xdei-group.github.io/Y-BotFrame/.

</details>


### [155] [EA-WM: Event-Aware World Models with Task-Specification Grounding for Long-Horizon Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13053)
*Kailin Wang,Haoxiang Jie,Yaoyuan Yan,Jiacheng Zhou,Zhiyou Heng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EA-WM是一个事件感知的世界模型框架，通过在预训练视觉特征空间中展开候选未来轨迹，解码为结构化事件状态，并利用任务进度、语义一致性、物理可行性和不确定性评分进行验证，从而提升机器人长程操作中特征空间世界模型的可解释性和任务对齐性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练特征世界模型在视觉或潜在预测上无法判断未来是否满足任务相关事件，长程操作需要关系性、谓词级别且物理上可落地的进度信号（如对象是否移动、接触状态是否改变、放置谓词是否满足、候选未来是否足够可靠）。

Method: EA-WM框架将冻结的视觉特征动力学与任务规范引导的事件预测和验证相结合：在预训练视觉特征空间中展开候选未来轨迹，解码为结构化事件状态，使用任务进度、语义一致性、物理可行性和不确定性四项评分进行验证；验证器指导基于采样的规划、门控候选动作，并在接触敏感的LIBERO酒架场景中从PPO生成的动作提案中选择最优动作。

Result: 在导航、可变形物体、墙壁约束和语言描述的操作任务中，EA-WM表明事件感知验证能使特征空间世界模型更可解释，且与任务进度更好地对齐。

Conclusion: 事件感知的验证机制是提升特征空间世界模型在长程操作任务中实用性的有效途径，能够使模型输出与任务目标更一致。

Abstract: Pretrained-feature world models provide a useful substrate for robot imagination, but visual or latent prediction alone does not determine whether an imagined future satisfies task-relevant events. Long-horizon manipulation requires progress signals that are relational, predicate-level, and physically grounded: whether an object has moved, whether a drawer or contact state has changed, whether a placement predicate is satisfied, and whether a candidate future is reliable enough for execution. We introduce EA-WM, an event-aware world-model framework that augments frozen visual-feature dynamics with task-specification-grounded event prediction and verification. EA-WM rolls out candidate futures in pretrained visual-feature space, decodes them into structured event states, and scores them using task-progress, semantic-consistency, physical-feasibility, and uncertainty terms. The verifier guides sampling-based planning, gates candidate actions, and, in the contact-sensitive LIBERO wine-rack setting, selects among PPOgenerated proposals. Across navigation, deformable-object, wall-constrained, and languagedescribed manipulation studies, EA-WM shows that event-aware verification can make featurespace world models more interpretable and better aligned with task progress.

</details>


### [156] [FTP-1: A Generalist Foundation Tactile Policy Across Tactile Sensors for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13102)
*Chengbo Yuan,Zicheng Zhang,Mingjie Zhou,Wendi Chen,Yi Wang,Zhuoyang Liu,Dantong Niu,Shuo Wang,Hui Zhang,Wenkang Zhang,Yingdong Hu,Yuanqing Gong,Wanli Xing,Chuan Wen,Cewu Lu,Kaifeng Zhang,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FTP-1是首个通用基础触觉策略，通过异构编码器将多种触觉信号（图像、阵列、状态）统一为形态感知潜在令牌，在26个数据源、21种传感器、约3000小时触觉操作数据上预训练，实现跨传感器和本体的触觉操作技能迁移，在已见传感器上提升17.2%，在未见传感器上提升31%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有触觉策略紧绑定固定传感器和本体构型，无法跨传感器泛化，因为触觉信号在硬件层面高度异构。

Method: 使用异构编码器将不同类型触觉输入（图像、阵列、状态信号）投影到统一的形态感知潜在令牌，再由共享的触觉Transformer专家联合建模，在26个数据源（约3000小时、21种传感器）上进行预训练。

Result: 在5种硬件配置的下游微调实验中，已见传感器设置上提升17.2%成功率；在两种未见触觉传感器设置上实现31%的成功率提升。

Conclusion: FTP-1建立了首个统一的基础触觉操作基线，为未来触觉策略提供了共享的模型级起点，验证了跨传感器和本体的触觉技能迁移能力。

Abstract: Despite the success of vision-based generalist robotic policies, existing tactile-based policies remain tied to fixed embodiments and sensor setups. This is because tactile signals are highly heterogeneous across hardware, making cross-sensor generalization difficult. We present FTP-1,the first generalist foundation tactile policy pretrained to acquire transferable tactile manipulation abilities across diverse sensors and embodiments. FTP-1 supports varied tactile inputs, including image-, array-, and state-based signals, by using heterogeneous encoders to project them into unified morphology-aware latent tokens that are jointly modeled by a shared tactile Transformer expert. Pretrained on around 3,000 hours of tactile manipulation data aggregated from 26 data sources, spanning human and robot demonstrations across 21 sensors, FTP-1 learns tactile skills that transfer beyond the sensors seen during pretraining. Across downstream finetuning experiments spanning 5 hardware configurations, FTP-1 improves contact-rich manipulation on seen sensor setups by +17.2% and, surprisingly, transfers to two previously unseen tactile-sensor setups, achieving a +31% gain in success rate. FTP-1 establishes the first unified foundation baseline for tactile manipulation, providing future tactile policies with a shared model-level starting point. Pretrained models, datasets, training code and more visualization at https://ftp1-policy.github.io.

</details>


### [157] [Redesigning Regularization for Effective Policy Smoothing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13169)
*Taisuke Kobayashi,Naoto Yamanaka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种改进的正则化方法，有效平滑强化学习中的策略函数，解决了原始实现中理论-实现不一致、平滑不足的问题，并在多任务及四足机器人sim-to-real迁移中验证了平滑策略对突发速度指令的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始的正则化设计虽然旨在增强Lipschitz连续性以平滑策略函数，但存在全局Lipschitz与表达能力之间的权衡，且原始实现繁琐、平滑效果不足；因此需要识别问题根源并提供修复方案。

Method: 识别原始实现失效的三个原因并提出相应补救措施，改进正则化设计以更有效地平滑策略函数。

Result: 改进后的正则化在多个任务和算法上表现良好，实现了平滑运动并提升了控制性能；在四足机器人sim-to-real迁移中，平滑策略对目标速度指令的突变具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 适当修正实现方式的正则化可以有效平滑策略函数，不仅提升控制性能，还能增强策略在动态环境下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel regularization design to effectively smooth policy functions in reinforcement learning. While regularization that enhances ``global'' Lipschitz continuity was initially considered, it has been limited to ``local'' Lipschitz continuity due to a tradeoff between smoothness and expressiveness. However, it has become apparent that the original implementation is cumbersome and does not provide sufficient smoothing, leading to a preference for simpler implementations. This stems from a discrepancy between theory and implementation, and a more appropriate implementation can expect to facilitate smoothing. Therefore, this paper identifies three reasons why the original implementation does not function adequately and provide remedies for them. This modified regularization performs well across multiple tasks and algorithms, successfully achieving smooth motion while improving control performance. Furthermore, by applying it to sim-to-real reinforcement learning for a quadruped robot, it is demonstrated that smooth motion provides robustness against sudden changes in target velocity commands.

</details>


### [158] [Multi-Modal Multi-Agent Robotic Cognitive Alignment enabled by Non-Invasive Consumer Brain Computer Interfaces: A Proof of Concept Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13190)
*Nataliya Kosmyna,Liz Jenkins,Anoop K. Sinha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种“认知对齐”的多智能体交互框架，通过消费级BCI实时监测人类EEG频谱能量，在高认知负荷时将主智能体置于保持状态，待认知负荷降低后释放消息，实现非侵入式的人机交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人交互方法忽略了人类内部认知状态，导致多智能体系统常在不当时刻打断用户，造成认知过载和任务绩效下降。

Method: 采用消费级BCI实时监测EEG频谱带功率，构建基于HTTP保持机制的闭环架构：检测到高投入状态时将主智能体的输入输出保持，由次要智能体后台处理复杂任务；待认知负荷回归基线后释放队列消息。

Result: 初步结果验证了利用实时信号处理、LLMs和物理机器人实体构建认知感知、非侵入式多智能体系统的可行性。

Conclusion: 通过生理神经信号实时调控智能体交互时序，可有效减少不当打断，实现认知对齐的人机协作。

Abstract: While non-verbal behaviors and expressive movements are essential for natural human-robot interaction, existing methods often overlook a crucial element: the human's internal cognitive state. Frequently, proactive multi-agent systems can interrupt humans at inopportune moments, leading to cognitive overload and decreased task performance. This paper introduces a framework for generating "cognitively aligned" multi-agent interactions, enhancing the ability of robotic systems to contextually defer communications to the user of an agent system during moments of high human mental workload and engagement. We present the design and implementation of a closed-loop architecture that explores the interplay between autonomous task execution and real-time neurophysiological focus. Using a consumer-grade Brain-Computer Interface (BCI), our approach continuously monitors Electroencephalography (EEG) spectral band powers while a human performs an engagement-inducing task. We propose an engagement-driven pipeline where an HTTP-based signaling mechanism places a primary agent's sensory inputs and audio outputs into a holding state upon detecting high engagement. This allows secondary agents to seamlessly process complex, delegated tasks in the background. Once the human's cognitive state returns to a lower cognitive load baseline, the primary agent releases the queued agent message. Our preliminary results demonstrate the feasibility of leveraging real-time signal processing, Large Language Models (LLMs), and physical robotic embodiments to create cognitively-aware, non-intrusive multi-agent systems.

</details>


### [159] [Embedding ISO 10218 Safety Compliance in Robots via Control Barrier Functions for Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13203)
*Federico Parma,Cesare Tonola,Nicola Pedrocchi,Manuel Beschi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出利用人体加速度数据构建控制障碍函数（CBF），结合序列二次规划（SQP）框架预测人机最小安全距离，在满足ISO 10218安全标准的同时减少不必要的停机，其中任务缩放SQP控制器（Method II）相比CBF-PD安全滤波器（Method I）降低63%平均轨迹误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有标准速度和分离监控（SSM）滤波器基于保守假设（如人体匀速运动），无法准确预测最小分离距离，导致不必要的操作停机，影响人机协作效率。

Method: 提出两种方法：Method I为CBF约束的PD安全滤波器；Method II为任务缩放SQP控制器，通过施加空间管状约束强制执行安全不等式。两者均将预测性CBF作为不等式约束集成到SQP框架中。

Result: Method II相比Method I平均轨迹误差降低63%，避免过度回避机动，在遵守ISO 10218 SSM准则的同时保证高任务吞吐量。

Conclusion: 结合人体加速度数据的预测性CBF-SQP方法能有效平衡人机协作中的安全性与任务效率，Method II在安全约束下实现更优的轨迹跟踪性能。

Abstract: Human-Robot Collaboration (HRC) requires strict adherence to safety standards, such as ISO 10218, to prevent harmful interactions. Standard Speed and Separation Monitoring (SSM) filters calculate safe robotic speeds based on conservative assumptions, such as constant human velocity, which prevents accurate predictions of minimum separation distances and causes unnecessary operational halts. This paper proposes a Control Barrier Function (CBF) that explicitly incorporates human acceleration data to analytically forward-predict the minimum human-robot separation distance during a worst-case robotic stopping trajectory. To guarantee safety at the control level, this predictive CBF is integrated as an inequality constraint within a Sequential Quadratic Programming (SQP) framework. Specifically, two methods are proposed: Method I, a CBF-constrained PD safety filter; and Method II, a task-scaling SQP controller that enforces a spatial tube constraint. Simulated and real-world experiments on a UR10e robot evaluate the two proposed methods against a standard industrial SSM module baseline. Results demonstrate that Method II dynamically modulates execution speed and confines spatial deviations. Compared to Method I, Method II achieves a 63\% reduction in mean trajectory error and avoids excessive evasive manoeuvres, ensuring high task throughput while complying with ISO 10218 SSM guidelines.

</details>


### [160] [Proprioceptive-visual correspondence enables self-other distinction in humanoid robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13222)
*Yurun Chen,Tianyuan Gao,Yizhong Ge,Shikun Ban,Yizhou Wang,Hongkai Xiong,Wenjun Zeng,Wentao Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文展示了人形机器人能够通过本体感觉-视觉对应学习自我-他人区分，无需身份标签或运动学模型，进而构建预测性自身体模型，支持目标抓取、碰撞感知运动规划和人体到机器人运动重定向等下游任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在共享工作空间中缺乏区分自我与他人的能力，而这是社交智能的前提条件。

Method: 利用本体感觉（关节配置）与视觉（3D身体占用）之间的对应关系进行无监督学习，无需身份标签或预定义运动学模型；通过自我-他人区分引导自身体模型的建立。

Result: 系统能在多智能体场景中（包括人类和形态相同的人形机器人）可靠识别自我，学习3D自身体模型，并成功应用于目标抓取、碰撞感知运动规划和人体到机器人运动重定向。

Conclusion: 这项工作为机器人在共享物理环境中建立身体自我表征提供了一条可行路径，使机器人能够与他人协调行动。

Abstract: Distinguishing self from others is a prerequisite for social intelligence, yet humanoid robots that increasingly share workspaces with humans still lack this ability. Here we show that a humanoid robot can learn self-other distinction from proprioceptive-visual correspondence, without any identity labels or kinematic models. Once established, this distinction bootstraps a predictive self-model that maps joint configurations to three-dimensional body occupancy, capturing how the robot's body changes with action. In multi-agent scenes involving humans or morphologically identical robots, the system reliably identifies itself, learns a 3D self-model, and supports downstream tasks including target reaching, collision-aware motion planning, and human-to-robot motion retargeting. Together, these results outline a route toward bodily self-representation in robots that act and coordinate alongside others in shared physical environments. Project page: https://euron-zc.github.io/humanoid-self-model/.

</details>


### [161] [WT-UMI: Tactile-based Whole-Body Manipulation via Force-Supervised Contact-Aware Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13232)
*Jaehwi Jang,Zhaoyuan Gu,Alfred Cueva,Zimeng Chai,Junjie Sheng,Thong Nguyen,Himank Galundia,Yifan Wu,Huishu Xue,Isaac Legene,Ojas Mediratta,Davin Doan,Andrew Collins,Sarah Sadegh,KyoungMok Kim,Rishita Dhalbisoi,Zun Chen,Ye Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WT-UMI是一种可穿戴全身触觉接口，支持人类演示和人形遥操作两种模式，通过力条件目标姿态校正模块、力监督规划器和基于触觉的导纳控制器，在变形物体、笨重刚体和人机协作等接触丰富任务中提升了成功率和接触位置跟踪精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全身人形操作笨重、可变形和共享负载物体需要分布式接触感知和显式力控制，但现有模仿策略大多仅隐式处理接触力；同时不同演示源（人类演示提供自然接触力但非机器人可执行动作，遥操作记录机器人动作但力调节不够自然）存在互补模态与固有权衡。

Method: 提出WT-UMI可穿戴全身触觉接口，支持人类演示和人形遥操作双模式；设计力条件目标姿态校正模块，从遥操作数据学习将人类姿态转换为接触感知的机器人目标；提出力监督规划器，预测末端执行器姿态块和接触力轨迹；预测的接触力作为基于触觉的导纳控制器参考输入。

Result: 在变形物体、笨重刚体和人机协作五类接触丰富任务中，WT-UMI相比四种基线策略提升了成功率并降低了接触位置跟踪误差。

Conclusion: 通过融合人类演示的自然力交互与遥操作的机器人可执行动作，WT-UMI有效解决了全身人形操作中接触力显式建模的关键问题，验证了力条件策略在复杂接触任务中的优势。

Abstract: Whole-body humanoid manipulation of bulky, deformable, and shared-load objects requires distributed contact sensing and explicit force regulation, yet most imitation policies treat contact force only implicitly. On the other hand, different demonstration sources provide complementary modalities with inherent trade-offs: human demonstrations capture natural contact forces but not robot-executable actions, while teleoperation directly records robot actions but with less natural force regulation. This paper presents \textbf{WT-UMI}, a wearable whole-body tactile interface worn by human operators or mounted on humanoids, providing accurate observations of tactile images, contact forces, and end-effector poses across both human demonstration and humanoid teleoperation modes. We introduce a force-conditioned target-pose correction module that converts measured human poses into contact-aware robot targets by learning corrections from teleoperation data. To leverage the natural force interaction in human data, we propose a force-supervised planner that predicts end-effector pose chunks and contact-force trajectories. The predicted contact force serves as the reference for a tactile-based admittance controller. Across five contact-rich tasks spanning deformable objects, bulky rigid objects, and human--humanoid collaboration, WT-UMI improves success rate and reduces contact-position tracking error over four policy baselines. Our project page is available at https://wt-umi.github.io/WTUMI/.

</details>


### [162] [Humor Style Drives Laughter, Topic Shapes Acceptability: Evaluating Bilingual Personal and Political Robot-Delivered AI Jokes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13256)
*Anna-Maria Velentza,Anne-Gwenn Bosser*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究探索了在群体场景中，机器人通过LLM生成不同风格（亲和型、自我增强型、攻击型、自嘲型）和内容（人物相关 vs. 政治）的笑话对趣味性、适当性及语言偏好的影响。结果表明，幽默类型显著影响趣味性（攻击型和亲和型评分更高），笑话内容主要影响适当性（人物相关笑话更受青睐），语言偏好受内容及参与者语言流利度和幽默习惯共同影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 幽默在人类社交关系中至关重要，计算幽默的最新进展为将幽默融入人机交互（HRI）创造了新机遇。然而，在群体场景中，幽默风格、笑话内容和语言偏好如何影响机器人传递幽默的感知，目前尚不清楚。

Method: 采用混合因子设计，在高校教室中让参与者评估由机器人通过LLM生成的不同类型（亲和型、自我增强型、攻击型、自嘲型）和内容（人物相关 vs. 政治）的笑话，测量趣味性、适当性及语言偏好。

Result: 幽默类型显著影响趣味性，攻击型和亲和型幽默评分更高；笑话内容主要影响适当性，人物相关笑话优于政治笑话；语言偏好受笑话内容及参与者自报语言流利度和幽默实践共同影响。

Conclusion: 幽默类型与笑话内容对机器人幽默感知有不同维度的作用，语言偏好受个体差异调节，为群组HRI中的幽默设计提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Humor plays a central role in human social relationships, and recent advances in computational humor create new opportunities for integrating humor into human-robot interaction (HRI). While large language models (LLMs) can generate diverse forms of humor, it remains unclear how humor style, joke content, and language preference shape perceptions of robot-delivered humor in group settings. In this exploratory study, we employed a mixed factorial design in which participants evaluated AI-generated jokes delivered by a robot in a university classroom. We examined the effects of humor type (Affiliative, Self-Enhancing, Aggressive, Self-Defeating) and joke content (person-related vs. political) on perceived funniness and appropriateness, as well as preferred language. Results show that humor type significantly influences funniness, with Aggressive and Affiliative humor rated higher, while joke content primarily affects appropriateness, with person-related jokes preferred over political ones. Language preference was shaped by both joke content and participants' self-reported fluency and humor practices.

</details>


### [163] [See Selectively, Act Adaptively: Dual-Level Structural Decomposition for Bimanual Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13279)
*Yoon-Ji Choi,Young-Chae Son,Soo-Chul Lim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对双人机器人操作中视觉信息随任务阶段变化、双臂交互模式动态切换的问题，提出基于双层结构分解的VLA框架：视图选择视觉路由器动态调整腕部视角权重，交互感知动作混合专家将动作生成分解为协调模式和单臂路径，在模拟和真实任务中分别比基线提升27.7%和43.3%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单一VLA策略通过共享表示和动作生成路径处理多样化的视觉输入和双臂交互模式，未能分别处理视觉相关性和双臂交互结构，导致策略学习困难。

Method: 提出双层结构分解的双臂操作VLA框架：(1)视图选择视觉路由器动态调整腕部视角贡献以突出相关视觉线索；(2)交互感知动作混合专家将动作生成分解为协调路径和单臂路径，以适应不同的双臂交互模式。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0的六个模拟任务中平均成功率提升27.7%，在三个长时域真实世界任务中平均成功率提升43.3%，且一致优于单模块变体。

Conclusion: 联合考虑选择性视觉处理和双臂交互结构的显式分解为鲁棒的双臂操作提供了有效的归纳偏置。

Abstract: In bimanual robotic manipulation, task-relevant visual information varies with the task stage and context, while the interaction of the two arms shifts between independent and coordinated modes, making policy learning challenging. However, existing monolithic Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies process diverse visual inputs and interaction patterns through a single shared representation and action generation pathway, often failing to separately account for visual relevance and bimanual interaction structure. To address this issue, we propose a bimanual manipulation VLA framework based on Dual-Level Structural Decomposition. The View-Selective Visual Router dynamically adjusts wrist-view contributions to emphasize relevant visual cues, while the Interaction-Aware Action Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) decomposes action generation into coordinated and arm-wise pathways to adapt to varying bimanual interaction modes. We evaluate the proposed method on six simulated bimanual manipulation tasks in RoboTwin 2.0 and three long-horizon real-world tasks. Our model improves the overall average success rate over a monolithic baseline by 27.7% in simulation and 43.3% in real-world evaluation, while consistently outperforming single-module variants across both settings. These results demonstrate that jointly considering selective visual processing and explicit decomposition of bimanual interaction structures provides an effective inductive bias for robust bimanual manipulation.

</details>


### [164] [EMG-Based Adaptation of Anisotropic Virtual Fixtures for Robot-Assisted Surgical Resection and Dissection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13340)
*Dario Onfiani,Michael Dyck,Luigi Biagiotti,Julian Klodmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于机器人辅助腹腔镜手术的自适应各向异性虚拟夹具框架，通过EMG信号实时推断外科医生意图并动态调整约束几何，在标准手术训练任务中显著提升了任务精度、运动一致性，并降低了认知负荷和挫败感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统虚拟夹具由固定几何形状定义，缺乏适应手术流程或外科医生即时意图的灵活性，无法满足精细手术任务（如切除和剥离）中对动态引导的需求。

Method: 提出自适应各向异性虚拟夹具框架，结合EMG信号推断外科医生意图，通过前臂肌肉收缩实时扩展或解除约束，实现精确引导与自由 repositioning 之间的无缝切换。

Result: 在标准化手术训练任务的受试者研究中，该方法显著提升了任务精度和运动一致性，同时降低了感知认知负荷、努力程度和挫败感。

Conclusion: 所提出的自适应虚拟夹具框架在机器人辅助腹腔镜手术中有效，为手术引导系统的动态适应性提供了可行方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we address the development of an adaptive assistance system for robot-assisted laparoscopic surgery, specifically for delicate tasks such as Resection and Dissection. Even if Virtual Fixtures offer significant advantages for guiding a surgeon's movements, conventional Virtual Fixtures are often defined by fixed geometries, lacking the flexibility to adapt to the surgical workflow or the surgeon's immediate intent. To address these limitations, we propose a novel framework for an adaptive and anisotropic virtual fixture. In addition, we introduce an intuitive control interface that modulates the fixture's geometry in real-time based on the surgeon's intent, inferred from EMG signals. This approach allows the surgeon to dynamically expand or disengage the constraint by contracting their forearm muscles, enabling seamless transitions between precise guided motion and free repositioning of the tool. Experimental results from a pilot user study, based on a standardized surgical training task, demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method. The system showed significant improvements in task accuracy and movement consistency, alongside a reduction in perceived cognitive load, effort, and frustration.

</details>


### [165] [Low cost, easily manufactured, highly flexible strain and touch sensitive fiber for robotics applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13352)
*Christian Diaz Herrera,Srushti Raste,Simin Liu,Miles Modeste,Jiyang,Yin,Katelyn McCall,Yuxing Jared Yao,Roopkamal Chahal,Simon Chidley,Trung Ha,T. David Westmoreland,Sonia Roberts*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种使用廉价商用材料（导电缝纫线、硅胶管）和工具（穿针器）快速制造的导电纤维，可作为电阻应变传感器和电容传感器用于机器人触觉和接近感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器人拉伸和触觉传感器在材料成本、制造设备或制造时间方面至少有一项昂贵，需要一种低成本、快速制造且性能可用的传感器方案。

Method: 使用商用导电缝纫线（$0.07/英尺）和硅胶管（$0.94/英尺），通过穿针器将线穿过硅胶管，2分钟内可制造20cm长的导电纤维；将其用作电阻应变传感器和电容传感器，并通过针织展示其柔性。

Result: 作为电阻应变传感器：触发气动辅助手指抓取、感知气动机器人带的位置、估计柔性固体姿态；作为电容传感器：触发商业机械臂运动（触觉）、实现机械臂跟随移动手（近场传感）。同时展示了断裂纤维的修复方法。

Conclusion: 该导电纤维制造成本低、速度快，具备电阻应变和电容传感双重功能，可扩展制造并修复，为低成本机器人传感提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Existing stretch and touch sensors for robots are generally expensive with respect to at least one of material costs, required manufacturing equipment, or manufacturing time. We present and experimentally characterize a conductive fiber made using only inexpensive commercial off-the-shelf parts (conductive thread at $0.07/ft, silicone tubing at $0.94/ft) and tools (loop-style needle threader at $2), which can be manufactured quickly (20 cm length in 2 minutes.) We demonstrate its use as a resistive strain sensor with three applications: Triggering a grasp in a pneumatically actuated assistive finger, sensing the pose of a pneumatically actuated robotic strap, and estimating the pose of a flexible solid. We also demonstrate that it can be used as a capacitive sensor with two applications: First, as a touch sensor which triggers a commercial robot arm to move, and second, as a near-field sensor enabling the robot arm to follow a moving hand. The capacitive sensors are knitted, showcasing the high flexibility of the fiber. We discuss methods for improving manufacturing scalability and their cost trade-offs. Finally, we demonstrate a method for repairing a cut fiber.

</details>


### [166] [Real-Time Execution with Autoregressive Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13355)
*Sangkyu Lee,Seohyeon Park,Tackgeun You,Avi Caciularu,Idan Szpektor,Hwasup Lim,Youngjae Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文证明自回归策略可以通过调整token化粒度和应用约束解码实现实时执行，在保证严格延迟边界的同时进行多轨迹解码，在仿真和真实环境中持续优于同等水平的流匹配策略，并具有更快的收敛速度和更好的指令跟随泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模视觉-语言-动作模型的实际部署需要实时执行，但现有实时执行研究主要聚焦扩散策略变体，而自回归策略由于同步推理时 rollout 速度较慢，其实时执行能力更值得关注但未被充分研究。

Method: 通过调整token化粒度（tokenization horizon）和应用约束解码（constrained decoding）来实现严格的延迟边界，从而支持多轨迹解码以最大化性能。

Result: 自回归策略在仿真和真实环境中持续优于同等水平的流匹配策略，同时同步推理的任务完成速度显著提升。

Conclusion: 自回归策略凭借更快的收敛速度和更好的指令跟随泛化能力，仍然可以是支持实时执行的竞争性策略类型。

Abstract: Real-time execution, enabled by asynchronous inference that ensures both smooth action trajectories and fast reactivity, is critical for realistic deployments of large-scale Vision-Language-Action models. However, recent work on real-time execution primarily focuses on variants of diffusion policies, even though it is more critical for autoregressive policies given their slower rollout speed in synchronous inference. In contrast, we demonstrate that autoregressive policies can achieve real-time execution by adjusting the tokenization horizon and applying constrained decoding, thereby guaranteeing strict latency bounds that enable multi-trajectory decoding to maximize performance. Across simulated and real-world environments, we find that the autoregressive policy consistently outperforms its equivalent-level flow-matching policy counterpart while achieving significantly improved task completion speeds from synchronous inference. Coupled with the inherent advantages of autoregressive policies, such as faster convergence and better generalizability in instruction-following, these results confirm that autoregressive policies can remain a competitive policy type supporting real-time execution.

</details>


### [167] [GeoHAT: Geometry-Adaptive Hybrid Action Transformer for Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13394)
*Xiangyu Zhu,Renjun Wu,Luzhou Ge,Jinyan Liu,Xuesong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GeoHAT是一个基于扩散的端到端框架，通过轻量级傅里叶空间编码器将密集3D坐标映射为几何token，并以门控融合方式选择性注入视觉基础模型特征，同时采用混合全身动作解码器将机械臂与底盘解耦为独立子空间进行稀疏交叉注意力，在ManiSkill-HAB仿真中实现79.3%平均成功率（超越最强基线23.7%），并在真实世界任务中取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全身移动操作需要协调移动底盘和机械臂在不同视角下的操作，现有方法存在三个核心问题：1）依赖2D特征或稀疏3D表示，缺乏密集空间结构；2）将机械臂和底座编码在同一动作向量中，忽略了两者不同的控制需求；3）现有的密集融合策略在噪声深度下会破坏预训练表示，且计算开销大。

Method: GeoHAT包含三大核心组件：1）轻量级傅里叶空间编码器，将逐像素密集3D坐标映射为几何token，无需额外3D视觉骨干；2）基于深度有效性的逐token门控融合机制，将几何token选择性注入视觉基础模型特征，保留语义先验的同时增强空间理解；3）混合全身动作解码器，将机械臂和底盘动作解耦为独立子空间，各动作模态通过稀疏交叉注意力关注任务相关视觉上下文，并使用因果时序建模捕获帧内协调与帧间依赖。

Result: 在ManiSkill-HAB仿真基准上，GeoHAT达到79.3%的平均成功率，超越最强基线23.7%。在真实世界的多样化任务中，该方法也一致性地优于所有基线方法。

Conclusion: GeoHAT验证了"几何只应在可靠处注入、只应在需要处关注"这一简单原则的有效性，通过选择性几何注入与解耦动作生成，在全身移动操作任务上实现了显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Whole-body mobile manipulation requires coordinating mobile base and manipulator under shifting viewpoints, posing challenges in geometric perception and action generation. Current policies either rely on 2D features or sparse 3D representations that lack dense spatial structure, and typically encode arm and base within one action vector that ignores their distinct control demands. Moreover, existing dense fusion strategies risk corrupting pretrained representations under noisy depth while incurring heavy computational overhead. We present GeoHAT, an end-to-end diffusion-based framework built on a simple principle: geometry should be injected only where reliable and attended to only where needed. GeoHAT employs a lightweight Fourier spatial encoder that maps dense per-pixel 3D coordinates into geometric tokens without an additional 3D vision backbone. These tokens are then selectively injected into vision foundation model features through per-token gated fusion modulated by depth validity, preserving the semantic prior while enriching spatial understanding. For action generation, a Hybrid Whole-Body Action Decoder decomposes arm and base into distinct subspaces and lets each action modality attend to its task-relevant visual context through sparse cross-attention, while causal temporal modeling captures intra-timestep coordination and inter-timestep dependencies. Experiments on the ManiSkill-HAB simulation benchmark demonstrate that GeoHAT achieves a 79.3% mean success rate, surpassing the strongest baseline by 23.7%. Furthermore, real-world experiments on diverse tasks also confirm consistent improvements over all baselines.

</details>


### [168] [GIVE: Grounding Human Gestures in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13435)
*Pengfei Liu,Gen Li,Junqiao Fan,Boyu Ma,Jindou Jia,Yang Xiao,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出GIVE方法，通过视觉路径（手部骨架与指尖射线）和语义路径（手势描述与任务指令）增强预训练VLA模型的手势理解能力，在真实HRI实验中目标识别准确率提升40%，任务成功率提升80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型将机器人操作视为纯文本驱动任务，忽略了手势在HRI中的关键作用，导致语言指令模糊或未明确指定时意图接地不准确、操作不可靠。

Method: 通过两条互补路径增强预训练VLA模型：视觉路径将手部骨架和指尖射线叠加到机器人观测中实现显式物体接地；语义路径生成手势与任务指令的高层描述实现鲁棒的意图接地。

Result: 真实HRI实验中，目标物体识别准确率提升40%，任务成功率提升80%，对未见空间布局和不同参与者具有良好的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: GIVE能够在不修改模型架构的前提下有效融入手势信息，使VLA策略更好地将手势与操作行为关联，适应动态交互意图。

Abstract: Human communication is inherently multimodal, where language is often accompanied by non-verbal cues such as gestures to convey intentions. However, current Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models treat robotic manipulation as a pure text-driven task, overlooking the important role of gestures in Human-Robot Interaction (HRI). This often leads to inaccurate intent grounding and unreliable manipulation when language instructions are ambiguous or underspecified. To address this challenge, we propose GIVE (Gesture Intent via Visual-Semantic Enhancement), an effective approach that enhances pre-trained VLA models with human gesture understanding without architectural modifications. Specifically, GIVE incorporates gesture information through two complementary pathways: a visual pathway that overlays hand skeletons and fingertip rays onto robot observations for explicit object grounding, and a semantic pathway that generates high-level descriptions of human gestures and task instructions for robust intent grounding. By jointly leveraging visual and semantic guidance, GIVE enables VLA policies to better associate gestures with manipulation behaviors and adapt to dynamic interaction intents. In real-world HRI experiments, GIVE substantially outperforms the baseline, improving target object recognition accuracy by 40% and overall task success rate by 80%, while demonstrating strong robustness and generalization to unseen spatial layouts and diverse participants.

</details>


### [169] [NavWAM: A Navigation World Action Model for Goal-Conditioned Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13494)
*Daichi Azuma,Taiki Miyanishi,Koya Sakamoto,Shuhei Kurita,Yaonan Zhu,Petr Khrapchenkov,Motoaki Kawanabe,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NavWAM是一种扩散变换器策略，通过将未来观测、目标进度值和动作块编码到共享潜序列中，将导航世界模型的视觉预测直接转化为可执行的动作，无需外部规划器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有导航世界模型仅作为预测模块，需要外部规划器将预测的未来转换为闭环控制，无法直接用于机器人控制。

Method: 提出NavWAM，一种扩散变换器策略：将未来观测、目标进度值和动作块表示在共享潜序列中，联合学习未来预测与动作及价值目标，通过仿真预训练和真实机器人适配实现。

Result: 在离线基准测试和真实机器人闭环部署中，NavWAM在默认策略模式（无需CEM风格的动作搜索）下优于基于规划的世界模型基线。

Conclusion: NavWAM通过联合学习预测与决策，使视觉前向预测直接可作用于机器人控制，有效弥合了世界模型与闭环策略之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Goal-conditioned visual navigation requires a robot to act under partial observability by anticipating how its motion will change the future egocentric view and whether that change brings it closer to the goal. Navigation world models provide such visual foresight, but they remain prediction modules that require an external planner to convert predicted futures into closed-loop control. We propose Navigation World Action Model (NavWAM), a diffusion-transformer policy that turns navigation world-model prediction into executable action by representing future observations, goal-progress values, and action chunks in a shared latent sequence. By learning future prediction jointly with the action and value targets that determine closed-loop behavior, NavWAM makes visual foresight directly usable for robot control. We build NavWAM through simulation pretraining and real-robot adaptation, and evaluate it on image-goal navigation against planning-based world models and a representative direct navigation policy. Across offline benchmarks and closed-loop real-robot deployment, NavWAM improves over planning-based world-model baselines in our evaluations while using the default policy mode without CEM-style action search. Project page: https://dachii-azm.github.io/navwam/

</details>


### [170] [SPARC: Reliable Spatial Annotations from Robot Demonstrations at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13497)
*Nils Blank,Paul Mattes,Maximilian Xiling Li,Jakub Suliga,Thomas Roth,Moritz Reuss,Pankhuri Vanjani,Rudolf Lioutikov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SPARC是一种风险感知框架，利用机器人演示中的时空结构自动生成带可靠性校准的结构化空间标注（如边界框、物体轨迹、操作阶段标签），在保留更多有用样本的同时显著减少噪声标签。实验表明，基于SPARC标注微调的模型在物体定位和指向基准上达到SOTA，且训练的机器人策略在真实场景中优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化标注管线虽可大规模生成结构化空间标注，但其检测器置信度与标注正确性之间存在校准不良的问题，导致研究者不得不在接受噪声标签与丢弃有用样本之间做出权衡。SPARC旨在通过利用机器人任务固有的时空结构来生成可靠性信号，在减少噪声标签的同时保留更多有用样本。

Method: SPARC利用机器人演示中蕴含的时空结构信息（如物体随时间的运动轨迹、操作阶段之间的时序依赖）来校准标注的可靠性分数，而非仅依赖单帧检测器置信度。同时引入IA-Bench基准，用于评估模型在机器人演示中对交互物体位置定位的准确性。

Result: 在跨越多种实体和场景的1,700个人工标注演示上，SPARC在定位精度上显著优于仅依赖检测器的基线方法，同时在高精度运行点下保留了三倍以上的样本。基于其标注微调的模型在物体定位和指向基准上达到同等规模模型中的SOTA，在更广泛的空间推理测试集上也保持竞争力。此外，在杂乱且视觉模糊的真实场景中，基于SPARC标注训练的策略优于基线方法。

Conclusion: SPARC通过引入可靠性校准机制，有效解决了自动化标注管线中噪声标签与样本保留之间的权衡问题，在无需人工验证或标注训练数据的情况下，提升了机器人感知与策略学习的性能。

Abstract: This work introduces Spatial Annotations from Robot Demonstrations with Reliability Calibration (SPARC), a risk-aware framework that automatically labels robot demonstrations with structured spatial annotations and assigns each annotation a reliability score. Structured spatial annotations, such as bounding boxes, object trajectories, and manipulation phase labels, benefit a broad range of robotics applications from training grounded robot policies and embodied foundation models to motion planning and hierarchical task composition. Existing automated pipelines generate such annotations at scale but provide no reliable quality signal: detector confidence is poorly calibrated for annotation correctness, forcing a choice between accepting noisy labels or discarding useful samples. In contrast to existing automated pipelines, SPARC leverages the spatio-temporal structure inherent to robot tasks to generate a reliability signal, reducing noisy labels and retaining more useful samples. We further introduce Interaction-Aware Bench (IA-Bench), a benchmark that measures model accuracy in grounding the locations of interacted objects in robot demonstrations. On 1.7k human-annotated demonstrations spanning diverse embodiments and scenarios, SPARC significantly outperforms detection-only baselines in localization accuracy while retaining three times more samples at high-precision operating points. Our experiments demonstrate that models finetuned on our annotations achieve state-of-the-art results on object-grounding and pointing benchmarks among similarly sized models, while remaining competitive on broader spatial-reasoning suites without manually verified or annotated training data. Furthermore, policies trained on SPARC-generated annotations outperform baselines in cluttered, visually ambiguous real-world scenes. Code, data, and models are available at intuitive-robots.github.io/sparc-labeling.

</details>


### [171] [MCR-Bionic Hand: Anatomical Structural Priors for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13601)
*Haosen Yang,Guowu Wei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出“结构智能”框架，将人手灵巧性归因于骨骼韧带的物理架构（而非纯控制算法），并基于此开发了MCR-Bionic仿生手，通过1:1肌肉骨骼仿生实现低维姿态输入到高维抓取映射及接触后精细调节，实验证明解剖仿生学的价值在于复用人体结构中的控制功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统灵巧手研究侧重于自由度、驱动与算法等主动控制层面，而人手灵巧性部分编码在骨骼、韧带、肌腱等物理结构中，现有工作缺乏对这一结构性智能的系统建模与工程复现。

Method: 提出双层结构智能框架：(1) 结构先验生成——利用腕指联带运动、FDS/FDP肌腱路径、背侧伸肌腱帽等将低维姿态输入转换为默认抓取构型及PIP-DIP协调；(2) 肌肉介导调制——外肌、蚓状肌、骨间肌围绕默认状态调节MCP姿态、远端稳定性、指尖力路径与接触状态。基于此设计MCR-Bionic手，实现1:1肌肉骨骼仿生。

Result: 功能演示与几何力学模型表明：腕姿诱导多关节预塑形、伸肌腱帽将PIP姿态映射为耦合的DIP响应、内在肌通路调节远端稳定性与指尖力方向。硬币旋转、笔转移、手背翻硬币、方块操作等接触密集型任务验证了低维状态生成与接触后精细调节的耦合能力。

Conclusion: 解剖仿生学的价值不在于视觉相似性，而在于识别并复用人体手部结构中执行部分控制功能的物理机制，为灵巧手设计提供新的范式。

Abstract: Dexterous robotic hands are usually formulated as high dimensional active control systems governed by degrees of freedom, actuation, and algorithms. Human hand dexterity, however, is partly encoded in the physical architecture of bones, ligaments, tendons, aponeuroses, and intrinsic muscles. This work describes that contribution as two linked forms of structural intelligence: structural prior generation, in which wrist to finger tenodesis, FDS/FDP routing, and the dorsal extensor hood transform low dimensional posture inputs into default grasp configurations and PIP to DIP coordination; and muscle mediated modulation, in which extrinsic muscles, lumbricals, and interossei regulate MCP posture, distal stability, fingertip force paths, and contact states around that default state.
  Based on this framework, MCR-Bionic Hand is developed as a 1:1 musculoskeletal biomimetic hand integrating a two row eight bone wrist, cross wrist tendons, anatomical flexor routing, volar plate and collateral ligament constraints, the dorsal extensor hood, and intrinsic muscle pathways within one body. Functional demonstrations and geometric mechanical models show that wrist posture induces multi joint pre shaping, the extensor hood maps PIP posture to a coupled DIP response, and intrinsic plus pathways modulate distal stability and fingertip action direction after grasp formation. Contact rich tasks, including coin rotation, pen transfer, dorsal coin flipping, and cube manipulation, show that MCR-Bionic links low dimensional state generation with fine post contact modulation. These results suggest that anatomical biomimetics is valuable not for visual similarity, but for identifying human hand structures that perform part of control.

</details>


### [172] [$\texttt{WEAVER}$, Better, Faster, Longer: An Effective World Model for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13672)
*Arnav Kumar Jain,Yilin Wu,Jesse Farebrother,Gokul Swamy,Andrea Bajcsy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出WEAVER，一种多视角世界模型架构，通过流匹配损失预测未来潜变量和奖励值，同时满足保真度、一致性和效率三个要求，在机器人操作任务中实现了SOTA效果，在策略评估（ρ=0.870）、策略改进（成功率提升38%）和测试时规划（成功率提升14%，速度提升5-10倍）方面均表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型在机器人领域具有广泛应用前景（策略评估、策略改进、测试时规划），但现有方法难以同时满足保真度（仿真轨迹与真实世界相关）、一致性（长时域连贯性）和效率（快速生成）三个关键要求，限制了其在下游任务中的应用。

Method: WEAVER采用多视角世界模型架构，通过流匹配损失（flow-matching loss）训练模型预测未来潜变量和奖励值。论文提炼了在模型架构、记忆机制和预测目标方面的关键设计决策，以解决长时域动态操作任务中的难题。

Result: 在机器人硬件实验中，WEAVER在策略评估中达到ρ=0.870的相关性；在策略改进中使π_{0.5}基础模型的真实世界成功率提升38%；在测试时规划中实现14%的成功率提升，且速度相比先前世界模型提升5-10倍。在分布外场景中也优于先前方法。

Conclusion: WEAVER通过精心设计的架构和训练目标，首次同时实现了世界模型的保真度、一致性和效率三个关键要求，为世界模型在机器人操作任务中的实际应用提供了有效方案。

Abstract: The potential impacts of world models (WMs, i.e., learned simulators) on robotics are far-reaching -- policy evaluation, policy improvement, and test-time planning -- all with limited real-world interaction. To unlock these downstream capabilities, a WM needs to jointly satisfy three desiderata: $\textit{(i)}$ fidelity (i.e., producing simulated trajectories that correlate with reality), $\textit{(ii)}$ consistency (i.e., producing simulated trajectories that are coherent over long horizons), and $\textit{(iii)}$ efficiency (i.e., producing simulated trajectories quickly). We propose $\texttt{WEAVER}$ (World Estimation Across Views for Embodied Reasoning): a WM architecture that simultaneously achieves all three desiderata, providing state-of-the-art results on robotic manipulation tasks. $\texttt{WEAVER}$ is a multi-view WM trained to predict future latents and reward values via a flow-matching loss. We distill the key design decisions across model architecture, memory, and prediction objectives required to unlock the kinds of long-horizon dynamic manipulation tasks that have confounded prior world modeling approaches. We apply $\texttt{WEAVER}$ in robotic hardware, demonstrating its effectiveness at policy evaluation ($ρ$=0.870 correlation with real-world success rate), policy improvement (real-world success rate improvement of $38\%$ on top of the $π_{0.5}$ robot foundation model), and test-time planning (real-world success rate improvement of $14\%$ with a $5-10\times$ speedup over prior WMs). $\texttt{WEAVER}$ also demonstrates better performance than prior WMs when evaluated on out-of-distribution scenarios. Code, models, and videos at: https://arnavkj1995.github.io/WEAVER/ .

</details>


### [173] [Improving Robotic Generalist Policies via Flow Reversal Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13675)
*Andy Tang,William Chen,Andrew Wagenmaker,Chelsea Finn,Sergey Levine*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Flow Reversal Steering (FRS)方法，通过将次优动作反向映射到流匹配通用策略的潜在噪声空间，再映射到邻近的动作模式，从而提升通用策略在未见任务上的表现。FRS在模拟和真实操作任务中均有效，支持零样本控制、知识蒸馏和强化学习引导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用策略虽能从多样化数据集学习广泛技能，但在处理新颖或困难任务时，直接命令策略常常失败，需要一种方法从策略丰富的行为先验中推断并调用合适的动作。

Method: 提出Flow Reversal Steering (FRS)：首先获取次优但"合理"的动作，通过流策略反向传递找到其潜在噪声，然后将这些噪声映射到邻近的通用策略动作模式，从而获得更优的动作输出。

Result: FRS在多种模拟和真实操作场景中验证有效：可将人类或VLM的粗粒度语义指导转化为优质机器人动作；通过行为克隆训练辅助策略输出噪声，可在不到一分钟训练内实现高达95%的绝对任务成功率提升；能够引导强化学习在标准RL难以改进的任务上取得进展。

Conclusion: FRS是一种有效的通用策略增强方法，支持零样本控制提升、知识蒸馏以及强化学习引导，能够解决直接命令策略失败的问题。

Abstract: Generalist policies can learn a wide range of skills from diverse robot datasets. In order to solve or improve on challenging news tasks, we need a way to infer and invoke the appropriate actions from the policy's rich behavioral prior, especially when directly commanding the policy fails. We focus on flow matching generalists and propose Flow Reversal Steering (FRS): a method that takes suboptimal but ``reasonable'' actions, finds their latent noises by passing them through the flow policy in reverse, and maps them to nearby generalist action modes. We evaluate FRS across many simulated and real-world manipulation settings. First, FRS can turn coarse semantic guidance from humans or vision-language models (VLMs) into corresponding good robot actions, improving zero-shot control. These gains can be distilled with behavioral cloning by training an auxiliary policy to output noises that the generalist maps to good actions -- showing up to 95% absolute task success rate boosts in under a minute of training. Finally, FRS enables policy improvement by bootstrapping reinforcement learning with semantic knowledge, improving on several tasks that standard RL fails to improve on.

</details>


### [174] [Mana: Dexterous Manipulation of Articulated Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13677)
*Zhao-Heng Yin,Guanya Shi,Pieter Abbeel,C. Karen Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Mana框架，将灵巧操作重新解释为动画问题，通过粗到细的流水线实现从程序化生成抓取关键帧到操作轨迹的转化，在四种铰接工具上实现了零样本仿真到现实的迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铰接工具操作因需要协调内部自由度与接触交互而极具挑战性，现有工作多聚焦于刚体物体，铰接工具操作因其物理复杂性和功能抓取策略学习困难而研究不足。

Method: 受计算机动画启发，采用粗到细流水线：1) 程序化生成抓取关键帧；2) 通过运动规划和强化学习将关键帧转化为操作轨迹。数据生成过程高度自动化，仅需少量鼠标点击（每工具<1分钟）来指定功能可供性。

Result: 在四种不同尺度和关节类型的铰接工具上，实现了抓取和手中操作的零样本仿真到现实迁移。

Conclusion: 展示了灵巧铰接工具操作的可扩展方法，证明将操作问题重新解释为动画问题的有效性。

Abstract: Articulated tool manipulation remains a major challenge in dexterous robotics due to the need to coordinate internal degrees of freedom and contact-rich interactions. While prior work has largely focused on rigid objects, articulated tool use remains underexplored because of its physical complexity and the difficulty of learning functional grasping and manipulation policies. We present Mana (Manipulation Animator), a general sim-to-real framework that reinterprets dexterous manipulation as an animation problem. Inspired by computer animation, Mana employs a coarse-to-fine pipeline that transforms procedurally-generated grasp keyframes into manipulation trajectories through motion planning and reinforcement learning. The data generation process is largely automatic, requiring only a few mouse clicks to specify functional affordances (<1 minute per tool). Across four articulated tools spanning different scales and joint types, Mana achieves zero-shot sim-to-real transfer for both grasping and in-hand manipulation, demonstrating a scalable approach to dexterous articulated tool use.

</details>


### [175] [Learning Robot Safety from Sparse Human Feedback using Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.04823)
*Aaron O. Feldman,Joseph A. Vincent,Maximilian Adang,JunEn Low,Mac Schwager*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于共形预测和人类二元反馈的机器人安全保证方法，通过构建潜在状态空间中的不安全区域预警系统，以用户可指定的错误率检测策略故障，并用于策略改进。在四旋翼飞行器视觉运动控制任务中验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人安全保证具有挑战性：用户定义的约束可能遗漏边缘情况，即使从安全数据训练的策略也可能变得不安全，且安全性本身具有主观性。因此需要一种能够从人类反馈中学习安全偏好并提供统计保证的方法。

Method: 向人类展示策略轨迹并收集二元安全反馈；利用共形预测（conformal prediction）统计学方法，基于最近邻分类识别状态空间（或潜在空间）中的区域，该区域保证包含用户指定比例的未策略错误；构建预警系统在机器人到达疑似不安全区域时发出警报；通过避开该区域实现策略改进。

Result: 从视频标注中，系统能够检测四旋翼飞行器视觉运动策略何时无法通过指定门。通过避免疑似不安全区域改进了模型预测控制器的安全性，在30次四旋翼飞行实验（6个导航任务）中验证了效果。

Conclusion: 该方法样本高效（基于最近邻分类，避免了常见的留出数据做法），能够以可保证的漏报率模拟人类的安全偏好，并可用于策略改进以提升机器人安全性。

Abstract: Ensuring robot safety can be challenging; user-defined constraints can miss edge cases, policies can become unsafe even when trained from safe data, and safety can be subjective. Thus, we learn about robot safety by showing policy trajectories to a human who flags unsafe behavior. From this binary feedback, we use the statistical method of conformal prediction to identify a region of states, potentially in learned latent space, guaranteed to contain a user-specified fraction of future policy errors. Our method is sample-efficient, as it builds on nearest neighbor classification and avoids withholding data as is common with conformal prediction. By alerting if the robot reaches the suspected unsafe region, we obtain a warning system that mimics the human's safety preferences with guaranteed miss rate. From video labeling, our system can detect when a quadcopter visuomotor policy will fail to steer through a designated gate. We present an approach for policy improvement by avoiding the suspected unsafe region. With it we improve a model predictive controller's safety, as shown in experimental testing with 30 quadcopter flights across 6 navigation tasks. Code and videos are provided.

</details>


### [176] [Data-Driven Soft Robot Control via Adiabatic Spectral Submanifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.10919)
*Roshan S. Kaundinya,John Irvin Alora,Jonas G. Matt,Luis A. Pabon,Marco Pavone,George Haller*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于绝热谱子流形（aSSM）的数据驱动模型预测控制策略，用于解决软机器人在非线性大变形路径上的控制难题。利用软机器人过阻尼特性产生的低维吸引不变流形，该方法在多种任务中展现出优越性能，五/六维aSSM简化模型在闭环控制任务中的追踪性能优于其他数据驱动方法达10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软机器人的机械复杂性使得基于模型的控制面临重大挑战，线性数据驱动模型无法有效控制软机器人在空间扩展路径上探索具有显著非线性行为的区域。

Method: 基于绝热谱子流形（aSSM）理论，利用软机器人过阻尼特性（内部振动衰减远快于期望路径运动速度），从路径上提取低维吸引不变流形承载主导动力学，构建纯数据驱动的模型预测控制方案。

Result: 在软躯干机器人和Cosserat-rod弹性软臂的高保真有限元模型上验证，五/六维aSSM简化模型在所有闭环控制任务中的追踪性能优于其他数据驱动方法达10倍，且在实验噪声下仍保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: aSSM-based模型预测控制策略有效解决了软机器人在非线性大变形路径上的控制问题，低维简化模型显著提升了追踪精度和鲁棒性。

Abstract: The mechanical complexity of soft robots creates significant challenges for their model-based control. Specifically, linear data-driven models have struggled to control soft robots on complex, spatially extended paths that explore regions with significant nonlinear behavior. To account for these nonlinearities, we develop here a model-predictive control strategy based on the recent theory of adiabatic spectral submanifolds (aSSMs). This theory is applicable because the internal vibrations of heavily overdamped robots decay at a speed that is much faster than the desired speed of the robot along its intended path. In that case, low-dimensional attracting invariant manifolds (aSSMs) emanate from the path and carry the dominant dynamics of the robot. Aided by this recent theory, we devise an aSSM-based model-predictive control scheme purely from data. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our data-driven model in tracking dynamic trajectories across diverse tasks. We validate on high-fidelity, high-dimensional finite-element models of a soft trunk robot and Cosserat-rod-based elastic soft arms, with additional experiments confirming robust performance even in the presence of experimental noise. Notably, we find that five- or six-dimensional aSSM-reduced models outperform the tracking performance of other data-driven modeling methods by a factor up to 10 across all closed-loop control tasks.

</details>


### [177] [Adaptive Model-Predictive Control of a Soft Continuum Robot Using a Physics-Informed Neural Network Based on Cosserat Rod Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.12681)
*Johann Licher,Max Bartholdt,Henrik Krauss,Tim-Lukas Habich,Thomas Seel,Moritz Schappler*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于域解耦物理信息神经网络（DD-PINN）的实时非线性模型预测控制（MPC）框架，用于软连续体机器人（SCRs）的动态控制。DD-PINN作为Cosserat杆动力学模型的替代模型，加速比高达44,000倍，并结合无迹卡尔曼滤波器（UKF）估计模型状态和弯曲柔顺性。在GPU上以70 Hz运行的进化MPC实现了动态轨迹的精确跟踪，端效应器位置误差低于3 mm（作动器长度的2.3%），并在实际实验中达到了3.55 m/s²的加速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软连续体机器人的动态控制因精确动力学模型计算需求高而具有挑战性。现有数据驱动方法（如Koopman算子方法）缺乏适应性，且无法重构完整的机器人形状，限制了其应用。

Method: 提出域解耦物理信息神经网络（DD-PINN）作为动态Cosserat杆模型的替代模型，实现44,000倍加速；结合无迹卡尔曼滤波器（UKF）从端效应器位置测量中估计模型状态和弯曲柔顺性；在GPU上实现70 Hz的非线性进化MPC。

Result: 仿真中实现了动态轨迹的精确跟踪，端效应器位置误差低于3 mm（作动器长度的2.3%）；实际实验中达到类似精度和3.55 m/s²的加速度。

Conclusion: 所提框架实现了软连续体机器人的实时、精确动态控制，具备高加速能力和良好的适应性，有望扩展SCR的应用范围。

Abstract: Dynamic control of soft continuum robots (SCRs) holds great potential for expanding their applications, but remains a challenging problem due to the high computational demands of accurate dynamic models. While data-driven approaches like Koopman-operator-based methods have been proposed, they typically lack adaptability and cannot reconstruct the full robot shape, limiting their applicability. This work introduces a real-time-capable nonlinear model-predictive control (MPC) framework for SCRs based on a domain-decoupled physics-informed neural network (DD-PINN) with adaptable bending stiffness. The DD-PINN serves as a surrogate for the dynamic Cosserat rod model with a speed-up factor of up to 44,000. It is also used within an unscented Kalman filter for estimating the model states and bending compliance from end-effector position measurements. We implement a nonlinear evolutionary MPC running at 70 Hz on the GPU. In simulation, it demonstrates accurate tracking of dynamic trajectories and setpoint control with end-effector position errors below 3 mm (2.3\% of the actuator's length). In real-world experiments, the controller achieves similar accuracy and accelerations up to 3.55 m/s2.

</details>


### [178] [GLIDE: A Coordinated Aerial-Ground Framework for Search and Rescue in Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.14210)
*Seth Farrell,Chenghao Li,Henrik I. Christensen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为GLIDE的协作式空地搜索救援框架，利用两架无人机与一辆无人地面车辆协同工作，实现快速受害者定位和障碍物感知导航。无人机分别负责目标搜索和地形侦察，地面车辆融合空中与本地传感信息进行A*路径规划与重规划。硬件实验与仿真消融验证了该框架在提升到达时间和导航安全性方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在未知环境下实现快速受害者定位与安全导航，解决传统单一平台在搜索救援任务中感知范围有限、规划效率低的问题。

Method: 提出GLIDE框架：1) 目标搜索无人机负责实时受害者检测与地理定位；2) 地形侦察无人机在UGV规划路径前方飞行，提供中程可通行性更新；3) UGV融合空中线索与本地感知，进行时间高效的A*规划与连续重规划。

Result: 硬件演示与仿真消融实验表明，无人机间的明确角色分离结合地形侦察与引导规划，在时间关键的搜索救援任务中显著缩短了到达时间并提高了导航安全性。

Conclusion: 显式的无人机角色分工、地形侦察与引导式规划相结合，能够有效提升空地协同搜救系统的任务性能。
