Abstract: We present a cooperative aerial-ground search-and-rescue (SAR) framework that pairs two unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) with an unmanned ground vehicle (UGV) to achieve rapid victim localization and obstacle-aware navigation in unknown environments. We dub this framework Guided Long-horizon Integrated Drone Escort (GLIDE), highlighting the UGV's reliance on UAV guidance for long-horizon planning. In our framework, a goal-searching UAV executes real-time onboard victim detection and georeferencing to nominate goals for the ground platform, while a terrain-scouting UAV flies ahead of the UGV's planned route to provide mid-level traversability updates. The UGV fuses aerial cues with local sensing to perform time-efficient A* planning and continuous replanning as information arrives. Additionally, we present a hardware demonstration (using a GEM e6 golf cart as the UGV and two X500 UAVs) to evaluate end-to-end SAR mission performance and include simulation ablations to assess the planning stack in isolation from detection. Empirical results demonstrate that explicit role separation across UAVs, coupled with terrain scouting and guided planning, improves reach time and navigation safety in time-critical SAR missions.

</details>


### [179] [Active Semantic Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.05430)
*Huayi Tang,Pratik Chaudhari*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于多层场景图和大语言模型的主动语义感知方法，用于室内环境中的语义探索，能够在模拟和真实机器人上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在复杂室内环境的语义探索中缺乏高层空间推理能力，无法有效推理未观测区域的结构和语义信息（如"哪扇门通往厨房"），因此需要一种能够结合场景语义进行主动探索的方法。

Method: 构建紧凑的多层场景图（包含房间、物体、墙、窗等节点及几何细节）；利用大语言模型从未观测区域的部分观察中采样生成合理的场景图；设计基于场景图的信息增益计算方法，用于评估潜在路径点的探索价值，支持高层空间推理。

Result: 在3D室内公寓模拟环境和Unitree Go 2真实机器人上进行了评估，定性和定量分析表明该方法比现有方法更快、更准确地获取环境中的高层和低层语义信息。

Conclusion: 所提出的主动语义感知方法通过场景图和大语言模型的结合，显著提升了机器人对复杂室内环境的高层语义理解与探索效率。

Abstract: We develop an approach for active semantic perception, which refers to using the semantics of the scene for tasks such as exploration. We build a compact, multi-layer scene graph that can represent large, complex indoor environments at various levels of abstraction, e.g., nodes corresponding to rooms, objects, walls, windows etc., as well as fine-grained details of their geometry. We develop a procedure based on large language models (LLMs) to sample new plausible scene graphs of unobserved regions that are consistent with partial observations of the scene. We develop a procedure to compute the information gain of a potential waypoint upon this scene graph to enable sophisticated spatial reasoning: for example, of the two doors that lead out of the living room, one probably leads to the kitchen and the other to the bedroom. We evaluate our approach in realistic 3D indoor apartments in simulation and also on a Unitree Go 2 robot in the real world. Qualitative and quantitative analysis shows that our approach can pin down high-level and low-level semantic information in the environment quickly and more accurately than existing approaches.

</details>


### [180] [Miniature Testbed for Validating Multi-Agent Cooperative Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.11022)
*Hyunchul Bae,Eunjae Lee,Jehyeop Han,Minhee Kang,Jaehyeon Kim,Junggeun Seo,Minkyun Noh,Heejin Ahn*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了CIVAT，一个1:15比例的小型测试平台，用于验证协同自动驾驶，该平台集成了智能路侧基础设施（具备感知、边缘计算和通信能力）、自动驾驶车辆以及V2V/V2I通信，并通过基于基础设施的感知和交叉口管理实验进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试平台均未采用具备感知、边缘计算和通信能力的智能基础设施，无法满足协同自动驾驶中车-路-云实时协作的验证需求。

Method: 设计并实现了1:15比例的小型测试平台CIVAT，包含缩比城市地图、搭载车载传感器的自动驾驶车辆和智能路侧基础设施；通过共享Wi-Fi和ROS2框架，采用发布-订阅模式集成V2V和V2I通信，实现车与基础设施之间的信息交换。

Result: 通过基于基础设施的感知实验和交叉口管理实验，验证了CIVAT系统的有效性。

Conclusion: CIVAT测试平台填补了现有协同自动驾驶测试平台缺乏智能基础设施的空白，为协同驾驶功能验证提供了有效的小型化实验环境。

Abstract: Cooperative autonomous driving, which extends vehicle autonomy by enabling real-time collaboration between vehicles and smart roadside infrastructure, remains a challenging yet essential problem. However, none of the existing testbeds employ smart infrastructure equipped with sensing, edge computing, and communication capabilities. To address this gap, we design and implement a 1:15-scale miniature testbed, CIVAT, for validating cooperative autonomous driving, consisting of a scaled urban map, autonomous vehicles with onboard sensors, and smart infrastructure. The proposed testbed integrates V2V and V2I communication with the publish-subscribe pattern through a shared Wi-Fi and ROS2 framework, enabling information exchange between vehicles and infrastructure to realize cooperative driving functionality. As a case study, we validate the system through infrastructure-based perception and intersection management experiments.

</details>


### [181] [Learning Visually Interpretable Oscillator Networks for Soft Continuum Robots from Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.18322)
*Henrik Krauss,Johann Licher,Naoya Takeishi,Annika Raatz,Takehisa Yairi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Attention Broadcast Decoder (ABCD)和Visual Oscillator Networks (VONs)，从视频中学习软体连续机器人(SCR)动力学，实现可解释的潜空间动态建模，在双段SCR上将预测误差降低5.8倍（Koopman算子）和3.5倍（振荡器网络）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有从视频学习SCR动力学的方法要么缺乏可解释性，要么依赖先验假设和人工设计；基于模型的方法需要先验知识和手工设计，因此需要一种无需先验假设、兼具可解释性和预测准确性的完全数据驱动方法。

Method: (1) ABCD：即插即用的注意力广播解码器模块，用于自编码器潜动力学学习，生成像素级注意力图，定位每个潜变量的贡献并过滤静态背景，实现空间可解释的潜变量和图像叠加可视化。(2) VONs：2D潜振荡器网络，与ABCD注意力图耦合，在图像上可视化学习到的质量、耦合刚度和力，实现机械可解释性。

Result: 在单段和双段SCR上验证：ABCD模型显著提升多步预测精度，在双段机器人上Koopman算子误差降低5.8倍，振荡器网络误差降低3.5倍；VONs自主发现振荡器的链式结构。

Conclusion: 该完全数据驱动方法生成了紧凑且具有机械可解释性的模型，对未来控制应用具有潜在价值。

Abstract: Learning soft continuum robot (SCR) dynamics from video offers flexibility but existing methods lack interpretability or rely on prior assumptions. Model-based approaches require prior knowledge and manual design. We bridge this gap by introducing: (1) The Attention Broadcast Decoder (ABCD), a plug-and-play module for autoencoder-based latent dynamics learning that generates pixel-accurate attention maps localizing each latent dimension's contribution while filtering static backgrounds, enabling visual interpretability via spatially grounded latents and on-image overlays. (2) Visual Oscillator Networks (VONs), a 2D latent oscillator network coupled to ABCD attention maps for on-image visualization of learned masses, coupling stiffness, and forces, thereby enabling mechanical interpretability. We validate our approach on single- and double-segment SCRs, demonstrating that ABCD-based models significantly improve multi-step prediction accuracy with 5.8x error reduction for Koopman operators and 3.5x for oscillator networks on a two-segment robot. VONs autonomously discover a chain structure of oscillators. This fully data-driven approach yields compact, mechanically interpretable models with potential relevance for future control applications.

</details>


### [182] [DiskChunGS: Large-Scale 3D Gaussian SLAM Through Chunk-Based Memory Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.23030)
*Casimir Feldmann,Maximum Wilder-Smith,Vaishakh Patil,Michael Oechsle,Michael Niemeyer,Keisuke Tateno,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DiskChunGS提出了一种基于外存(out-of-core)的3D高斯溅射(3DGS) SLAM系统，通过将场景划分为空间块(chunks)，仅在GPU内存中保留活跃区域，非活跃区域存储于磁盘，从而突破GPU内存容量限制，实现大规模场景的可扩展3DGS SLAM重建。在Replica、TUM-RGBD、KITTI等数据集上验证了有效性，并成功完成所有11个KITTI序列，无内存故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS SLAM系统受限于GPU内存容量，只能重建小规模环境，无法扩展到大规模场景，存在根本的可扩展性瓶颈。

Method: 采用外存(out-of-core)方法，将场景划分为空间块(spatial chunks)，仅将活跃区域保留在GPU内存中，非活跃区域存储于磁盘。该架构可无缝集成现有SLAM框架进行位姿估计和闭环检测，实现全局一致的大规模重建。

Result: 在室内场景(Replica, TUM-RGBD)、城市驾驶场景(KITTI)以及资源受限的Nvidia Jetson平台上均验证了有效性。独特地完成了所有11个KITTI序列，无内存故障，并实现了优越的视觉质量。

Conclusion: 通过算法创新（外存空间块管理）克服了以往3DGS SLAM方法的内存限制，证明该方法能有效扩展3DGS SLAM到大规模环境。

Abstract: Recent advances in 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) have demonstrated impressive results for novel view synthesis with real-time rendering capabilities. However, integrating 3DGS with SLAM systems faces a fundamental scalability limitation: methods are constrained by GPU memory capacity, restricting reconstruction to small-scale environments. We present DiskChunGS, a scalable 3DGS SLAM system that overcomes this bottleneck through an out-of-core approach that partitions scenes into spatial chunks and maintains only active regions in GPU memory while storing inactive areas on disk. Our architecture integrates seamlessly with existing SLAM frameworks for pose estimation and loop closure, enabling globally consistent reconstruction at scale. We validate DiskChunGS on indoor scenes (Replica, TUM-RGBD), urban driving scenarios (KITTI), and resource-constrained Nvidia Jetson platforms. Our method uniquely completes all 11 KITTI sequences without memory failures while achieving superior visual quality, demonstrating that algorithmic innovation can overcome the memory constraints that have limited previous 3DGS SLAM methods.

</details>


### [183] [ReactEMG Stroke: Healthy-to-Stroke Few-shot Adaptation for sEMG-Based Intent Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22090)
*Runsheng Wang,Katelyn Lee,Xinyue Zhu,Lauren Winterbottom,Dawn M. Nilsen,Joel Stein,Matei Ciocarlie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种健康到中风的自适应迁移学习管道，利用大规模健康人群sEMG数据预训练模型，仅用少量受试者数据微调即可提升中风患者手部运动意图检测的准确性和鲁棒性。在3名慢性中风患者的实验中，最佳方法将过渡准确率从0.42提升至0.61，原始准确率从0.69提升至0.78。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表面肌电图（sEMG）是中风后手部康复的有前景控制信号，但从中风患者麻痹肌肉检测运动意图通常需要冗长、个体化的校准，且对信号变异性脆弱。现有方法校准负担重，难以实用化。

Method: 构建一个健康到中风的迁移学习管道：首先在大规模健康人群sEMG数据上预训练意图检测模型，然后仅用少量中风患者数据对模型进行微调。在3名慢性中风患者的新数据集上，比较了三种适应策略：仅头部调优、参数高效LoRA适配器以及全端到端微调，并在包含会话内漂移、姿势变化和臂带重新定位等实际分布偏移的测试集上评估。

Result: 与零样本迁移和仅使用中风数据训练相比，健康预训练适应策略在所有条件下均一致提升中风意图检测性能。最佳适应方法将平均过渡准确率从0.42提升至0.61，原始准确率从0.69提升至0.78。

Conclusion: 转移可复用的健康领域EMG表示可以减轻校准负担，同时提高实时中风后意图检测的鲁棒性，证明了该方法在临床康复中的潜力。

Abstract: Surface electromyography (sEMG) is a promising control signal for assist-as-needed hand rehabilitation after stroke, but detecting intent from paretic muscles often requires lengthy, subject-specific calibration and remains brittle to variability. We propose a healthy-to-stroke adaptation pipeline that initializes an intent detector from a model pretrained on large-scale able-bodied sEMG, then fine-tunes it for each stroke participant using only a small amount of subject-specific data. Using a newly collected dataset from three individuals with chronic stroke, we compare adaptation strategies (head-only tuning, parameter-efficient LoRA adapters, and full end-to-end fine-tuning) and evaluate on held-out test sets that include realistic distribution shifts such as within-session drift, posture changes, and armband repositioning. Across conditions, healthy-pretrained adaptation consistently improves stroke intent detection relative to both zero-shot transfer and stroke-only training under the same data budget; the best adaptation methods improve average transition accuracy from 0.42 to 0.61 and raw accuracy from 0.69 to 0.78. These results suggest that transferring a reusable healthy-domain EMG representation can reduce calibration burden while improving robustness for real-time post-stroke intent detection. Our project website, video, code, and dataset are available at: https://roamlab.github.io/reactemg-stroke/.

</details>


### [184] [Extending the Law of Intersegmental Coordination: Implications for Powered Prosthetic Controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.02181)
*Elad Siman Tov,Nili E. Krausz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文开发了分析下肢截肢者步态中节段间协调性（ISC）的方法，并提出了“抬升空间力矩”（ESM）作为新的协调定律。基于ISC约束设计了主动假肢控制框架，并发布了开源ISC3d工具箱。研究发现，主动假肢虽能维持抬升角的平面协调，但力矩协调性仍存在缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低截肢者步行代谢能耗仍是开放问题。节段间协调性（ISC）已被观察到与步态能量消耗相关，但鲜少在截肢者步态中分析或应用。作者旨在将ISC分析扩展到下肢3D运动学数据，并探索其用于假肢控制的可能性。

Method: 1）开发了基于3D运动学数据的ISC分析方法；2）将ISC从运动学扩展到力矩域，提出抬升空间力矩（ESM）；3）分析健全步态、被动与主动假肢截肢者步态的ISC特征；4）基于健康ISC约束设计主动假肢控制框架，预测小腿角度/力矩以补偿被动足部造成的代偿；5）发布开源ISC3d工具箱。

Result: 健全步态中抬升角和力矩均表现出平面协调性；截肢者步态中抬升角仍保持平面性，但ESM缺乏平面协调；基于ISC约束的主动假肢控制框架可预测小腿运动/力矩。

Conclusion: ISC可扩展至力矩域（ESM），形成新的协调定律；ISC驱动的控制框架有潜力改善假肢步态协调性；ISC3d工具箱为深入研究步态协调机制及神经控制提供了工具。

Abstract: Powered prostheses are capable of providing net positive work to amputees and have advanced in the past two decades. However, reducing amputee metabolic cost of walking remains an open problem. The Law of Intersegmental Coordination (ISC) has been observed across gaits and previously implicated in energy expenditure of walking, yet it has rarely been analyzed or applied within the context of lower-limb amputee gait. This law states that the elevation angles of the thigh, shank and foot over the gait cycle covary. In this work, we developed a method to analyze intersegmental coordination for lower-limb 3D kinematic data, to simplify ISC analysis. Moreover, inspired by motor control, biomechanics and robotics literature, we used our method to extend ISC to a new law of coordination of moments. We find these Elevation Space Moments (ESM), and present results showing a moment-based coordination for able bodied gait. We also analyzed ISC for amputee gait with powered and passive prostheses, and found that while elevation angles remained planar, the ESM lacked planar coordination. We present an ISC-driven powered prosthetic control framework, using healthy coordination as a constraint to predict the shank angles/moments to compensate for alterations due to a passive foot. We developed the ISC3d toolbox that is freely available online, which may be used to compute kinematic and kinetic ISC in 3D. This provides a means to further study the role of coordination in gait and may help address fundamental questions of the neural control of human movement.

</details>


### [185] [SCALE: Self-uncertainty Conditioned Adaptive Looking and Execution for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.04208)
*Hyeonbeom Choi,Daechul Ahn,Youhan Lee,Taewook Kang,Seongwon Cho,Jonghyun Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SCALE，一种基于自不确定性的单次前向推理策略，无需额外训练或验证器，通过联合调制视觉感知和动作来提升VLA模型在感知模糊场景下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA的测试时扩展方法需额外训练、验证器和多次前向传播，且仅干预动作解码而忽略视觉感知重构，无法应对感知模糊场景。

Method: 基于主动推理中的不确定性驱动探索理论，提出SCALE策略：在高不确定性时同时扩大感知和动作的探索空间，在低置信度时聚焦利用，仅需单次前向传播。

Result: 在模拟和真实世界基准测试中，SCALE提升了最先进VLA的性能，并超越现有TTS方法，同时保持单次前向传播的高效性。

Conclusion: SCALE通过联合调制感知与动作的推理策略，为VLA模型提供了一种轻量级、高效的测试时鲁棒性提升方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a promising paradigm for general-purpose robotic control, with test-time scaling (TTS) gaining attention to enhance robustness beyond training. However, existing TTS methods for VLAs require additional training, verifiers, and multiple forward passes, making them impractical for deployment. Moreover, they intervene only at action decoding while keeping visual representations fixed-insufficient under perceptual ambiguity, where reconsidering how to perceive is as important as deciding what to do. To address these limitations, we propose SCALE, a simple inference strategy that jointly modulates visual perception and action based on 'self-uncertainty', inspired by uncertainty-driven exploration in Active Inference theory-requiring no additional training, no verifier, and only a single forward pass. SCALE broadens exploration in both perception and action under high uncertainty, while focusing on exploitation when confident-enabling adaptive execution across varying conditions. Experiments on simulated and real-world benchmarks demonstrate that SCALE improves state-of-the-art VLAs and outperforms existing TTS methods while maintaining single-pass efficiency.

</details>


### [186] [Adaptive-Horizon Conflict-Based Search for Closed-Loop Multi-Agent Path Finding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.12024)
*Jiarui Li,Federico Pecora,Runyu Zhang,Gioele Zardini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ACCBS算法，结合有限时域CBS和迭代加深MPC思想，通过动态调整规划时域和复用约束树，实现闭环、渐近最优且抗干扰的大规模机器人协调方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MAPF方法要么是开环规划器（固定轨迹，难以处理扰动），要么是闭环启发式方法（缺乏可靠性能保证），限制了在安全关键场景中的部署。需要一种兼具理论最优性和实际鲁棒性的闭环方法。

Method: 基于有限时域CBS变体，引入类似MPC中迭代加深的时域变化机制。动态根据可用计算预算调整规划时域，复用单一约束树实现时域间的无缝切换，展现出任意时间行为。

Result: 能够快速生成高质量可行解，随预算增加渐近最优；案例研究表明该方法兼具抗干扰灵活性和强性能保证。

Conclusion: 有效弥合了理论最优性与实际鲁棒性之间的差距，适用于大规模机器人安全关键部署。

Abstract: MAPF is a core coordination problem for large robot fleets in automated warehouses and logistics. Existing approaches are typically either open-loop planners, which generate fixed trajectories and struggle to handle disturbances, or closed-loop heuristics without reliable performance guarantees, limiting their use in safety-critical deployments. This paper presents ACCBS, a closed-loop algorithm built on a finite-horizon variant of CBS with a horizon-changing mechanism inspired by iterative deepening in MPC. ACCBS dynamically adjusts the planning horizon based on the available computational budget, and reuses a single constraint tree to enable seamless transitions between horizons. As a result, it produces high-quality feasible solutions quickly while being asymptotically optimal as the budget increases, exhibiting anytime behavior. Extensive case studies demonstrate that ACCBS combines flexibility to disturbances with strong performance guarantees, effectively bridging the gap between theoretical optimality and practical robustness for large-scale robot deployment.

</details>


### [187] [Lyapunov-Based PI-Like Control for Robust Trajectory Tracking of a Four-Wheel Independently Driven and Steered Robot: Design and Experimental Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.15424)
*Branimir Ćaran,Vladimir Milić,Marko Švaco,Bojan Jerbić*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对四轮独立驱动独立转向移动机器人，提出了一种基于李雅普诺夫方法的类PI鲁棒轨迹跟踪控制器，通过增广李雅普诺夫稳定性分析获得实用稳定性和一致最终有界性的显式条件，并在实验平台上验证了其优于传统PI和滑模控制器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四轮独立驱动独立转向移动机器人存在构型依赖的残余动力学和未建模效应，需要一种兼具严格稳定性保证和实际可实现的鲁棒轨迹跟踪控制器，以弥补传统PI控制器鲁棒性不足和滑模控制器实现复杂度高的问题。

Method: 采用显式结构验证的数学模型实现系统化控制器设计；开发增广李雅普诺夫实用稳定性分析方法，联合处理速度误差和积分误差动力学；导出实用稳定性和一致最终有界性的显式界值与充分条件；控制律保持类PI结构并加入基于模型的前馈补偿。

Result: 在四轮独立驱动独立转向移动机器人平台上进行了水平与垂直工况下的实验验证，与传统PI控制器和滑模控制器进行了基准对比，实验结果表明所提方法在轨迹跟踪精度和鲁棒性方面具有优势。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于李雅普诺夫方法的类PI控制器兼具严格稳定性保证和实际可实现性，在标准嵌入式平台上易于实现，对构型依赖的残余动力学和未建模效应具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: In this paper, a Lyapunov-based synthesis of a PI-like controller is proposed for robust trajectory tracking of an independently driven and steered four-wheel mobile robot. For the robot considered in this work, an explicit structurally verified mathematical model is used to enable systematic controller design with rigorous stability guarantees suitable for real time implementation. An augmented Lyapunov-based practical stability analysis is developed for the combined velocity-error and integral-error dynamics of the inner loop, yielding explicit bounds and sufficient conditions for practical stability and uniform ultimate boundedness of the combined velocity-error and integral-error state. The resulting control law retains a PI-like structure with model-based feedforward compensation, making it suitable for implementation on standard embedded platforms while improving robustness against configuration dependent residual dynamics and unmodelled effects. The effectiveness and robustness of the proposed design are demonstrated experimentally on a four-wheel independently steered and independently driven mobile robot platform, under both horizontal and vertical operating conditions and benchmarked against a PI controller and a sliding-mode controller.

</details>


### [188] [EgoMoD: Predicting Global Maps of Dynamics from Local Egocentric Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00167)
*Iacopo Catalano,David Morilla-Cabello,Jorge Pena-Queralta,Eduardo Montijano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出EgoMoD，首个从机器人短时自我中心视频片段中学习预测全局运动动力学图（Maps of Dynamics）的方法，无需外部全局观测，即可实现动态环境中的预判性导航规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Maps of Dynamics（MoDs）的构建需要长时间全局环境观测，依赖外部全局感知基础设施，难以在实际机器人应用中部署。现有方法仅能在机器人视野内扩展过去的运动模式，无法预判全局运动倾向，限制了动态场景下的预判性导航能力。

Method: 提出视频与位姿条件化的架构EgoMoD，以外部观测计算的MoDs作为特权监督信号，学习从短时自我中心视频片段中推断环境级别的运动倾向。利用局部动态线索作为全局运动结构的预测信号，实现从局部观测到全局运动动力学的映射。

Result: 在大规模仿真环境中，EgoMoD能在有限观测条件下准确预测未来MoDs；在真实图像上展示了零样本迁移能力，验证了其可部署于真实系统。

Conclusion: EgoMoD用标准机载传感器替代了传统MoD方法所需的外部全局感知基础设施，实现了从局部自我中心观测到全局未来运动动力学的预测，为动态环境中的预判性导航提供了可行且可迁移的解决方案。

Abstract: Efficient navigation in dynamic environments requires anticipating how motion patterns evolve beyond the robot's immediate perceptual range, enabling preemptive rather than purely reactive planning in crowded scenes. Maps of Dynamics (MoDs) offer a structured representation of motion tendencies in space useful for long-term global planning, but constructing them traditionally requires global environment observations over extended periods of time. We introduce EgoMoD, the first approach that learns to predict future MoDs directly from short egocentric video clips collected during robot operation. Our method learns to infer environment-wide motion tendencies from local dynamic cues using a video- and pose-conditioned architecture trained with MoDs computed from external observations as privileged supervision, allowing local observations to serve as predictive signals of global motion structure. Thanks to this, we offer the capacity to forecast future motion dynamics over the whole environment rather than merely extend past patterns in the robot's field of view. As a site-specific dynamic prior, EgoMoD replaces the external global sensing infrastructure required by prior MoD methods at inference time with standard onboard sensors. Experiments in large simulated environments show that EgoMoD predicts future MoDs under limited observability, while evaluation with real images showcases its zero-shot transferability to real systems.

</details>


### [189] [PROBE: Probabilistic Occupancy BEV Encoding with Analytical Translation Robustness for 3D Place Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05965)
*Jinseop Lee,Byoungho Lee,Gichul Yoo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PROBE是一种无学习的LiDAR地点识别描述子，将每个BEV网格的占用建模为伯努利随机变量，通过极坐标雅可比矩阵解析地边缘化连续笛卡尔平移，在O(R·S)时间内实现距离自适应的角度不确定性。在四个数据集上达到手工描述子中最高的多会话精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LiDAR地点识别方法依赖数据增广或学习范式，难以实现跨传感器泛化且需要大量调参。作者希望设计一种无学习、物理可解释的描述子，显式建模点云中的空间不确定性，实现跨传感器鲁棒性和良好泛化。

Method: 1) 将BEV每个单元的占用建模为伯努利随机变量；2) 通过极坐标雅可比矩阵解析地边缘化连续笛卡尔平移，推导出距离自适应的角度不确定性σ_θ=σ_t/r，计算复杂度O(R·S)；3) 主参数σ_t为期望平移不确定性（单位为米），是传感器无关的物理量；4) 相似度度量结合了伯努利KL Jaccard距离、指数不确定性门控以及基于FFT的高度余弦相似度用于旋转对齐。

Result: 在四个涵盖四种不同LiDAR类型的数据集上，PROBE在多会话评估中达到所有手工描述子中的最高精度，在单会话评估中与有监督基线及手工描述子相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: PROBE通过概率化建模和解析边缘化，实现了无学习、跨传感器泛化能力强的LiDAR地点识别描述子，减少了数据集调参需求，在多种传感器设置下均表现优异。

Abstract: We present PROBE (PRobabilistic Occupancy BEV Encoding), a learning-free LiDAR place recognition descriptor that models each BEV cell's occupancy as a Bernoulli random variable. Rather than relying on discrete point-cloud perturbations, PROBE analytically marginalizes over continuous Cartesian translations via the polar Jacobian, yielding a distance-adaptive angular uncertainty $σ_θ= σ_t / r$ in $\mathcal{O}(R{\cdot}S)$ time. The primary parameter $σ_t$ represents the expected translational uncertainty in meters, a sensor-independent physical quantity that enhances cross-sensor generalization while reducing the need for extensive per-dataset tuning. Pairwise similarity combines a Bernoulli-KL Jaccard with exponential uncertainty gating and FFT-based height cosine similarity for rotation alignment. Evaluated on four datasets spanning four diverse LiDAR types, PROBE achieves the highest accuracy among handcrafted descriptors in multi-session evaluation and competitive single-session performance relative to both handcrafted and supervised baselines. The source code and supplementary materials are available at https://sites.google.com/view/probe-pr.

</details>


### [190] [Safety Case Patterns for VLA-based driving systems: Insights from SimLingo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16013)
*Gerhard Yu,Fuyuki Ishikawa,Oluwafemi Odu,Alvine Boaye Belle*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RAISE方法，为基于VLA的自动驾驶系统设计安全案例，通过扩展HARA分析和定制化模式，解决指令驱动系统特有的安全风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA自动驾驶系统虽能结合视觉理解、语言解释和动作生成，实现更灵活、自适应的驾驶行为，但其多模态控制回路中集成开放自然语言输入可能导致不可预测的不安全行为，威胁乘员和行人安全，亟需系统化的安全保障方法。

Method: 提出RAISE方法，包括：(1)针对指令驱动驾驶系统（如VLA）的新安全案例模式；(2)扩展HARA分析，详细说明安全场景及其结果；(3)创建VLA系统安全案例的设计技术。

Result: 通过SimLingo案例研究表明，RAISE方法可用于构建基于证据的严谨安全声明，支持VLA自动驾驶系统的安全保证。

Conclusion: RAISE为VLA自动驾驶系统的安全保证提供系统化方法，有助于建立对该类新兴系统的信任。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA)-based driving systems represent a significant paradigm shift in autonomous driving since, by combining traffic scene understanding, linguistic interpretation, and action generation, these systems enable more flexible, adaptive, and instruction-responsive driving behaviors. However, despite their growing adoption and potential to support socially responsible autonomous driving as well as understanding high-level human instructions, VLA-based driving systems may exhibit new types of hazardous behaviors. For instance, the integration of open-ended natural language inputs (e.g., user or navigation instructions) into the multimodal control loop may lead to unpredictable and unsafe behaviors that could endanger vehicle occupants and pedestrians. Hence, assuring the safety of these systems is crucial to help build trust in their operations. To support this, we propose a novel safety case design approach called RAISE. Our approach introduces novel patterns tailored to instruction-based driving systems such as VLA-based driving systems, an extension of Hazard Analysis and Risk Assessment (HARA) detailing safe scenarios and their outcomes, and a design technique to create the safety cases of VLA-based driving systems. A case study on SimLingo illustrates how our approach can be used to construct rigorous, evidence-based safety claims for this emerging class of autonomous driving systems.

</details>


### [191] [AssemLM: A Spatial Reasoning Multimodal Large Language Model for Robotic Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08983)
*Zhi Jing,Jinbin Qiao,Ouyang Lu,Jicong Ao,Shuang Qiu,Huazhe Xu,Yu-Gang Jiang,Chenjia Bai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AssemLM是一个面向机器人装配的空间多模态大语言模型，通过整合装配手册、点云和文本指令，实现了具有显式几何理解的6D装配姿态预测；同时提出了包含90万+多模态样本和精确6D姿态标注的大规模基准AssemBench，在真实机器人评估中达到了最先进的6D姿态推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉语言模型的方法依赖粗粒度的2D感知，难以对复杂3D几何结构进行精确推理，限制了细粒度操作任务（如机器人装配）中的空间推理能力。

Method: 提出AssemLM，集成装配手册、点云和文本指令，采用专用点云编码器提取细粒度几何与旋转特征，实现准确的3D空间推理；并构建AssemBench基准（90万+多模态样本，精确6D姿态标注）。

Result: 在6D姿态推理任务上达到最先进性能，真实机器人实验表明其能有效支持细粒度、多步骤的装配任务。

Conclusion: 融合3D几何感知与语言推理的AssemLM显著提升了机器人装配中的空间推理能力，填补了从2D定位到完整3D几何推理的评估空白，代码、模型和数据集将开源。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning is a fundamental capability for embodied intelligence, especially for fine-grained manipulation tasks such as robotic assembly. Recent methods based on vision-language models (VLMs) largely rely on coarse 2D perception and struggle to perform accurate reasoning over complex 3D geometry. To address this limitation, we propose AssemLM, a spatial multimodal large language model for robotic assembly that integrates assembly manuals, point clouds, and textual instructions to predict task-critical 6D assembly poses with explicit geometric understanding. To bridge raw 3D perception and high-level linguistic reasoning, AssemLM employs a specialized point cloud encoder to extract fine-grained geometric and rotational features for accurate 3D spatial reasoning in assembly tasks. In addition, we introduce AssemBench, a large-scale benchmark for assembly-oriented spatial reasoning with over 900K multimodal samples and precise 6D pose annotations, extending evaluation from 2D grounding to full 3D geometric inference. Extensive experiments and real-robot evaluations demonstrate that AssemLM achieves state-of-the-art 6D pose reasoning performance and effectively supports fine-grained, multi-step assembly tasks in real-world settings. Code, models, and the AssemBench dataset will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [192] [Lexicographic Minimum-Violation Motion Planning using Signal Temporal Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20428)
*Patrick Halder,Lothar Kiltz,Hannes Homburger,Johannes Reuter,Matthias Althoff*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将词典式STL最小违规运动规划转化为单目标标量优化问题的方法，通过非均匀量化和位移技术扩展了确定性MPPI求解器，并引入了一种结合时空违规的新型谓词鲁棒性度量，实现了可解释且可扩展的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶运动规划中常需满足多个有条件冲突的规约，当无法同时满足所有规约时，需要最小违规运动规划。然而，规约的全序化导致词典式优化问题，使用标准方法求解计算成本高昂。

Method: 将多目标词典式优化问题通过非均匀量化和位移技术转化为单目标标量优化问题；扩展确定性模型预测路径积分（MPPI）求解器以高效求解无二次输入成本的优化问题；引入一种结合空间和时间违规的新型谓词鲁棒性度量。

Result: 所提方法在单目标求解器框架内为词典式STL最小违规运动规划提供了可解释且可扩展的解决方案。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了词典式优化计算成本高的问题，在保持可解释性的同时实现了可扩展性，为自动驾驶中多规约冲突下的运动规划提供了一种实用方案。

Abstract: Motion planning for autonomous vehicles often requires satisfying multiple conditionally conflicting specifications. In situations where not all specifications can be met simultaneously, minimum-violation motion planning maintains system operation by minimizing violations of specifications in accordance with their priorities. Signal temporal logic (STL) provides a formal language for rigorously defining these specifications and enables the quantitative evaluation of their violations. However, a total ordering of specifications yields a lexicographic optimization problem, which is typically computationally expensive to solve using standard methods. We address this problem by transforming the multi-objective lexicographic optimization problem into a single-objective scalar optimization problem using non-uniform quantization and bit-shifting. Specifically, we extend a deterministic model predictive path integral (MPPI) solver to efficiently solve optimization problems without quadratic input cost. Additionally, a novel predicate-robustness measure that combines spatial and temporal violations is introduced. Our results show that the proposed method offers an interpretable and scalable solution for lexicographic STL minimum-violation motion planning within a single-objective solver framework.

</details>


### [193] [RGB-S: Image-Aligned Tactile Saliency for Robust Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08765)
*Shengcheng Luo,Kefei Wu,Xiaoying Zhou,Wanlin Li,Ziyuan Jiao,Chenxi Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种显式将触觉信号投影到RGB图像平面并渲染力调制高斯显著性图的方法，通过零初始化的条件注入架构将物理接触先验融入视觉骨干网络，在严重视觉遮挡下显著提升了灵巧操作的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视触觉融合方法通常依赖有限演示隐式学习跨模态对应关系，缺乏几何先验，导致数据效率低且在视觉观测退化时泛化能力差。

Method: 利用机器人正向运动学和相机标定将触觉传感器位置投影到RGB图像平面，渲染力调制高斯显著性图以建模空间不确定性，通过零初始化条件注入架构将2D空间锚点整合进标准视觉骨干网络，同时保留预训练的视觉表征。

Result: 在严重视觉遮挡下的真实世界灵巧操作任务中，相比最强隐式视触觉基线方法，成功率提升了26.7个百分点。

Conclusion: 显式的RGB-S触觉空间接地在图像域中显著提升了空间推理能力和对遮挡的鲁棒性，验证了物理接触先验在视触觉融合中的有效性。

Abstract: Effective visuo-tactile integration is critical for robotic dexterous manipulation, especially when visual observations are unreliable or occluded. However, robustly aligning sparse, heterogeneous tactile measurements with dense visual representations remains a fundamental challenge. Most existing approaches require policies to learn cross-modal correspondences implicitly from limited demonstrations, without leveraging geometric priors. As a result, they are often data-inefficient and generalize poorly when visual observations are degraded. To address this limitation, we propose a framework that explicitly grounds physical contacts in the image domain. Using robot forward kinematics and camera calibration, we project tactile sensor locations directly onto the RGB image plane. We then render force-modulated Gaussian saliency maps to model spatial uncertainty arising from kinematic and calibration errors. By integrating these 2D spatial anchors through a zero-initialized conditioning architecture, our method injects physical contact priors into standard visual backbones while preserving pre-trained visual representations. We evaluate our method on six dexterous manipulation tasks in both simulation and the real world under severe visual occlusions. Real-world experiments show that explicit RGB-S grounding in the image domain improves real-world occluded manipulation success rates by $26.7$ percentage points over the strongest implicit visuo-tactile baseline, suggesting its improved spatial reasoning and robustness to occlusion. Project page: touch-as-saliency.github.io

</details>


### [194] [UniDexTok: A Unified Dexterous Hand Tokenizer from Real Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10683)
*Dong Fang,Youjun Wu,Yuanxin Zhong,Rui Zhang,Yunlong Wang,Xiaosong Jia,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出统一灵巧手模型(UDHM)和基于该模型的UniDexTok状态分词器，将人类和机器人手部状态映射到共享的22自由度语义接口，实现异构灵巧手的统一表示，在重建精度上实现从厘米级到亚毫米级的突破（MPJAE降低98.98%，MPJPE降低99.03%），并展现出跨本体迁移和少样本学习能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手在精细操作中至关重要，但不同硬件设计（运动学、关节定义、自由度差异）导致状态表示不统一，使得灵巧手数据碎片化，难以进行联合训练。

Method: 提出UDHM将人/机器人手状态映射到22自由度语义接口；基于此设计UniDexTok，一种无需重定向的状态分词器，学习基于本体条件的离散token，直接从标准化真实关节状态中生成统一表示。

Result: 相比UniHM基线，MPJAE从15.63°降至0.16°（降98.98%），MPJPE从18.51mm降至0.18mm（降99.03%），重建精度从厘米级提升至亚毫米级；跨本体数据能提升目标本体重建精度；展现出强大的零样本和少样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: UniDexTok有效解决了异构灵巧手数据碎片化问题，实现了高精度统一表示，验证了跨本体tokenization的收益，为灵巧手数据的联合训练和迁移学习提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Dexterous hands are essential for fine-grained manipulation, but their hardware designs vary substantially across embodiments. Differences in kinematics, joint definitions, and degrees of freedom make it difficult to define a shared state representation compared with parallel grippers. As a result, dexterous-hand data remains fragmented and difficult to use for joint training. In this work, we propose the Unified Dexterous Hand Model (UDHM), which maps human and robot hand states into a shared 22-DoF semantic interface. Based on UDHM, we introduce UniDexTok, a retargeting-free state tokenizer that learns embodiment-conditioned discrete tokens from standardized real joint states. UniDexTok provides a unified representation for heterogeneous dexterous hands without relying on retargeting or simulation data. Compared with the recent baseline UniHM, UniDexTok reduces MPJAE from 15.63 degrees to 0.16 degrees and MPJPE from 18.51 mm to 0.18 mm, corresponding to error reductions of 98.98% and 99.03%, respectively. These results improve reconstruction from centimeter-scale to sub-millimeter accuracy. Experiments further show that data from other embodiments improves target-embodiment reconstruction accuracy, demonstrating the benefit of cross-embodiment tokenization. UniDexTok also shows strong zero-shot and few-shot reconstruction ability when new dexterous hands are introduced.

</details>


### [195] [RoboNaldo: Accurate, Stable and Powerful Humanoid Soccer Shooting via Motion-Guided Curriculum Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11092)
*Yichao Zhong,Yidan Lu,Yuhang Lu,Tianyang Tang,Haoguang Mai,Yixuan Pan,Tianyu Li,Li Chen,Jingbo Wang,Zhongyu Li,Peng Lu,Hongyang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboNaldo 是一个三阶段课程强化学习框架，利用单一人体踢球参考运动，逐步优化人形机器人的高冲击力射门能力，涵盖固定球、任意球和运动球三种场景。在仿真中射门误差降低48.6%、速度提升2.96倍；在真实Unitree G1机器人上，3米距离平均射门误差为0.73米（任意球）和0.86米（运动球），触球后速度达13.10 m/s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在两难：基于运动跟踪的强化学习虽能保证全身协调稳定性，但固定参考轨迹难以适应不同球位和击球时机；基于任务奖励的强化学习则难以从零探索出有效的高强度踢球动作。因此需要一种既能利用参考运动保持稳定性、又能灵活适应不同球况的高冲击人形机器人射门框架。

Method: 提出三阶段运动引导课程强化学习框架：阶段一学习稳定的全身踢球先验（基于单一人体踢球参考）；阶段二将踢球策略适应到任意球场景（球静止于随机位置）；阶段三扩展到运动球射门（通过移动指令和踢球触发接口实现）。训练时高层启发式规划器控制接口，推理时可用其他高层控制器驱动同一低层策略。

Result: 仿真中任意球射门误差降低48.6%，射门速度提升2.96倍。真实Unitree G1机器人上，3米距离任意球平均误差0.73米，运动球平均误差0.86米，触球后球速达13.10 m/s，达到专业球员运动战射门速度的59-71%。

Conclusion: RoboNaldo通过运动引导的课程强化学习有效解决了人形机器人高冲击射门中稳定性与灵活性的矛盾，在仿真和真实环境中均显著优于基线方法，展示了从单一参考运动泛化到多种球况的强大能力，且球速接近专业人类水平。

Abstract: Elite humanoid soccer shooting requires whole-body stability, high-impulse whole-body interactions, and accuracy to targets. Motion tracking-driven reinforcement learning (RL) provides stability in whole-body movement coordination, but a fixed reference makes it hard to adapt to varied ball positions and strike timings; in contrast, task reward-driven RL struggles to explore and discover valid kicks from scratch. We therefore introduce RoboNaldo, a three-stage motion-guided curriculum RL framework for high-impulse humanoid interaction. A single human-kick reference is used as a scaffold and progressively shifts optimization towards shooting performance. The curriculum first learns a stable whole-body kicking prior, then adapts the kick to free-kick settings where the ball is stationary at random positions, and finally extends it to moving-ball shooting through a locomotion-command and kick-trigger interface. A high-level heuristic planner controls this interface during training, while alternative high-level controllers can drive the same low-level policy at inference. In simulation, RoboNaldo demonstrates free-kick shot error 48.6% lower and shoot velocity 2.96x than prior work baselines. In real world on a Unitree G1 with onboard perception, RoboNaldo attains 0.73 m and 0.86 m average target shooting error from 3 m away in free-kick and moving-ball cases, accordingly. And the post-contact ball velocity reaches 13.10 m/s, which is 59-71% of reported professional open-play shot speed. Project page: https://opendrivelab.com/RoboNaldo.

</details>


### [196] [Blind Dexterous Grasping via Real2Sim2Real Tactile Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11767)
*Shengcheng Luo,Xiyan Huang,Zhe Xu,Wanlin Li,Ziyuan Jiao,Chenxi Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合Real2Sim触觉校准、布局感知触觉编码器和扩散策略聚合的框架，实现了在真实多指机器人手上仅依赖触觉的盲抓取，在20个物体上达到27%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手盲抓取是关键的操控能力，但由于触觉的仿真到真实差距（sim-to-real gap）以及稀疏触觉信号的表达力有限，在真实机器人上学习仅依赖触觉的策略仍然具有挑战性。

Method: 1) Real2Sim触觉校准流水线，构建接触校准的数字孪生模拟器，可复现真实触觉信号；2) 布局感知触觉编码器，通过自监督预训练融入传感器几何先验，增强稀疏触觉观测的表达力；3) 在校准模拟器中训练物体特定的强化学习专家，将其成功抓取轨迹聚合为触觉条件扩散策略。

Result: 在配备分布式触觉传感的LEAP Hand上，对10个见过和10个未见过的物体实现了27%的真实世界抓取成功率。仿真消融实验表明布局感知触觉预训练提升了抓取性能，传感层面评估证实Real2Sim校准提高了触觉接触事件在仿真与硬件之间的一致性。

Conclusion: 接触事件校准、几何感知触觉表征学习和基于扩散的策略聚合为在真实灵巧机器人手上实现仅依赖触觉的盲抓取提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Blind grasping with a dexterous hand is a crucial manipulation capability. Nevertheless, learning such tactile-only policies for real robots remains challenging due to the tactile sim-to-real gap and the limited expressiveness of sparse tactile signals. To bridge this gap, we propose a framework for tactile-only blind grasping that is deployable on a physical multi-fingered robotic hand. Our approach combines three key components. First, we introduce a Real2Sim tactile calibration pipeline that constructs a contact-calibrated digital-twin simulator capable of reproducing real tactile signals. Second, we improve the expressiveness of sparse tactile observations using a layout-aware tactile encoder, which incorporates sensor-geometry priors through self-supervised pretraining. Third, to improve generalization to unseen objects, we train object-specific reinforcement-learning experts in the calibrated simulator and aggregate their successful grasp trajectories into a tactile-conditioned Diffusion Policy. We evaluate our method on a physical LEAP Hand equipped with distributed tactile sensing across 10 seen and 10 unseen objects. The deployed policy achieves a 27\% real-world grasp success rate across all 20 objects, without real-world grasping demonstrations or visual input. Simulation ablations show that layout-aware tactile pretraining improves grasping performance, while sensing-level evaluations confirm that Real2Sim calibration increases the consistency of tactile contact events between simulation and hardware. Together, these results suggest that contact-event calibration, geometry-aware tactile representation learning, and diffusion-based policy aggregation provide an effective path toward tactile-only blind grasping on real dexterous robotic hands. Project page:Dex-Blind-Grasp.github.io.

</details>


### [197] [DrivingAgent: Design and Scheduling Agents for Autonomous Driving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12236)
*Zhongyu Xia,Wenhao Chen,Yongtao Wang,Ming-Hsuan Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DrivingAgent是一种面向自动驾驶系统设计与实时调度的智能代理框架，通过自动化模块开发（设计阶段）和基于强化学习轻量级LLM的动态调度（调度阶段），在nuScenes和Bench2Drive基准上实现了更优的速度-精度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶系统在集成基础模型时面临两大挑战：(i) 新模型的设计与集成依赖人工，过程繁琐低效；(ii) 缺乏智能的动态调度机制以满足严格的实时性约束。现有基于LLM的代理框架无法区分系统设计与实时调度的根本差异，将模块视为黑盒，且不适合持续运行。

Method: DrivingAgent包含两个阶段：(1) 设计阶段：通过解释系统架构、生成代码并利用超网络训练验证模块，实现模块开发自动化；(2) 调度阶段：采用经强化学习训练的轻量级LLM，配合融合长期存储与带时间戳短期上下文的结构化记忆，实时动态编排系统模块。

Result: 在nuScenes和Bench2Drive两个基准测试上，DrivingAgent取得了更优的速度-精度权衡。

Conclusion: DrivingAgent为自动驾驶系统的设计自动化与实时动态调度提供了一种有效的智能代理方案，在性能与效率之间实现了更好的平衡。

Abstract: Many autonomous driving systems are increasingly incorporating foundation models to improve generalization and handle long-tail scenarios. However, this trend introduces two key challenges: (i) the manual and labor-intensive process of designing and integrating new models, and (ii) the lack of intelligent, dynamic scheduling mechanisms to meet strict real-time constraints. While Large Language Model (LLM)-based agents offer a promising avenue for automation, existing frameworks are ill-suited for autonomous driving. Specifically, they fail to distinguish between the fundamentally different requirements of system design and real-time scheduling, treat modules as opaque black boxes, and are not designed for continuous operation. To address these limitations, we propose DrivingAgent, a novel agent framework tailored to the dual challenges of autonomous driving system design and scheduling. In the design phase, DrivingAgent automates module development by interpreting system architecture, generating code, and validating modules via super-network training. In the scheduling phase, it employs a lightweight LLM trained with reinforcement learning to dynamically orchestrate system modules in real time, supported by a structured memory that integrates long-term storage with timestamped short-term context. Experimental results demonstrate that DrivingAgent achieves a superior speed--accuracy trade-off on both the nuScenes and Bench2Drive benchmarks.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [198] [Quickest Detection of Hallucination Onset: Delay Bounds and Learned CUSUM Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12476)
*Igor Itkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将幻觉检测重新定义为快速变化检测问题，而非传统的token级分类。通过马尔可夫模型和CUSUM方法，在RAGTruth数据集上实现了比线性基线更低的检测延迟（11-13 tokens vs 31 tokens），并从信息论角度解释了性能差距（仅利用了特征中1/4.5的散度）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统token级幻觉检测器使用AUC等分类指标评估，但流式监控更关注反应时间（从幻觉发生到告警的token数）。现有评估方式掩盖了延迟结构，需要新的检测框架来量化延迟性能。

Method: 1) 将幻觉检测形式化为快速变化检测问题；2) 基于RAGTruth数据集验证一阶马尔可夫模型刻画忠实/幻觉隐状态的合理性；3) 应用Lorden下界计算理论最优延迟（约1.3 tokens）；4) 使用因果循环标注器实现CUSUM（累积和）检测器，学习增量函数；5) 通过受控分解分离token级分数与时间累积的贡献；6) 利用Donsker-Varadhan型信息率最优性定理解释性能差距。

Result: 在匹配的虚警率（0.01）下：因果循环标注器检测延迟11-13 tokens，线性per-token基线为31 tokens，理论下界约1.3 tokens。性能优势主要来自更好的token级分数而非时间累积。学习到的分数仅实现了特征携带散度的1/4.5，剩余差距为有限时域效应。

Conclusion: 分类指标（AUC）掩盖了检测延迟结构，序贯分析使延迟可量化。当前方法距离理论最优仍有显著差距，主要源于学习到的表征散度不足，且该差距无法通过重校准消除。

Abstract: Token-level hallucination detectors are evaluated as classifiers, by AUC over all tokens, yet a streaming monitor is judged by its reaction time: the number of tokens that pass between the onset of a hallucination and the alarm. We formulate hallucination onset detection as a quickest change detection problem. A first-order Markov model of the latent faithful/hallucinated state, validated on RAGTruth, places the task inside classical change-point theory and yields Lorden's lower bound on detection delay: about 1.3 tokens at a false-alarm rate of 0.01. We then show that a causal recurrent labeler acts as a CUSUM with a learned increment; at a matched false-alarm rate it detects in 11-13 tokens, against 31 for a linear per-token baseline, and a controlled decomposition attributes most of this advantage to a better per-token score rather than to temporal accumulation. An information-rate optimality theorem of Donsker-Varadhan type explains the remaining order-of-magnitude gap: the learned score realizes only 1/4.5 of the divergence the features carry, a deficit that recalibration cannot remove, with the remainder a finite-horizon effect. Classification metrics conceal this delay structure; sequential analysis makes it measurable

</details>


### [199] [Boltzmann Attention: Learnable Ising Couplings for Cooperative Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12478)
*Gilhan Kim,Daniel K. Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出玻尔兹曼注意力机制，通过伊辛模型引入可学习的成对耦合项，使注意力层能显式建模位置间的协同或对抗关系，在字符级语言建模和括号匹配任务上优于标准注意力，且序列越长优势越明显。该框架还可通过量子退火进行高效训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准注意力机制仅通过查询-键相似度计算相关性，softmax归一化虽引入位置间竞争，但未能显式参数化注意力决策之间的可学习交互，限制了模型对注意力机制内部协同或对抗结构的直接建模能力。

Method: 提出玻尔兹曼注意力（Boltzmann attention），将注意力模式建模为伊辛模型：以数据相关的局部场（标准注意力分数）为基础，加入可学习的成对耦合参数，通过能量函数定义位置间的全局交互分布，替代传统的softmax归一化。

Result: 在字符级语言建模和合成括号匹配任务中，玻尔兹曼注意力在标准Transformer架构上持续优于softmax注意力，且序列越长优势越显著。四路消融实验证实性能提升来自可学习的成对耦合项。量子退火训练方法在保持竞争性能的同时提供了实用训练途径。

Conclusion: 显式的位置间交互为基于注意力的序列建模提供了有原理性的增强方案，伊辛公式还为基于量子计算的采样策略打开了自然通道。

Abstract: Attention mechanisms are central to modern sequence models, yet standard attention computes relevance primarily through individual query--key similarities. Although softmax normalization introduces competition among positions, a standard attention layer does not explicitly parameterize learnable interactions between attention decisions. This limits its ability to directly model cooperative or antagonistic co-attention structure within the attention mechanism itself. We propose Boltzmann attention, an energy-based generalization in which attention patterns are governed by an interacting Ising model. The method augments the usual data-dependent local fields with learnable pairwise couplings, allowing the model to represent inter-position correlations beyond those captured by softmax or sigmoid attention. Experiments on character-level language modeling and synthetic bracket matching show that Boltzmann attention consistently improves over standard softmax attention within a standard Transformer architecture, with the advantage becoming more pronounced as sequence length increases. A four-way ablation confirms that the improvement arises from the learnable pairwise couplings. These results suggest that explicit inter-position interactions provide a principled enhancement for attention-based sequence modeling. Moreover, the Ising formulation opens a natural path toward quantum-computing-based sampling strategies: we demonstrate that diabatic quantum annealing provides a practical training method while maintaining competitive performance with exact Boltzmann computation.

</details>


### [200] [ReCal: Reward Calibration for RL-based LLM Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12479)
*Qihang Yu,Hanwen Tong,Zhengqi Zhang,Bo Zheng,Feng Wei,Shengyu Zhang,Zemin Liu,Fei Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ReCal框架，通过层次化奖励分解与分布感知优化策略，解决RL-based LLM路由中多目标冲突和奖励信号异质性问题，在7个数据集上提升了路由性能和训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL-based LLM路由方法在异构任务下面临两个主要问题：1）多目标（如正确性、格式行为）被聚合为单一标量奖励，导致信用分配模糊和优化信号冲突；2）奖励信号在不同实例间存在显著方差，简单样本产生更高或更易波动的奖励，引入优化偏差。

Method: ReCal框架包含两个核心组件：1）层次化奖励分解机制，将奖励分解到不同目标维度并进行组件级别的优势估计；2）分布感知优化策略，通过方差感知重加权和每个数据集独立归一化来校准优化中的变异。

Result: 在7个数据集上的实验表明，ReCal在路由性能和训练稳定性方面一致优于基线方法。

Conclusion: ReCal通过奖励校准有效解决了多目标冲突和奖励异质性问题，为RL-based LLM路由提供了更稳健且高效的训练范式。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) routing has emerged as an effective paradigm for leveraging the complementary strengths of multiple LLMs through dynamic model and reasoning-strategy selection. Recent reinforcement learning (RL)-based routing methods further improve routing quality by optimizing routing policies from interaction feedback. However, they still struggle to provide informative and comparable learning signals under heterogeneous tasks with varying difficulty. In practice, multiple objectives (e.g., correctness, format behavior) are aggregated into a single scalar reward, leading to ambiguous credit assignment and conflicting optimization signals. Moreover, reward signals exhibit significant variability across instances, where some instances produce higher or more variable rewards, introducing optimization bias that favors trivial samples over informative ones. To address these issues, we propose \textbf{ReCal}, a \textbf{\underline{Re}}ward \textbf{\underline{Cal}}ibration framework for RL-based LLM routing. We first introduce a hierarchical reward decomposition mechanism with component-wise advantage estimation. We further propose a distribution-aware optimization strategy that calibrates optimization variability through variance-aware reweighting and per-dataset normalization. Experiments on seven datasets demonstrate that ReCal consistently improves routing performance, and training stability over baselines. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ReCal.

</details>


### [201] [Representing Time Series as Structured Programs for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12481)
*Jaeho Kim,Changhun Oh,Seokhyun Lee,Irina Rish,Changhee Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出T2SP方法，将时间序列转化为结构化符号程序表示，使预训练LLM无需微调即可进行时间序列推理，在编辑、描述和问答任务上优于原始数值序列表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法直接将原始数值序列输入LLM或微调LLM处理时间序列，存在模态不匹配、长序列性能下降和计算开销大的问题。需要一种能弥合时间序列与LLM文本模态之间差距的有效表示方法。

Method: T2SP是一种确定性、无需训练的方法，将时间序列分解为趋势、周期和显著事件，并以结构化符号程序的形式表示，使其与LLM原生训练的文本和代码模态对齐。

Result: 在编辑、描述和问答三项推理任务上，T2SP相比原始字符串表示，一致地提升了性能、减少了推理时间并降低了失败率。

Conclusion: T2SP通过在表示层面而非模型层面提取时间结构，为LLM与时间序列之间提供了有效的接口，使现成的LLM能利用其已有推理能力进行时间序列理解。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong reasoning and instruction-following capabilities, making them potentially powerful tools for time-series analysis. However, time series lie outside their native textual modality, raising a fundamental question: how should time series be represented so that LLMs can reason about them effectively? Existing work typically serializes raw numerical sequences or fine-tunes pre-trained LLMs on time-series data. These approaches place the burden of extracting temporal structure directly on the LLM, creating a modality mismatch that often degrades performance on long sequences and introduces substantial computational overhead. In this work, we introduce Time-Series-to-Structured-Program representation (T2SP), a deterministic, training-free method that represents a time series as a structured symbolic program. T2SP decomposes time series into trends, periods, and salient events, expressing them in a program-friendly format aligned with the textual and code-like modalities on which LLMs are natively trained. By shifting temporal-structure extraction from the model to the representation itself, T2SP enables off-the-shelf LLMs to leverage their existing reasoning capabilities for time-series understanding. We evaluate T2SP on three reasoning tasks -- editing, captioning, and question answering -- where it consistently improves performance, reduces reasoning time, and lowers failure rates compared with raw-string representations. Our results demonstrate that T2SP provides an effective interface between time series and LLMs.

</details>


### [202] [Scalable anomaly detection via a univariate Christoffel function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12483)
*Florian Grivet,Didier Henrion,Jean-Bernard Lasserre,Louise Travé-Massuyès*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出UCF（单变量Christoffel函数），通过基于查询点与支持点之间平方距离的设计，解决了Christoffel函数在异常检测中因矩阵求逆导致的高维计算瓶颈，在ADBench基准上以Average Precision指标持续优于14个SOTA基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Christoffel函数方法虽在异常检测中具有坚实的数学基础和计算经济性，但其矩阵求逆的规模随数据维度指数增长，导致中等维度数据集即不可行，严重限制了实际应用。

Method: 提出UCF（Univariate Christoffel Function），基于查询点与支持点之间的平方距离来构建单变量Christoffel函数，避免了原始方法中高维矩阵求逆的指数级计算开销。

Result: 在ADBench基准上的大量实验表明，UCF在Average Precision指标上一致优于14个SOTA基线方法。

Conclusion: UCF解决了Christoffel函数在异常检测中的可扩展性瓶颈，同时保留了其核心理论性质（on-off支持二分性和精确支持形状捕捉），提供了一种稳健、有理论根基且普适的异常检测方法。

Abstract: Anomaly detection plays a critical role in identifying unusual patterns across domains such as fraud detection, network intrusion, and system fault diagnosis. Recently, Christoffel function-based methods, rooted in polynomial optimization, have emerged as promising alternatives to deep learning due to their strong mathematical foundations and computational frugality. However, their practical applicability is hindered by the need to invert a matrix whose size grows exponentially with the data dimension, rendering the method intractable even for moderate-dimensional datasets. This paper addresses the dimensionality limitations of Christoffel function-based anomaly detection while preserving its key theoretical properties, i.e., the on-off support dichotomy behavior and the accurate support shape capture. We introduce UCF, a univariate Christoffel function which is based on the squared distance between the query point and the support points. Extensive experiments on the ADBench benchmark demonstrate that UCF consistently outperforms 14 state-of-the-art baselines in terms of Average Precision. By resolving the scalability bottleneck of the Christoffel Function, this work expands the toolkit of anomaly detection methods with a robust, theoretically grounded, and universally applicable approach.

</details>


### [203] [Extracting Governing Equations from Latent Dynamics via Multi-View Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13260)
*Paolo Muratore,Mackenzie Weygandt Mathis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DYSCO是一种多视图时间对比学习算法，能够从带噪高维观测中联合恢复潜在轨迹和动力学方程，并在仿射规范下实现符号级方程恢复，理论上保证了强可识别性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从带噪高维观测数据中识别潜在动力系统是表示学习、系统辨识和科学发现中的核心难题，现有方法在噪声非线性观测下的可识别性缺乏理论保障。

Method: 提出多视图时间对比学习算法DYSCO，利用同一过程的多组独立带噪观测来解耦信号与噪声；将动力学参数化在结构化函数基中，实现仿射规范下的符号方程恢复。

Result: 在混沌、振荡、亚稳态等多种动力系统下，高斯和泊松观测噪声下均能准确恢复潜在轨迹和流场。

Conclusion: 将可识别性理论结果推广到带噪非线性观测的真实场景，并在神经记录等应用中具有重要价值。

Abstract: Identifying latent dynamical systems from noisy, high-dimensional measurements is a central problem at the intersection of representation learning, system identification, and scientific discovery. We present DYSCO, a multi-view temporal contrastive learning algorithm that jointly recovers latent trajectories and the governing dynamics from such observations, by leveraging multiple independent noisy views of the same underlying process to disentangle signal from noise. By parameterizing the dynamics in a structured functional basis, our framework further enables symbolic recovery of the governing equations within an affine gauge. We offer theoretical guarantees for strong identification up to an affine indeterminacy, extending prior identifiability results to the realistic setting of noisy nonlinear observations. Empirically, we demonstrate accurate recovery of both latent trajectories and flow fields across a diverse set of dynamical regimes (e.g., chaotic, oscillatory, and metastable) under both Gaussian and Poisson observation noise, the latter being particularly relevant for neural recordings.

</details>


### [204] [Speculative Rollback Correction for Quality-Diverse Web Agent Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12485)
*Longkun Hao,Hongyu Lin,Hao Li,Zhichao Yang,Haojie Hao,Dongshuo Huang,Haitao Yang,Hongyu Ge,Ming jie Xie,Yanjun Wu,Zi Hao Yin,Yan Bai,Yihang Lou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Speculative Rollback Correction (SRC)，一种用于可重置智能体环境的分支级模仿学习框架。SRC通过固定水平分支审查（学生先执行短推测片段，教师仅在局部进展卡顿时定位首次有害偏离）来平衡专家干预时机，并利用硬验证器过滤成功轨迹存入质量多样性档案，从而在WebArena-Infinity上收集了977条通过验证的轨迹和9,183个下一动作示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专家干预时机是交互式网络智能体模仿学习中的关键挑战：延迟干预导致早期错误累积使页面状态不可恢复，过早或过度干预则使智能体过度依赖专家策略而陷入单一刚性轨迹的局部最优。

Method: SRC采用固定水平分支审查：学生在教师审查前执行短推测片段，仅当局部进展卡顿时教师才定位首次有害偏离。回滚保留有用前缀，成功轨迹经硬验证器过滤后存入轻量级质量多样性档案，数据用于下一动作的监督微调。

Result: 在WebArena-Infinity上，SRC收集了977条通过验证的轨迹和9,183个下一动作示例；固定水平审查相比步级审查在恢复性与查询量之间取得了更优的权衡，同时保留了通过验证的解决方案变体。

Conclusion: SRC通过分支级模仿框架有效解决了专家干预时机难题，在恢复效率与查询成本之间取得更好平衡，并能保留多样化的成功轨迹。

Abstract: Training interactive web agents through imitation learning from expert trajectories has emerged as a highly effective approach. However, determining the optimal timing for expert intervention presents a critical challenge in this context. Delayed intervention often leads to the accumulation of early-stage errors, pushing the page state into an irrecoverable regime. Conversely, premature or excessive intervention causes the agent to become overly reliant on expert policies, trapping the model in local optima characterized by a single, rigid trajectory. We propose Speculative Rollback Correction (SRC), a branch-level imitation framework for resettable agent environments. Instead of requesting teacher labels at every visited state or correcting only after a completed trajectory, SRC uses fixed-horizon branch review: the student executes a short speculative segment before teacher review, and the teacher localizes the first harmful deviation only when local progress breaks. Rollback preserves useful prefixes, while successful rollouts are filtered by a hard verifier and retained in a lightweight quality-diversity archive. The resulting data supports next-action supervised fine-tuning on both localized corrections and verifier-passing trajectories. On WebArena-Infinity, SRC collects 977 verifier-passing trajectories and 9,183 next-action examples; fixed-horizon review improves the recovery-versus-query tradeoff over step-level review while retaining verifier-passing solution variants. Code is available at https://github.com/LongkunHao/SRC_gui_agent.

</details>


### [205] [An Empirical Study on Predictive Maintenance for Component X in Heavy-Duty Scania Trucks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12486)
*Valeriu Dimidov,Sasan Jafarnejad,Raphaël Frank*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了一种基于条件预测维护（PdM）的方法论，该方法假设被监测组件的磨损状态可表示为单调非递减时间序列，通过选取最近观测值并转换为表格格式，结合AutoML进行机器学习分类，在Scania Component X数据集上相比当前SOTA方法降低了成本并简化了建模过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卡车车队条件预测维护（PdM）虽能减少停机时间和成本，但因数据量大、故障检测复杂以及实现成本难以权衡，实际部署面临挑战，亟需更有效且简化的解决方案。

Method: 假设组件磨损状态为单调非递减时间序列；仅选取最近观测值，将时间序列转换为表格格式；使用适用于表格数据的机器学习模型（结合AutoML）进行分类。

Result: 在Scania Component X数据集上，所提方法相比当前SOTA方法降低了成本，并通过AutoML简化了建模流程。

Conclusion: 该方法在降低预测维护成本的同时，简化了建模过程，验证了基于单调非递减假设和最近观测值转换策略在卡车车队PdM中的有效性。

Abstract: Condition-based Predictive Maintenance (PdM) for truck fleets has gained momentum in recent years. This maintenance strategy aims to minimize unplanned downtimes and reduce costs by monitoring the health status of vehicles and taking proactive action based on their condition. However, the implementation of condition-based PdM systems is challenging due to the large volume of data generated by the trucks, the inherent complexity of detecting failures through sensor data and the difficulties in finding cost-effective trade-offs in the solution's implementation. In this paper, we define and validate a condition-based PdM methodology built on the assumption that the wear-and-tear state of the monitored component can be represented as a monotonically non-decreasing time series. It involves selecting only the most recent observations from the time series and transforming them into a tabular format for classification using machine learning (ML) models designed for tabular data. Our results indicate that the proposed methodology reduces costs on the Scania Component X dataset compared to current state-of-the-art (SOTA) approaches, while also simplifying the modeling process through AutoML.

</details>


### [206] [DynamicPTQ: Mitigating Activation Quantization Collapse via Residual-Stream Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12487)
*Zimo Zhao,Maolin Wang,Bowen Yu,Bowen Liu,Xiao Han,Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DynamicPTQ，一种动态后训练量化策略，通过识别残差流中量化敏感层并对其使用8-bit激活精度，其余保持4-bit，解决了W4A4KV4全量化中因跨层残差动态变化导致的激活量化不稳定问题，在LLaMA-2/3上提升了困惑度和零样本QA性能，同时实现了1.05-1.07倍的吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的后训练量化方法主要通过基于变换的平滑技术（如正交旋转和仿射缩放）来缓解massive activations问题，但这些静态方法忽略了残差流的跨层动态变化。论文发现大规模激活以阶段式模式在网络深度上出现和消失，导致残差变化巨大，使得静态变换无法完全解决动态量化不稳定性。

Method: 提出DynamicPTQ策略：（1）引入Jump Ratio和Historical Feature SNR两个指标来刻画跨层残差动态行为；（2）基于残差流动态识别量化敏感层；（3）对这些敏感层采用8-bit激活精度，其他层及权重、KV缓存保持4-bit精度；（4）可与QuaRot、SpinQuant、FlatQuant等强基线直接集成。

Result: 在LLaMA-2和LLaMA-3上，DynamicPTQ在W4A4KV4量化设置下持续改善困惑度和零样本QA性能，同时实现1.05至1.07倍的吞吐量提升，内存开销适度。

Conclusion: 动态、感知阶段的混合精度量化策略能够有效解决全低比特LLM推理中因跨层残差变化导致的量化不稳定问题，为鲁棒的低比特LLM推理提供了一条实用路径。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) is essential for efficient large language model inference, but reliably quantizing activations remains challenging when weights, activations, and KV caches are all quantized to 4-bit precision. A key difficulty lies in massive activations, whose extreme values dominate the activation range and amplify quantization errors. State-of-the-art methods mainly mitigate massive activations through transformation-based smoothing, such as orthogonal rotations and affine scaling, but overlook the cross-layer dynamics of the residual stream. In this paper, we show that massive activations emerge and disappear in a phase-wise pattern across network depth, triggering large residual changes. These changes cause newly injected layer-wise updates to dominate the 4-bit quantization scale and weaken historical residual information. To characterize this behavior, we introduce Jump Ratio and Historical Feature SNR. This suggests that static transformation-based smoothing cannot fully resolve dynamic quantization instability caused by cross-layer residual changes. Based on this analysis, we propose DynamicPTQ, a Dynamic Post-Training Quantization policy for phase-aware mixed-precision activation quantization. DynamicPTQ identifies quantization-sensitive layers from residual-stream dynamics and assigns 8-bit activation precision only to these layers, while keeping weights, KV caches, and other activations in 4-bit precision. It can be directly integrated with strong PTQ baselines such as QuaRot, SpinQuant, and FlatQuant. Experiments on LLaMA-2 and LLaMA-3 show that DynamicPTQ consistently improves perplexity and zero-shot QA performance under W4A4KV4 quantization, while achieving 1.05 to 1.07 times throughput improvement with modest memory overhead. These results demonstrate a practical path toward robust low-bit LLM inference.

</details>


### [207] [A Stationary (and Therefore Compatible) Representation is All You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12488)
*Niccolò Biondi,Federico Pernici,Simone Ricci,Alberto Del Bimbo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了使用 d-Simplex 固定分类器学习的平稳表示天然具有兼容性，并提出通过交叉熵损失与对比度损失的凸组合来捕获高阶依赖关系，在模型更新和替换时实现无需重新处理数据的持续检索服务，达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习兼容表示旨在模型更新时特征表示可互换使用，但现有方法未能充分捕获模型更新间表示的高阶依赖关系。

Method: 利用 d-Simplex 固定分类器学习平稳表示；采用交叉熵损失与对比度损失的凸组合训练，在兼容性约束下捕获高阶依赖。

Result: 实验证明该方法在不重新处理图库图像的情况下实现不间断检索服务，在模型更新和替换场景中性能提升，达到 SOTA。

Conclusion: d-Simplex 固定分类器学习的平稳表示天然满足兼容性定义，结合对比度损失的凸组合训练能更全面捕获表示间依赖，为兼容表示学习奠定理论基础并提供实用方案。

Abstract: Learning compatible representations aims to learn feature representations that can be used interchangeably over time whenever a model undergoes updates. In this paper, we demonstrate that stationary representations learned by d-Simplex fixed classifiers imply compatibility as in its formal definition. This result establishes a foundation for future works and can be directly exploited in practical learning scenarios. We address the challenge of learning compatibility using $d$-Simplex fixed classifiers when the model is sequentially fine-tuned. Learning according to a d-Simplex fixed classifier with the cross-entropy loss aligns feature distributions at the first-order statistics. Consequently, it may not fully capture higher-order dependencies in the representation between model updates. To address this issue, we demonstrate that training the model using a $d$-Simplex fixed classifier through a convex combination of the cross-entropy loss and a contrastive loss not only captures higher-order dependencies, but is also equivalent to learning with the cross-entropy under the compatibility constraints. We confirm our findings with extensive experiments also considering a new scenario where a pre-trained model is sequentially fine-tuned and occasionally replaced with an improved model. We show that stationary representations enable uninterrupted retrieval services (without reprocessing gallery images) while improving performance during model updates and replacements, achieving state-of-the-art. Code at https://github.com/miccunifi/iamcl2r.

</details>


### [208] [Robustness Verification of Recurrent Neural Networks with Abstraction Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12490)
*Li-Jen Lin,Chih-Duo Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对循环神经网络（RNN）的抽象-精化验证框架，通过对跨零的预激活区间进行分支划分以消除主要的松弛误差，并引入SHAP引导的时间步选择策略来控制组合爆炸，实验表明该方法在验证成功率和鲁棒边界紧致性上均优于纯抽象基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RNN的局部鲁棒性验证具有挑战性，因为非线性松弛引入的近似误差会通过循环连接传播并随时间累积，导致可扩展的线性界传播方法过于保守，尤其在许多预激活区间跨零时无法验证实际鲁棒的输入。

Method: 提出抽象-精化框架，通过分区跨零的预激活区间来消除松弛误差（使ReLU精确，tanh/sigmoid的线性包络更紧致）；引入SHAP引导的时间步选择策略，按隐藏状态对验证目标的贡献排序，仅精化时序上最关键的时间步以控制组合成本。

Result: 在CIFAR10和MNIST stroke基准测试上，验证成功率和鲁棒边界紧致性均优于纯抽象基线方法，同时揭示了ReLU与tanh模型之间清晰的运行时权衡。

Conclusion: 所提抽象-精化框架能有效提升RNN局部鲁棒性验证的精度，SHAP引导的选择策略在长序列中控制了计算成本，但不同激活函数模型存在运行时权衡。

Abstract: Certified local robustness verification for recurrent neural networks (RNNs) is challenging because approximation errors introduced by nonlinear relaxations can propagate through recurrent connections and accumulate over time. As a result, scalable linear bound propagation methods often become overly conservative and fail to certify inputs that are in fact robust, especially when many pre-activation intervals cross zero. We propose an abstraction-refinement framework for RNN verification that partitions such intervals to remove the dominant relaxation error: on each refined branch, ReLU becomes exact, and smooth activations such as tanh and sigmoid admit substantially tighter linear envelopes. To control the combinatorial cost of splitting in long sequences, we introduce a SHAP-guided timestep selection strategy that ranks hidden states by their contribution to the verification objective and refines only the most critical timesteps in temporal order. Experiments on CIFAR10 and MNIST stroke benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements in verification success and robustness-margin tightness over abstraction-only baselines, while exposing clear runtime trade-offs between ReLU and tanh models.

</details>


### [209] [The Urysohn Machine: A Metric-Topological Model of Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.14143)
*Xin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Urysohn Machine，一种以度量分离、前沿结构和压缩为计算状态的分类模型，核心是Urysohn Triple（支撑区域、目标划分和可重用分类器）。通过构造性Urysohn实现定理构建分离器，引入决策边界宽度和Urysohn宽度两种复杂度度量，证明摊销分离定理，并提出对比分离算子用于估计边界宽度，最后给出动态Urysohn梯度的四个保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典分类计算模型忽略了几何与拓扑结构，缺乏对分类复杂度、摊销推理和组合重用的度量拓扑解释。作者试图构建一个显式将度量分离、前沿结构和压缩作为计算状态的模型，揭示纯符号描述中隐藏的几何结构。

Method: 基于Urysohn三元组（支撑区域、目标划分、可重用分类器）和构造性Urysohn实现定理，从二元梯度的嵌套多面体区域构建分离器，引入链级前沿演算（前沿是链，层间壳的边界是前沿差）。定义决策边界宽度和Urysohn宽度，证明摊销分离定理，提出对比分离算子（图割泛函估计边界宽度，拉普拉斯谱认证类分量结构和传导性），分析动态Urysohn梯度并证明四个保证。

Result: 证明了摊销分离定理：在明确的边界足迹假设下，逼近宽度为w的边界到精度ε所需的简单基三元组数量与边界宽度成正比、与分辨率成反比。对比分离算子的图割泛函能一致估计决策边界宽度，拉普拉斯谱可认证类分量结构。动态Urysohn梯度满足四个保证：商坍塌下的可分离性、承诺前沿的稳定性、压缩下的有界容量、商距离下的可扩展性。

Conclusion: Urysohn Machine为分类复杂度、摊销推理和组合重用提供了度量拓扑框架，在保持经典可计算性的同时暴露了纯符号描述隐藏的几何结构。

Abstract: We introduce the Urysohn Machine, an effective model of classification-oriented computation in which metric separation, frontier structure, and contraction are explicit parts of the computational state. Its basic object is a \emph{Urysohn Triple}: a support region, a target partition, and a separating classifier stored in a reusable Metric Library. The topological foundation is a constructive Urysohn Realization theorem for finite simplicial settings. It builds separators from dyadic ladders of nested polyhedral regions and equips their frontiers with a chain-level calculus: frontiers are cycles, and shells between levels have boundaries given by differences of frontiers. This construction yields two related complexity measures: decision-boundary width, the geometric measure of a single classifier's boundary, and Urysohn width, the total frontier mass represented by a library or realization. We prove an Amortized Separation Theorem showing that approximating a boundary of width to accuracy requires a number of simple basis triples proportional to boundary width and inversely proportional to resolution, under explicit boundary-footprint assumptions. We also introduce a contrastive separation operator whose graph-cut functional consistently estimates decision-boundary width from sampled metric data, while its Laplacian spectrum certifies class-component structure and conductance. Finally, we analyze the dynamic Urysohn ladder and prove four guarantees: separability under quotient collapse, stability of committed frontiers, bounded capacity under contraction, and scalability with quotient distance. Together, these results give a metric-topological account of classification complexity, amortized inference, and compositional reuse that preserves classical computability while exposing geometric structure hidden by purely symbolic descriptions.

</details>


### [210] [Net-Ev$^2$: A Generative Simulator for Network Event Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12494)
*Guangyu Wang,Zhaonan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Net-Ev²，一个联合事件语义与网络拓扑结构的生成式模拟器，用于模拟扰动事件在网络中的传播演化，在多个大规模道路网络数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中减少试错成本是决策的核心目标，而模拟扰动事件（如事故）在网络中的影响传播更具挑战性。现有方法无法同时建模事件的结构化属性与非结构化语义，也无法在模拟中捕捉网络拓扑结构。

Method: Net-Ev²包含两个阶段：结构引导的掩码预训练（structure-guided masked pre-training）和拓扑感知的扩散过程（topology-aware diffusion process），后者通过类似U-Net的图下采样和上采样实现去噪。推理时仅需自然语言事件输入即可生成模拟。

Result: 在四个大规模道路网络数据集上，Net-Ev²达到SOTA性能，并展现出强泛化能力。同时发布了Net-Ev²-6.5M多模态基准数据集和新拓扑感知评估指标JL-MMD。

Conclusion: Net-Ev²成功实现了联合事件语义与网络拓扑的生成式模拟，为网络扰动事件传播模拟提供了新范式，代码已开源。

Abstract: Reducing real-world trial and error has long been a central goal of decision making, and generative simulators advance this goal by modeling the evolution of future states. An even more challenging yet meaningful task is simulating how disturbance events (e.g., accidents) propagate their impacts across real-world networks. The existing approaches fall short of modeling both structured attributes and unstructured semantics of events, and capturing topological structures in simulating network event evolution. Therefore, we are motivated to propose Net-Ev$^2$ ($\underline{\textbf{Net}}$work $\underline{\textbf{Ev}}$ent $\underline{\textbf{Ev}}$olution), a novel generative simulator that jointly leverages event cues while preserving network topology in simulations. Specifically, the framework consists of two stages, namely structure-guided masked pre-training and topology-aware diffusion process, which is achieved by U-Net-like graph downsampling and upsampling during denoising. At inference time, Net-Ev$^2$ can generate simulations using natural-language event input only, with greater flexibility for practical usage. Furthermore, we introduce Net-Ev$^2$-6.5M, a multimodal benchmark of aligned event and network traffic data across four large-scale road networks, as well as a new topology-aware metric, namely JL-MMD, to evaluate topological fidelity in generated network dynamics. Extensive experiments demonstrate the state-of-the-art performance and strong generalization ability of Net-Ev$^2$. Code is made available at https://github.com/Guangyu4/Net-Ev-2.

</details>


### [211] [$μ$VLA: On Recurrent Memory for Partially Observable Manipulation in VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12497)
*Egor Cherepanov,Nikita Kachaev,Daniil Zelezetsky,Aydar Bulatov,Artem Pshenitsyn,Yuri Kuratov,Alexey Skrynnik,Aleksandr I. Panov,Alexey K. Kovalev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在强预训练VLA骨干网络中对循环机制进行受控隔离研究，通过添加少量可学习的记忆token并通过时间反向传播训练，在不改变架构和无需辅助损失的情况下，μVLA在部分可观测任务上成功率从0.42提升至0.84，而在完全可观测任务上保持96.2%的成功率，标定了最小化骨干内循环的能力边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强VLA模型同时引入循环、检索、压缩、辅助目标、层次记忆或任务特定架构修改等多种机制，导致循环本身对性能的贡献与额外组件纠缠不清，无法独立评估循环机制的实际效果。

Method: 在Transformer中增加少量跨时间步携带的可学习记忆token（通过自注意力更新），采用截断反向传播时间（TBPTT）进行端到端训练，无辅助损失和架构修改。实例化为μVLA系列，参数量化为记忆宽度m、TBPTT长度K和记忆更新规则（跨步梯度或分离EMA），使循环成为唯一变量。

Result: MIKASA-Robo上训练任务平均成功率从0.42提升至0.84；保留任务上循环模型达0.23而基线仅0.07；需要不同记忆结构的任务性能接近基线；LIBERO完全可观测任务上最强循环变体达96.2%平均成功率，无性能退化。

Conclusion: 标定了最小化骨干内循环机制的能力边界：在部分可观测场景中循环足以显著提升性能，但需要不同记忆结构的场景则需额外架构支撑，为后续记忆增强VLA设计提供了重要的消融基准。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models predict chunks of future actions from the current observation, an assumption that fails under partial observability, where decisions depend on information no longer visible. Existing memory-augmented VLAs simultaneously introduce recurrence, retrieval, compression modules, auxiliary objectives, hierarchical memory, or task-specific architectural changes, so the contribution of recurrence itself remains entangled with surrounding machinery. We present a controlled isolation study of recurrence in a strong pretrained VLA backbone. Our formulation augments the transformer with a small set of learnable memory tokens carried across timesteps and updated through self-attention, trained end to end with truncated backpropagation through time, with no auxiliary losses and no architectural changes. We instantiate this as $μ$VLA, a family of OpenVLA-OFT variants parameterized by memory width m, TBPTT length K, and the memory update rule (cross-step gradients or a detached EMA), so that recurrence is the only varying factor. On MIKASA-Robo, $μ$VLA improves average success rate on five training tasks from 0.42 to 0.84 at the strongest setting and reaches 0.23 on held-out tasks with the same memory structure versus 0.07 for the memoryless baseline. On tasks requiring different memory structure, performance remains near baseline. On LIBERO, the strongest recurrent variant achieves 96.2% average success, indicating no regression under full observability. We interpret these results as a calibration of the capability envelope of minimal in-backbone recurrence, identifying the regime in which it is sufficient and the regime where additional memory structure is required. Demos and videos can be found in https://avanturist322.github.io/mu-vla/.

</details>


### [212] [Improving Crash Frequency Prediction from Simulated Traffic Conflicts Using Machine Learning Based Microsimulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12500)
*Xian Liu,Carlo G. Prato,Gustav Markkula*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过对比规则驱动模型与机器学习行为模型在交通微观仿真中的表现，发现基于ML的模型能够更真实地再现冲突，结合极值理论可有效预测事故频率，且无需位置特定的模型校准，而规则模型则因缺乏校准而无法产生有意义的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于规则的微观仿真模型虽能合理再现交通流，但难以生成真实的冲突动力学，限制了事故预测精度。近年来基于机器学习的驾驶行为模型直接从大规模轨迹数据中学习人类驾驶行为，有望提升仿真真实性和事故预测能力，本研究旨在系统验证这一可能性。

Method: 选取英国利兹市五个真实信号交叉口，分别使用标准规则模型（规则驱动）和先进ML模型进行交通微观仿真；采用二维碰撞时间（2D TTC）指标识别仿真冲突，并利用极值理论（Extreme Value Theory）建模以预测事故频率。

Result: ML模型生成的冲突数据经极值理论建模后得到的事故预测与实际事故数据吻合，而规则模型因缺乏位置特定校准无法产生有意义预测。直接使用ML模型生成的仿真事故来预测真实事故频率效果较差。

Conclusion: 基于ML的行为模型在无需位置特定校准的情况下，能够通过仿真冲突有效提升事故预测能力，而当前ML模型虽能真实再现冲突但尚无法生成真实的事故，为ML驱动的交通微观仿真指明了未来发展方向。

Abstract: Traffic microsimulation combined with surrogate safety measures has increasingly been used as a proactive alternative to historical crash data for predicting crash frequency for current or planned road infrastructure designs. However, existing microsimulation-based safety studies have adopted simplified rule-based behaviour models, which reproduce traffic flow reasonably well but often fail to generate realistic conflict dynamics, limiting crash prediction accuracy. Recent advances in machine learning (ML)-based behaviour models offer a promising opportunity to potentially improve microsimulation realism and crash frequency predictions by learning human driving behaviour directly from large-scale trajectory datasets. To investigate this possibility, traffic microsimulation was conducted for five real-world signalised intersections in Leeds, UK, using both a standard rule-based model and a state-of-the-art ML model. Simulated vehicle trajectories were analysed using a two-dimensional Time-to-Collision metric to identify simulated conflicts, which were then modelled using Extreme Value Theory to predict crash frequency. Results show that conflicts from the ML model yielded crash predictions in line with the real-world crash data, whereas the rule-based model did not permit meaningful predictions, presumably due to a lack of model calibration to the specific simulated intersections. Directly using ML-generated simulated crashes to predict real-world crash frequency also yielded poor results, suggesting that while current ML models can realistically reproduce conflicts, they are not yet able to generate realistic crashes. Overall, the findings demonstrate that ML-based behaviour models are promising for improving crash prediction from simulated conflicts, without a need for location-specific model calibration, and suggest clear future directions for ML-based traffic microsimulation.

</details>


### [213] [Policy-driven Conformal Prediction for Trustworthy QoT Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12501)
*Kiarash Rezaei,Omran Ayoub,Paolo Monti,Carlos Natalino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Conformal QoT框架，结合统计保证的QoT估计与操作决策策略，在域偏移下实现可靠的光路可行性预测，将准确率从92%提升至99.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有光路可行性预测方法在域偏移（domain shift）场景下可靠性不足，需要一种具有统计保证且能结合操作策略的框架来提升预测准确性。

Method: 采用策略驱动（policy-driven）框架，将符合统计保证的QoT估计与操作决策策略相结合。

Result: 在开放数据集上，准确率从92%提升至99.6%。

Conclusion: Conformal QoT框架能够在域偏移下提供可靠的、具有统计保证的光路可行性预测。

Abstract: We propose Conformal QoT, a policy-driven framework that combines statistically guaranteed QoT estimation with operational decision policies, enabling reliable lightpath-feasibility predictions under domain shift and improving accuracy from 92\% to 99.6\% on open datasets.

</details>


### [214] [Dolph2Vec: Self-Supervised Representations of Dolphin Vocalizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12503)
*Chiara Semenzin,Faadil Mustun,Roberto Dessi,Pierre Orhan,Alexis Emanuelli,Yair Lakretz,Gonzalo de Polavieja,German Sumbre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文收集了超过五年、来自五只已知海豚的长时序录音数据集，并基于Wav2Vec2.0架构提出了首个大规模、物种特异性的自监督学习模型Dolph2Vec。该模型在签名口哨分类和口哨检测任务上显著优于通用基线，且学习到的嵌入和码本结构能捕捉与海豚口哨类别对应的可解释声学单元，展示了SSL作为动物沟通研究科学工具的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自监督学习模型在生物声学领域偏向跨物种的泛化，未能针对单个物种的精细交流结构进行优化，缺乏对特定物种个体沟通系统的深入分析能力。

Method: 收集了超过五年、来自五只已知海豚的长时序纵向录音数据集；基于Wav2Vec2.0架构进行领域适配，训练了首个大规模、物种特异性的自监督学习模型Dolph2Vec；在签名口哨分类和口哨检测两项生物学相关任务上进行基准测试。

Result: Dolph2Vec在签名口哨分类和口哨检测任务上均显著优于通用基线模型；学习到的嵌入和码本结构能够捕捉与海豚口哨类别对应的可解释声学单元，可能还揭示了子口哨级别的结构。

Conclusion: 自监督学习不仅可以作为高性能模型用于动物发声分析，还可以作为科学工具探索动物沟通研究中的假设，实现对个体交流模式的细粒度分析。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) has opened new opportunities in bioacoustics by enabling scalable modeling of animal vocalizations without the need for expensive manual annotation. However, current SSL models in this domain prioritize broad generalization across species and are not optimized for uncovering the fine-grained structure of individual communication systems. In this work, we collect and release a novel dataset of over five years of longitudinal recordings, from five known dolphins in a semi-naturalistic marine environment, an unprecedented resource for studying dolphin communication. We adapt the Wav2Vec2.0 Baevski et al. (2020) architecture to this domain and introduce Dolph2Vec, the first large-scale, species-specific SSL model trained exclusively on this data. We benchmark our model on two biologically relevant tasks: signature whistle classification and whistle detection. Dolph2Vec significantly outperforms general-purpose baselines in both tasks.
